server {
    listen 80;
    server_name _;

    root /usr/share/nginx/html;
    index index.html;

    location = /api/notifications/stream {
        proxy_pass http://backend:8080;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_set_header Connection "";
        proxy_buffering off;
        proxy_cache off;
        proxy_read_timeout 35m;
    }

    location /api/ {
        proxy_pass http://backend:8080;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }

    # Swagger UI и OpenAPI-схема backend-а.
    location = /swagger-ui.html {
        proxy_pass http://backend:8080;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }

    location /swagger-ui/ {
        proxy_pass http://backend:8080;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }

    location /v3/api-docs {
        proxy_pass http://backend:8080;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }

    location / {
        try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;
    }
}

=== ./src/entities/application-document/index.ts ===
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { z } from 'zod'
import { apiRequest, ApiError, useApiScope } from '@/shared/api'

export const applicationDocumentSchema = z.object({
  type: z.enum(['OVERDRAFT_AGREEMENT', 'INDIVIDUAL_TERMS']),
  title: z.string().min(1),
  content: z.string().min(1),
})

export const applicationDocumentsSchema = z.object({
  applicationId: z.number().int().positive(),
  offerId: z.number().int().positive().nullable(),
  documentVersion: z.string().min(1).nullable(),
  documents: z.array(applicationDocumentSchema),
  signingAllowed: z.boolean(),
  signedAt: z.iso.datetime({ offset: true }).nullable(),
  signedBy: z.object({
    id: z.number().int().positive(),
    fullName: z.string().min(1),
  }).nullable(),
  demo: z.boolean(),
}).superRefine((data, context) => {
  const complete = data.offerId !== null && data.documentVersion !== null &&
    data.documents.length === 2 && new Set(data.documents.map((doc) => doc.type)).size === 2
  if (
    (data.signedAt === null) !== (data.signedBy === null) ||
    ((data.signingAllowed || data.signedAt !== null) && !complete) ||
    (data.signingAllowed && data.signedAt !== null)
  ) context.addIssue({ code: 'custom', message: 'Некорректный пакет документов' })
})

export type ApplicationDocument = z.infer<typeof applicationDocumentSchema>
export type ApplicationDocuments = z.infer<typeof applicationDocumentsSchema>

/** Keep document caches isolated by session and application. */
export function applicationDocumentsKey(
  scope: ReturnType<typeof useApiScope>,
  applicationId: number | null | undefined,
) {
  return ['client', scope, 'documents', applicationId] as const
}

/** Bind the server package to the requested application before displaying it. */
export async function getApplicationDocuments(applicationId: number, signal?: AbortSignal) {
  const data = await apiRequest(
    `/api/applications/${applicationId}/documents`,
    applicationDocumentsSchema,
    { signal },
  )
  if (data.applicationId !== applicationId)
    throw new ApiError(200, 'INVALID_RESPONSE', 'Документы не относятся к выбранной заявке')
  return data
}

/** Acknowledgement belongs to the exact displayed texts, not just their filenames. */
export function documentPackageVersion(data: ApplicationDocuments) {
  return JSON.stringify([data.applicationId, data.offerId, data.documentVersion, data.documents])
}

/** Server data is shared by the documents page, dashboard and signing dialog. */
export function useApplicationDocuments(
  applicationId: number | null | undefined,
  enabled = true,
) {
  const scope = useApiScope()
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: applicationDocumentsKey(scope, applicationId),
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => {
      if (applicationId == null) throw new Error('Заявка не выбрана')
      return getApplicationDocuments(applicationId, signal)
    },
    enabled: enabled && applicationId != null && scope !== null,
    retry: false,
    staleTime: 15_000,
    refetchInterval: (query) => query.state.data?.signingAllowed ? 15_000 : false,
  })
}

=== ./src/entities/dashboard/index.ts ===
import { z } from 'zod'
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import {
  applicationDecisionSchema,
  applicationStatusSchema,
} from '@/entities/loan-application'
import { apiRequest, useApiScope } from '@/shared/api'

export const dashboardSchema = z.object({
  company: z.object({
    id: z.number().int(),
    name: z.string(),
    inn: z.string(),
    okved: z.string().nullable(),
    legalForm: z.string().nullable(),
    externalDemo: z
      .object({
        bkiScore: z.number().int(),
        delinquencies12m: z.number().int(),
        inquiries12m: z.number().int(),
        revenueTrend: z.enum(['GROWING', 'STABLE', 'DECLINING']),
        companyAgeYears: z.number().int(),
      })
      .nullish(),
  }),
  currentApplication: z
    .object({
      id: z.number().int(),
      amount: z.number(),
      termDays: z.number().int(),
      rate: z.number().nullable(),
      purpose: z.string().nullable(),
      status: applicationStatusSchema,
      decision: applicationDecisionSchema.nullable(),
      finalRate: z.number().nullable(),
      createdAt: z.iso.datetime({ offset: true }),
    })
    .nullable(),
  stages: z.array(
    z.object({
      code: z.enum(['SUBMITTED', 'REVIEW', 'OFFER', 'SIGNING', 'ISSUED']),
      title: z.string(),
      state: z.enum(['DONE', 'CURRENT', 'PENDING']),
    }),
  ),
  requiredActions: z.array(
    z.object({
      code: z.enum([
        'CREATE_APPLICATION',
        'ACCEPT_OFFER',
        'SIGN_DOCUMENTS',
      ]),
      title: z.string(),
    }),
  ),
  offers: z.array(
    z.object({
      id: z.number().int(),
      amount: z.number(),
      termDays: z.number().int(),
      finalRate: z.number(),
      monthlyPayment: z.number(),
      validUntil: z.iso.date(),
    }),
  ),
  activeLoan: z
    .object({
      id: z.number().int(),
      principal: z.number(),
      ratePercent: z.number(),
      termMonths: z.number().int(),
      monthlyPayment: z.number(),
      remainingBalance: z.number(),
      startDate: z.iso.date(),
    })
    .nullable(),
  nextPayment: z
    .object({ id: z.number().int(), dueDate: z.iso.date(), amount: z.number() })
    .nullable(),
  unreadNotifications: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
})
export type Dashboard = z.infer<typeof dashboardSchema>
/** Load the current client's dashboard and refresh pending credit decisions. */
export function useDashboard() {
  const scope = useApiScope()
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['client', scope, 'dashboard'],
    queryFn: ({ signal }) =>
      apiRequest('/api/dashboard', dashboardSchema, { signal }),
    enabled: scope !== null,
    refetchInterval: (query) => {
      const status = query.state.data?.currentApplication?.status
      return status === 'IN_REVIEW' || status === 'OFFER_READY' || status === 'SIGNATURE_PENDING' ? 15_000 : false
    },
  })
}

=== ./src/entities/application-form/index.ts ===
import { z } from 'zod'
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { apiOptionalRequest, useApiScope } from '@/shared/api'

const optionalTextSchema = z.string().nullable()
const optionalNumberSchema = z.number().nullable()
const requiredText = (message: string) =>
  z.string({ error: message }).trim().min(1, message)

export const revenueTrendSchema = z.enum([
  'GROWING',
  'STABLE',
  'DECLINING',
])

export const externalDataSchema = z.object({
  bkiScore: z.number().int().min(0).max(1_000).nullable(),
  delinquencies12m: z.number().int().min(0).max(24).nullable(),
  inquiries12m: z.number().int().min(0).max(30).nullable(),
  revenueTrend: revenueTrendSchema.nullable(),
  companyAgeYears: z.number().int().min(0).max(100).nullable(),
})

export const applicationApplicantSchema = z.object({
  type: optionalTextSchema,
  companyName: optionalTextSchema,
  inn: optionalTextSchema,
  taxSystem: optionalTextSchema,
  firstName: optionalTextSchema,
  lastName: optionalTextSchema,
  middleName: optionalTextSchema,
  ogrnip: optionalTextSchema,
  ogrn: optionalTextSchema,
  kpp: optionalTextSchema.optional(),
  registrationDate: z.iso.date().nullable(),
  mainOkved: optionalTextSchema,
  industry: optionalTextSchema,
  phone: optionalTextSchema,
  email: optionalTextSchema,
  registrationAddress: optionalTextSchema,
})

export const applicationFormSchema = z.object({
  applicationId: optionalTextSchema,
  createdAt: z.iso.datetime({ offset: true }).nullable(),
  status: optionalTextSchema,
  step1_financingParameters: z.object({
    businessTask: optionalTextSchema,
    amount: optionalNumberSchema,
    termDays: z.number().int().nullable(),
    rate: optionalNumberSchema.optional(),
    purpose: optionalTextSchema,
  }),
  step2_businessData: z.object({
    applicant: applicationApplicantSchema,
    financials: z.object({
      monthlyRevenue: optionalNumberSchema,
      existingDebt: optionalNumberSchema,
      annualRevenue: optionalNumberSchema.optional(),
      averageEmployeeCount: z.number().int().nullable().optional(),
      otherMonthlyObligations: optionalNumberSchema.optional(),
    }),
    externalData: externalDataSchema.nullish(),
  }),
  prefilled: z
    .object({
      source: optionalTextSchema,
      fetchedAt: z.iso.datetime({ offset: true }).nullable(),
      confirmedByUser: z.boolean().nullable(),
    })
    .nullable(),
  step3_verification: z.object({
    consents: z.object({
      personalData: z.boolean(),
      bkiRequest: z.boolean(),
      fnsRequest: z.boolean(),
      acceptedAt: z.iso.datetime({ offset: true }).nullable(),
    }),
  }),
  meta: z
    .object({
      formVersion: optionalTextSchema,
      events: z.array(z.string()).nullable(),
    })
    .nullable(),
})

export const prefillResponseSchema = z.object({
  form: applicationFormSchema,
  aiFilled: z.array(z.string()),
  missingFields: z.array(z.string()),
})

/** Bank form data may be incomplete in historical applications. */
export const applicationBusinessSummarySchema = z
  .object({
    step2_businessData: z
      .object({
        applicant: applicationApplicantSchema
          .pick({
            type: true,
            companyName: true,
            ogrn: true,
            kpp: true,
            registrationDate: true,
            mainOkved: true,
          })
          .partial()
          .nullish(),
        financials:
          applicationFormSchema.shape.step2_businessData.shape.financials
            .partial()
            .nullish(),
        externalData: externalDataSchema.nullish(),
      })
      .nullish(),
  })
  .nullable()

/** Required submission fields; incoming forms and drafts may remain incomplete. */
export const applicationApplicantSubmissionSchema =
  applicationApplicantSchema.extend({
    type: z.literal('IP'),
    companyName: requiredText('Укажите название'),
    inn: requiredText('Укажите ИНН').regex(
      /^\d{12}$/,
      'ИНН ИП должен содержать 12 цифр',
    ),
    taxSystem: requiredText('Выберите систему налогообложения'),
    firstName: requiredText('Укажите фамилию и имя предпринимателя'),
    lastName: requiredText('Укажите фамилию и имя предпринимателя'),
    ogrnip: requiredText('Укажите ОГРНИП').regex(
      /^\d{15}$/,
      'ОГРНИП должен содержать 15 цифр',
    ),
    // Registry metadata is supplied by the server; it may be absent on submission.
    registrationDate: z.iso
      .date({ error: 'Укажите дату регистрации в формате ДД.ММ.ГГГГ' })
      .refine(
        (value) => new Date(`${value}T00:00:00`) <= new Date(),
        'Дата регистрации не может быть в будущем',
      )
      .nullable(),
    mainOkved: requiredText('Укажите основной ОКВЭД').regex(
      /^\d{2}(?:\.\d{1,2}){0,2}$/,
      'Укажите код ОКВЭД, например 47.11',
    ),
    industry: requiredText('Выберите группу отраслей'),
    registrationAddress: requiredText('Укажите адрес регистрации'),
    phone: requiredText('Укажите телефон').regex(
      /^\+7[\s()-]*(?:\d[\s()-]*){10}$/,
      'Укажите российский номер в формате +7XXXXXXXXXX',
    ),
    email: requiredText('Укажите email').pipe(
      z.email({ error: 'Укажите корректный email' }),
    ),
  })

/** Corporate applicants have their own registry identifiers and tax regimes. */
export const oooApplicantSubmissionSchema = applicationApplicantSchema.extend({

=== ./src/entities/session/index.ts ===
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { z } from 'zod'
import {
  useQuery,
  useQueryClient,
  type QueryClient,
} from '@tanstack/react-query'
import {
  apiRequest,
  ApiError,
  beginSessionChange,
  endSessionChange,
  completeSessionRefresh,
  getSessionGeneration,
  hasActiveSessionChange,
  isSessionSwitching,
  sessionEvents,
  syncSessionChanges,
  useSessionTransition,
  type SessionChangeEvent,
} from '@/shared/api'

export const sessionSchema = z.object({
  userId: z.number().int().positive(),
  fullName: z.string(),
  role: z.enum(['CLIENT', 'BANK_OFFICER']),
  companyId: z.number().int().positive().nullable(),
  legalForm: z.string().nullable(),
  phone: z.string().nullable(),
  email: z.string().nullable(),
})
export const demoProfilesSchema = z.array(
  z.object({
    login: z.string(),
    password: z.string(),
    role: z.enum(['CLIENT', 'BANK_OFFICER']),
    fullName: z.string(),
    description: z.string().nullable(),
  }),
)
export type Session = z.infer<typeof sessionSchema>
export type DemoProfile = z.infer<typeof demoProfilesSchema>[number]
export const sessionKey = ['session'] as const

/** Accept private cache updates only for the initiating account and generation. */
export function isCurrentSession(
  cache: QueryClient,
  scope: number | null,
  generation: number,
) {
  syncSessionChanges()
  return (
    scope !== null &&
    generation === getSessionGeneration() &&
    !isSessionSwitching() &&
    cache.getQueryData<Session | null>(sessionKey)?.userId === scope
  )
}

async function fetchSession(signal: AbortSignal) {
  try {
    return await apiRequest('/api/auth/me', sessionSchema, { signal })
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 401) return null
    throw error
  }
}

/** Preserve the observed session query while discarding private data and settled mutations. */
export function resetSession(cache: QueryClient, session: Session | null) {
  void cache.cancelQueries()
  cache.setQueryData(sessionKey, session)
  cache.removeQueries({
    predicate: (query) =>
      query.queryKey[0] !== 'session' && query.queryKey[0] !== 'demo-profiles',
  })
  const mutations = cache.getMutationCache()
  mutations
    .getAll()
    .filter((mutation) => mutation.state.status !== 'pending')
    .forEach((mutation) => mutations.remove(mutation))
}

/** Own authentication transitions outside components that are unmounted by the UI fence. */
export async function changeSession(
  cache: QueryClient,
  operation: () => Promise<Session | null>,
) {
  if (cache.isMutating() > 1)
    throw new Error(
      'Дождитесь завершения текущего действия перед сменой профиля.',
    )
  const change = beginSessionChange()
  resetSession(cache, null)
  try {
    const session = await operation()
    syncSessionChanges()
    const current = change.generation === getSessionGeneration()
    if (current) resetSession(cache, session)
    endSessionChange(change, !current)
    return session
  } catch (error) {
    endSessionChange(
      change,
      true,
      error instanceof Error
        ? error
        : new Error('Не удалось сменить профиль. Повторите попытку.'),
    )
    throw error
  }
}

/** Attach the current QueryClient to cross-tab lifecycle notifications. */
export function useSessionIntegration() {
  const cache = useQueryClient()
  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true
    let refreshAttempt = 0
    const started = () => resetSession(cache, null)
    const refresh = () => {
      if (hasActiveSessionChange()) return
      const attempt = ++refreshAttempt
      resetSession(cache, null)
      const generation = getSessionGeneration()
      void cache
        .fetchQuery({
          queryKey: sessionKey,
          queryFn: ({ signal }) => fetchSession(signal),
          staleTime: 0,
          retry: false,
          networkMode: 'always',
        })
        .catch(() => undefined)
        .finally(() => {
          if (mounted && attempt === refreshAttempt)
            completeSessionRefresh(generation)
        })
    }
    const ended = (event: Event) => {
      if ((event as CustomEvent<SessionChangeEvent>).detail.refresh) refresh()
    }
    sessionEvents.addEventListener('change-start', started)
    sessionEvents.addEventListener('change-end', ended)
    sessionEvents.addEventListener('expired', started)
    syncSessionChanges()
    if (isSessionSwitching() && !hasActiveSessionChange()) refresh()
    return () => {
      mounted = false
      sessionEvents.removeEventListener('change-start', started)
      sessionEvents.removeEventListener('change-end', ended)
      sessionEvents.removeEventListener('expired', started)
    }
  }, [cache])
}

/** Observe the stable session query, pausing polling during authentication changes. */
export function useSession() {
  const transition = useSessionTransition()
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: sessionKey,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => fetchSession(signal),
    enabled: !hasActiveSessionChange(),
    networkMode: 'always',
    staleTime: 0,
    retry: false,
    refetchInterval: transition.switching ? false : 30_000,
  })
}

=== ./src/entities/company/index.ts ===
import { z } from 'zod'

export const companySchema = z.object({
  id: z.number().int().positive(),
  name: z.string(),
  inn: z.string(),
  okved: z.string().nullable(),
  legalForm: z.string().nullable(),
})
export type Company = z.infer<typeof companySchema>

/** Реквизиты, которые клиент может исправить в профиле компании. */
export const companyUpdateSchema = z.object({
  name: z
    .string()
    .trim()
    .min(1, 'Укажите название')
    .max(255, 'Название не должно превышать 255 символов'),
  inn: z
    .string()
    .trim()
    .regex(/^\d{10}(\d{2})?$/, 'ИНН должен содержать 10 или 12 цифр'),
  okved: z.union([
    z.literal(''),
    z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .regex(/^\d{2}(\.\d+)?$/, 'ОКВЭД должен начинаться с двух цифр'),
  ]),
})
export type CompanyUpdate = z.infer<typeof companyUpdateSchema>

export const industryGroups = [
  { key: 'IT', label: 'IT, связь (62, 63)', from: 62, to: 63 },
  { key: 'TRADE', label: 'Торговля (45–47)', from: 45, to: 47 },
  { key: 'PRODUCTION', label: 'Производство (10–33)', from: 10, to: 33 },
  { key: 'CONSTRUCTION', label: 'Строительство (41–43)', from: 41, to: 43 },
  { key: 'CATERING', label: 'Общепит, гостиницы (55, 56)', from: 55, to: 56 },
  { key: 'DEFAULT', label: 'По умолчанию', from: null, to: null },
] as const

/** Classifies an OKVED code for display, without calculating credit terms. */
export function getIndustryGroup(okved: string | null | undefined) {
  const match = okved?.trim().match(/^(\d{2})(?:\.|$)/)
  if (!match) return null
  const code = Number(match[1])
  return (
    industryGroups.find(
      (group) =>
        group.from !== null &&
        group.to !== null &&
        code >= group.from &&
        code <= group.to,
    ) ?? industryGroups[5]
  )
}

/** Formats completed calendar years/months at the application date, or days for a new company. */
export function getCompanyAge(
  registrationDate: string | null | undefined,
  applicationDate: string,
) {
  if (!registrationDate) return null
  const start = new Date(`${registrationDate}T00:00:00Z`)
  const end = new Date(`${applicationDate.slice(0, 10)}T00:00:00Z`)
  if (
    !Number.isFinite(start.getTime()) ||
    !Number.isFinite(end.getTime()) ||
    start > end ||
    start.toISOString().slice(0, 10) !== registrationDate
  )
    return null

  const months =
    (end.getUTCFullYear() - start.getUTCFullYear()) * 12 +
    end.getUTCMonth() -
    start.getUTCMonth() -
    (end.getUTCDate() < start.getUTCDate() ? 1 : 0)
  const format = (value: number, unit: 'year' | 'month' | 'day') =>
    new Intl.NumberFormat('ru-RU', {
      style: 'unit',
      unit,
      unitDisplay: 'long',
    }).format(value)

  if (months < 1) {
    const days = Math.floor((end.getTime() - start.getTime()) / 86_400_000)
    return days === 0 ? 'Зарегистрирована сегодня' : format(days, 'day')
  }
  return [
    months >= 12 ? format(Math.floor(months / 12), 'year') : '',
    months % 12 ? format(months % 12, 'month') : '',
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(' ')
}

  RENT: 'Оплата аренды',

/** Display contract purpose codes while preserving free-text purposes from AI. */
export function getApplicationPurposeLabel(value: string | null | undefined) {
  if (!value) return 'Не указана'
  return Object.hasOwn(applicationPurposeLabels, value)
    ? applicationPurposeLabels[value]
    : value
}

/** Load applications under the current session's isolated query key. */
export function useApplications() {
  const scope = useApiScope()
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['client', scope, 'applications'],
    queryFn: ({ signal }) =>
      apiRequest('/api/applications', applicationViewsSchema, { signal }),
    enabled: scope !== null,
    refetchInterval: (query) =>
      query.state.data?.some(
        (application) =>
          application.status === 'IN_REVIEW' ||
          application.status === 'OFFER_READY' ||
          application.status === 'SIGNATURE_PENDING',
      )
        ? 15_000
        : false,
  })
}

=== ./src/entities/loan-application/get-application-progress.ts ===
import type { Application } from './index'

const stages = [
  { code: 'SUBMITTED', title: 'Заявка' },
  { code: 'REVIEW', title: 'Проверка' },
  { code: 'DECISION', title: 'Статус' },
  { code: 'OFFER', title: 'Предложение' },
  { code: 'SIGNING', title: 'Подписание' },
  { code: 'ISSUED', title: 'Выдача' },
] as const

/** A product stage derived from the application's actual status. */
export interface ApplicationProgressStage {
  code: (typeof stages)[number]['code']
  title: (typeof stages)[number]['title']
  state: 'DONE' | 'CURRENT' | 'PENDING'
}

/** Completes the six-step demo flow after signing without changing loan balances. */
export function getApplicationProgress(
  status: Application['status'],
): ApplicationProgressStage[] {
  const currentCode = {
    IN_REVIEW: 'REVIEW',
    OFFER_READY: 'DECISION',
    ACCEPTED: 'ISSUED',
    DECLINED: 'DECISION',
    SIGNATURE_PENDING: 'SIGNING',
  } as const
  const currentIndex = stages.findIndex(
    (stage) => stage.code === currentCode[status],
  )
  // OFFER_READY awaits client choice; ACCEPTED finishes the displayed workflow.
  return stages.map((stage, index) => ({
    ...stage,
    state:
      status === 'ACCEPTED' || index < currentIndex
        ? 'DONE'
        : index === currentIndex
          ? 'CURRENT'
          : 'PENDING',
  }))
}

import { z } from 'zod'
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { apiRequest, ApiError, useApiScope } from '@/shared/api'

/** Contacts returned by the authenticated profile endpoint, including empty values. */
export const profileSchema = z.object({
  userId: z.number().int().positive(),
  fullName: z.string(),
  phone: z.string().nullable(),
  email: z.string().nullable(),
})

/** Both fields are required in PUT; empty strings and null clear a contact. */
export const profileContactsSchema = z.object({
  phone: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^$|^\+?[0-9][0-9\-\s()]{9,18}$/, 'Некорректный номер телефона')
    .nullable(),
  email: z
    .union([
      z.literal(''),
      z.email({ error: 'Укажите корректный email' }).max(
        255,
        'Email не должен превышать 255 символов',
      ),
    ])
    .nullable(),
})

export type Profile = z.infer<typeof profileSchema>
export type ProfileContactsInput = z.infer<typeof profileContactsSchema>

/** Isolate client profile data by the authenticated user, not by company. */
export const profileKey = (scope: number | null) =>
  ['client', scope, 'profile'] as const

/** Read the current user's contacts through the shared session-fenced transport. */
export function useProfile() {
  const scope = useApiScope()
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: profileKey(scope),
    queryFn: async ({ signal }) => {
      const profile = await apiRequest('/api/profile', profileSchema, { signal })
      if (profile.userId !== scope)
        throw new ApiError(
          200,
          'INVALID_RESPONSE',
          'Не удалось проверить владельца профиля.',
        )
      return profile
    },
    enabled: scope !== null,
    staleTime: 0,
    retry: false,
  })
}

=== ./src/shared/api/event-stream.test.cjs ===
const assert = require('node:assert/strict')
const { readFileSync } = require('node:fs')
const path = require('node:path')
const vm = require('node:vm')
const { test } = require('node:test')
const ts = require('typescript')

const root = path.resolve(__dirname, '../..')

function loadClient(session, EventSource) {
  const file = path.join(root, 'shared/api/client.ts')
  const source = readFileSync(file, 'utf8').replace(
    /const baseUrl =\s*\(import\.meta\.env[\s\S]*?\) \?\? ''/,
    "const baseUrl = ''",
  )
  assert.ok(!source.includes('import.meta.env'))
  const compiled = ts.transpileModule(source, {
    compilerOptions: {
      module: ts.ModuleKind.CommonJS,
      target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2023,
    },
  }).outputText
  const module = { exports: {} }
  vm.runInNewContext(
    `(function(require, module, exports) { ${compiled}\n})`,
    { AbortSignal, EventSource, Headers, fetch, setTimeout, clearTimeout },
    { filename: file },
  )((id) => {
    if (id === 'zod') return require('zod')
    if (id === './session-lifecycle') return session
    throw new Error(`Unexpected dependency: ${id}`)
  }, module, module.exports)
  return module.exports
}

class FakeEventSource {
  static CLOSED = 2
  static instances = []

  constructor(url, options) {
    this.url = url
    this.options = options
    this.readyState = 1
    this.closed = false
    this.listeners = new Map()
    FakeEventSource.instances.push(this)
  }

  addEventListener(name, listener) {
    const listeners = this.listeners.get(name) ?? []
    listeners.push(listener)
    this.listeners.set(name, listeners)
  }

  emit(name, data) {
    for (const listener of this.listeners.get(name) ?? []) listener({ data })
  }

  close() {
    this.closed = true
    this.readyState = FakeEventSource.CLOSED
  }
}

function environment() {
  const sessionController = new AbortController()
  const state = { generation: 0, switching: false }
  return {
    state,
    sessionController,
    api: loadClient(
      {
        expireSession() {},
        getSessionGeneration: () => state.generation,
        getSessionSignal: () => sessionController.signal,
        hasActiveSessionChange: () => false,
        isSessionSwitching: () => state.switching,
        syncSessionChanges() {},
      },
      FakeEventSource,
    ),
  }
}

test('SSE uses session cookies, named events and closes on a session abort', () => {
  FakeEventSource.instances = []
  const { api, sessionController } = environment()
  const events = []
  const close = api.apiEventStream('/api/notifications/stream', {
    events: {
      connected: (data) => events.push(['connected', data]),
      notification: (data) => events.push(['notification', data]),
    },
    onError: (error) => assert.fail(error.message),
  })
  const source = FakeEventSource.instances[0]
  assert.equal(source.url, '/api/notifications/stream')
  assert.equal(source.options.withCredentials, true)
  source.emit('connected', 'ok')
  source.emit('notification', '{"id":1}')
  assert.deepEqual(events, [
    ['connected', 'ok'],
    ['notification', '{"id":1}'],
  ])
  sessionController.abort()
  assert.equal(source.closed, true)
  source.emit('notification', '{"id":2}')
  assert.equal(events.length, 2)
  close()
})

test('a changed authentication generation fences late SSE events', () => {
  FakeEventSource.instances = []
  const { api, state } = environment()
  let received = 0
  api.apiEventStream('/api/notifications/stream', {
    events: { notification: () => received++ },
    onError: (error) => assert.fail(error.message),
  })
  const source = FakeEventSource.instances[0]
  state.generation++
  source.emit('notification', '{"id":1}')
  assert.equal(received, 0)
  assert.equal(source.closed, true)
})

=== ./src/shared/api/client.ts ===
import { z } from 'zod'
import {
  expireSession,
  getSessionGeneration,
  getSessionSignal,
  hasActiveSessionChange,
  isSessionSwitching,
  syncSessionChanges,
} from './session-lifecycle'

const errorSchema = z.object({ code: z.string(), message: z.string() })
const baseUrl =
  (import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL as string | undefined)?.replace(
    /\/$/,
    '',
  ) ?? ''

/** Validated server error or a transport/contract failure exposed to the UI. */
export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number
  code: string
  constructor(status: number, code: string, message: string) {
    super(message)
    this.status = status
    this.code = code
    this.name = 'ApiError'
  }
}

function assertGeneration(generation: number) {
  syncSessionChanges()
  if (generation !== getSessionGeneration())
    throw new ApiError(
      409,
      'SESSION_CHANGED',
      'Профиль изменился. Повторите действие в текущем профиле.',
    )
}

function networkError() {
  return new ApiError(
    0,
    'NETWORK_ERROR',
    'Не удалось связаться с сервером. Проверьте соединение и повторите попытку.',
  )
}

function assertRequest(
  generation: number,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  callerSignal?: AbortSignal | null,
) {
  assertGeneration(generation)
  if (callerSignal?.aborted) throw callerSignal.reason
  if (signal.aborted) throw networkError()
}

async function readJson(
  response: Response,
  generation: number,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  callerSignal?: AbortSignal | null,
): Promise<unknown> {
  try {
    const body: unknown = await response.json()
    assertRequest(generation, signal, callerSignal)
    return body
  } catch (error) {
    assertRequest(generation, signal, callerSignal)
    if (error instanceof Error && error.name === 'SyntaxError') {
      if (!response.ok) return undefined
      throw new ApiError(
        response.status,
        'INVALID_RESPONSE',
        'Сервер вернул данные в неожиданном формате. Повторите запрос.',
      )
    }
    if (error instanceof Error && error.name === 'AbortError') throw error
    throw networkError()
  }
}

async function request(path: string, options: RequestInit = {}) {
  syncSessionChanges()
  const generation = getSessionGeneration()
  const isAuthAction = path === '/api/auth/login' || path === '/api/auth/logout'
  if (
    isSessionSwitching() &&
    !isAuthAction &&
    path !== '/api/demo/profiles' &&
    !(path === '/api/auth/me' && !hasActiveSessionChange())
  ) {
    throw new ApiError(
      409,
      'SESSION_CHANGED',
      'Выполняется смена профиля. Дождитесь завершения.',
    )
  }
  let response: Response
  const headers = new Headers(options.headers)
  if (options.body && !headers.has('Content-Type'))
    headers.set('Content-Type', 'application/json')
  const signal = AbortSignal.any([
    getSessionSignal(),
    AbortSignal.timeout(120_000),
    ...(options.signal ? [options.signal] : []),
  ])
  try {
    assertRequest(generation, signal, options.signal)
    response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}${path}`, {
      ...options,
      credentials: 'include',
      headers,
      signal,
    })
  } catch (error) {
    assertRequest(generation, signal, options.signal)
    if (error instanceof Error && error.name === 'AbortError') throw error
    throw networkError()
  }
  assertRequest(generation, signal, options.signal)
  if (!response.ok) {
    const result = errorSchema.safeParse(
      await readJson(response, generation, signal, options.signal),
    )
    assertRequest(generation, signal, options.signal)
    const error = new ApiError(
      response.status,
      result.success ? result.data.code : 'HTTP_ERROR',
      result.success
        ? result.data.message
        : `Ошибка сервера (${response.status}). Повторите попытку.`,
    )
    if (
      response.status === 401 &&
      path !== '/api/auth/me' &&
      !path.startsWith('/api/auth/') &&
      error.code !== 'BAD_CREDENTIALS'
    )
      expireSession()
    throw error
  }
  return { response, generation, signal }
}

/** Send a cookie-authenticated request and validate its JSON response. */
export async function apiRequest<T>(
  path: string,
  schema: z.ZodType<T>,
  options?: RequestInit,
): Promise<T> {
  const { response, generation, signal } = await request(path, options)
  const result = schema.safeParse(
    await readJson(response, generation, signal, options?.signal),
  )
  assertRequest(generation, signal, options?.signal)
  if (!result.success)
    throw new ApiError(
      response.status,
      'INVALID_RESPONSE',
      'Сервер вернул данные в неожиданном формате. Повторите запрос.',
    )
  return result.data
}

export async function apiOptionalRequest<T>(
  path: string,
  schema: z.ZodType<T>,
  options?: RequestInit,
): Promise<T | null> {
  const { response, generation, signal } = await request(path, options)
  if (response.status === 204) return null
  const result = schema.safeParse(
    await readJson(response, generation, signal, options?.signal),
  )
  assertRequest(generation, signal, options?.signal)
  if (!result.success)
    throw new ApiError(
      response.status,
      'INVALID_RESPONSE',
      'Сервер вернул данные в неожиданном формате. Повторите запрос.',
    )
  return result.data
}

/** Send a request whose successful response does not require a JSON body. */
export async function apiEmpty(
  path: string,
  options?: RequestInit,
): Promise<void> {
  const { generation, signal } = await request(path, options)
  assertRequest(generation, signal, options?.signal)
}

/** Named SSE events share the HTTP client's origin, cookies and session fence. */
export interface ApiEventStreamOptions {
  events: Record<string, (data: string) => void>
  onError: (error: ApiError) => void
  signal?: AbortSignal
}

/** Subscribe until disposal or a session change; reconnect closed transports. */
export function apiEventStream(path: string, options: ApiEventStreamOptions) {
  syncSessionChanges()
  const generation = getSessionGeneration()
  const signal = AbortSignal.any([
    getSessionSignal(),
    ...(options.signal ? [options.signal] : []),
  ])
  let source: EventSource | null = null
  let retry: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
  let retryDelay = 3_000
  let disposed = false

  function close() {
    disposed = true
    clearTimeout(retry)
    source?.close()
    source = null
    signal.removeEventListener('abort', close)
  }

  function isCurrent() {
    syncSessionChanges()
    if (
      signal.aborted ||
      generation !== getSessionGeneration() ||
      isSessionSwitching()
    )
      close()
    return !disposed
  }

  function reconnect() {
    if (!isCurrent()) return
    clearTimeout(retry)
    retry = setTimeout(connect, retryDelay)
    retryDelay = Math.min(retryDelay * 2, 30_000)
  }

  function connect() {
    if (!isCurrent()) return
    try {
      const connection = new EventSource(`${baseUrl}${path}`, {
        withCredentials: true,
      })
      source = connection
      connection.addEventListener('open', () => {
        if (isCurrent() && source === connection) retryDelay = 3_000
      })
      for (const [name, handle] of Object.entries(options.events)) {
        connection.addEventListener(name, (event) => {
          if (!isCurrent() || source !== connection) return
          const data: unknown = (event as MessageEvent<unknown>).data
          if (typeof data === 'string') handle(data)
          else
            options.onError(
              new ApiError(200, 'INVALID_RESPONSE', 'Не удалось прочитать событие сервера.'),
            )
        })
      }
      connection.addEventListener('error', () => {
        if (!isCurrent() || source !== connection) return
        options.onError(
          new ApiError(0, 'STREAM_DISCONNECTED', 'Соединение прервано. Повторяем подключение.'),
        )
        // CONNECTING retries natively; HTTP failures may leave EventSource CLOSED.
        if (connection.readyState === EventSource.CLOSED) {
          connection.close()
          source = null
          reconnect()
        }
      })
    } catch {
      if (!isCurrent()) return
      options.onError(
        new ApiError(0, 'STREAM_DISCONNECTED', 'Не удалось подключиться к потоку событий.'),
      )
      reconnect()
    }
  }

  signal.addEventListener('abort', close, { once: true })
  connect()
  return close
}

=== ./src/shared/api/session-lifecycle.ts ===
import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react'

export const sessionEvents = new EventTarget()

const storageKey = 'session-lifecycle'
const transitionTimeout = 125_000
const source = crypto.randomUUID()
const active = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>()
const seen = new Set<string>()
let controller = new AbortController()
let refreshing = false
let snapshot = { generation: 0, switching: false, error: null as Error | null }

/** Cross-tab lifecycle notification. No identity or credentials are transmitted. */
export interface SessionChangeEvent {
  refresh: boolean
}

/** Token identifying the authentication generation that started a transition. */
export interface SessionChange {
  id: string
  generation: number
}

interface Message {
  source: string
  id: string
  phase: 'start' | 'end'
  time: number
}

function publish() {
  snapshot = { ...snapshot, switching: active.size > 0 || refreshing }
  sessionEvents.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'))
}

function invalidate() {
  controller.abort()
  controller = new AbortController()
  snapshot = { ...snapshot, generation: snapshot.generation + 1 }
}

function ended(refresh: boolean) {
  refreshing ||= refresh
  publish()
  sessionEvents.dispatchEvent(
    new CustomEvent<SessionChangeEvent>('change-end', { detail: { refresh } }),
  )
}

function receive(message: Message) {
  if (message.source === source) return
  const key = `${message.id}:${message.phase}`
  if (
    seen.has(key) ||
    (message.phase === 'start' && seen.has(`${message.id}:end`))
  )
    return
  seen.add(key)
  if (seen.size > 200) seen.delete(seen.values().next().value!)
  invalidate()
  if (message.phase === 'start') {
    snapshot = { ...snapshot, error: null }
    const remaining = Math.max(
      0,
      transitionTimeout - (Date.now() - message.time),
    )
    active.set(
      message.id,
      setTimeout(() => {
        active.delete(message.id)
        invalidate()
        ended(true)
      }, remaining),
    )
    publish()
    sessionEvents.dispatchEvent(new Event('change-start'))
  } else {
    clearTimeout(active.get(message.id))
    active.delete(message.id)
    ended(true)
  }
}

function readMessage(value: string | null): Message | null {
  try {
    const data: unknown = JSON.parse(value ?? 'null')
    if (typeof data !== 'object' || data === null) return null
    const message = data as Record<string, unknown>
    if (
      typeof message.source !== 'string' ||
      typeof message.id !== 'string' ||
      typeof message.time !== 'number' ||
      (message.phase !== 'start' && message.phase !== 'end')
    )
      return null
    return message as unknown as Message
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

const channel =
  typeof BroadcastChannel === 'undefined'
    ? null
    : new BroadcastChannel(storageKey)
if (channel)
  channel.onmessage = (event: MessageEvent<unknown>) => {
    const message = readMessage(JSON.stringify(event.data))
    if (message) receive(message)
  }
if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
  window.addEventListener('storage', (event) => {
    if (event.key !== storageKey) return
    const message = readMessage(event.newValue)
    if (message) receive(message)
  })
  window.addEventListener('pageshow', syncSessionChanges)
  window.addEventListener('focus', syncSessionChanges)
}

function send(id: string, phase: Message['phase']) {
  const message: Message = { source, id, phase, time: Date.now() }
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(message))
  } catch {
    /* BroadcastChannel remains available when storage is restricted. */
  }
  channel?.postMessage(message)
}

/** Read the shared fence before sending or accepting an API response. */
export function syncSessionChanges() {
  try {
    const message = readMessage(localStorage.getItem(storageKey))
    if (message) receive(message)
  } catch {
    /* Storage can be unavailable in privacy mode. */
  }
}

/** Start an exclusive local authentication transition and invalidate old requests. */
export function beginSessionChange(): SessionChange {
  syncSessionChanges()
  if (snapshot.switching)
    throw new Error('Смена профиля уже выполняется. Дождитесь завершения.')
  const id = crypto.randomUUID()
  invalidate()
  snapshot = { ...snapshot, error: null }
  active.set(
    id,
    setTimeout(
      () => endSessionChange({ id, generation: snapshot.generation }, true),
      transitionTimeout,
    ),
  )
  publish()
  send(id, 'start')
  sessionEvents.dispatchEvent(new Event('change-start'))
  return { id, generation: snapshot.generation }
}

/** Always finish a transition, including failed requests, then reconcile if necessary. */
export function endSessionChange(
  change: SessionChange,
  refresh = false,
  error: Error | null = null,
) {
  if (!active.has(change.id)) return
  clearTimeout(active.get(change.id))
  active.delete(change.id)
  invalidate()
  snapshot = { ...snapshot, error }
  send(change.id, 'end')
  ended(refresh)
}

/** Release the UI only if reconciliation still belongs to the current generation. */
export function completeSessionRefresh(generation: number) {
  if (generation !== snapshot.generation || active.size > 0) return
  refreshing = false
  publish()
}

/** Expire locally immediately and make other tabs verify their shared cookie. */
export function expireSession() {
  if (snapshot.switching) return
  const change = beginSessionChange()
  sessionEvents.dispatchEvent(new Event('expired'))
  endSessionChange(change)
}

export const getSessionGeneration = () => snapshot.generation
export const getSessionSignal = () => controller.signal
export const isSessionSwitching = () => snapshot.switching
export const hasActiveSessionChange = () => active.size > 0

function subscribe(listener: () => void) {
  sessionEvents.addEventListener('change', listener)
  return () => sessionEvents.removeEventListener('change', listener)
}

/** Shared synchronous UI fence for local and remote authentication changes. */
export function useSessionTransition() {
  return useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribe,
    () => snapshot,
    () => snapshot,
  )
}

syncSessionChanges()

=== ./src/shared/ui/Panel/Panel.tsx ===
import { useId, type ReactNode, type RefObject } from 'react'
import { SidePanel } from '@alfalab/core-components/side-panel'
import { CrossMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/CrossMIcon'
import styles from './Panel.module.scss'

export interface PanelProps {
  open: boolean
  title: string
  onClose: () => void
  children: ReactNode
  footer?: ReactNode
  dismissible?: boolean
  returnFocusRef?: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>
}

export function Panel({
  open,
  title,
  onClose,
  children,
  footer,
  dismissible = true,
  returnFocusRef,
}: PanelProps) {
  const titleId = useId()

  return (
    <SidePanel
      open={open}
      onClose={() => {
        if (dismissible) onClose()
      }}
      className={styles.panel}
      scrollLock
      disableEscapeKeyDown={!dismissible}
      disableBackdropClick={!dismissible}
      onUnmount={() => {
        if (returnFocusRef)
          requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            if (
              returnFocusRef.current?.isConnected &&
              !document.querySelector('[role="dialog"]')
            )
              returnFocusRef.current.focus()
          })
      }}
      wrapperProps={{ 'aria-labelledby': titleId }}
    >
      <SidePanel.Header
        hasCloser={dismissible}
        closerProps={{
          icon: () => <CrossMIcon aria-hidden="true" />,
          'aria-label': 'Закрыть панель',
        }}
        title={<span id={titleId}>{title}</span>}
      />
      <SidePanel.Content>{children}</SidePanel.Content>
      {footer && <SidePanel.Footer sticky>{footer}</SidePanel.Footer>}
    </SidePanel>
  )
}

=== ./src/shared/ui/Skeleton/Skeleton.tsx ===
import type { CSSProperties } from 'react'
import styles from './Skeleton.module.scss'

export interface SkeletonProps {
  width?: CSSProperties['width']
  height?: CSSProperties['height']
  radius?: CSSProperties['borderRadius']
  className?: string
}

export function Skeleton({
  width = '100%',
  height = '1rem',
  radius,
  className,
}: SkeletonProps) {
  return (
    <span
      className={`${styles.skeleton}${className ? ` ${className}` : ''}`}
      style={{ inlineSize: width, blockSize: height, borderRadius: radius }}
      aria-hidden="true"
    />
  )
}

=== ./src/shared/ui/RequestState/RequestState.tsx ===
import { Button } from '@alfalab/core-components/button'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import styles from './RequestState.module.scss'

export interface RequestStateProps {
  error?: Error | null
  retry?: () => void
  children?: ReactNode
}
export function RequestState({ error, retry, children }: RequestStateProps) {
  return (
    <div className={styles.state} role={error ? 'alert' : 'status'}>
      <p>{error ? error.message : (children ?? 'Загружаем данные…')}</p>
      {error && retry && (
        <Button view="secondary" onClick={retry}>
          Повторить
        </Button>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

=== ./src/shared/ui/FieldLabel/FieldLabel.tsx ===
import { useId, useState } from 'react'
import { TooltipDesktop } from '@alfalab/core-components/tooltip/desktop'
import { InformationCircleLineSIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/InformationCircleLineSIcon'
import styles from './FieldLabel.module.scss'

export interface FieldLabelProps {
  htmlFor?: string
  text: string
  hint?: string
}

export function FieldLabel({ htmlFor, text, hint }: FieldLabelProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const tooltipId = useId()
  return (
    <div className={styles.label}>
      {htmlFor ? <label htmlFor={htmlFor}>{text}</label> : <span>{text}</span>}
      {hint && (
        <TooltipDesktop
          open={open}
          trigger="hover"
          position="top"
          fallbackPlacements={['bottom', 'right', 'left']}
          onOpen={() => setOpen(true)}
          onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
          content={
            <span id={tooltipId} role="tooltip">
              {hint}
            </span>
          }
          contentClassName={styles.hint}
          targetTag="span"
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.button}
            aria-label={`${text}: подсказка`}
            aria-describedby={open ? tooltipId : undefined}
            onFocus={() => setOpen(true)}
            onBlur={() => setOpen(false)}
            onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
            onKeyDown={(event) => {
              if (event.key === 'Escape' && open) {
                event.preventDefault()
                event.stopPropagation()
                setOpen(false)
              }
            }}
          >
            <InformationCircleLineSIcon aria-hidden="true" />
          </button>
        </TooltipDesktop>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

=== ./src/shared/lib/format-date.ts ===
export function formatDate(value: string) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ru-RU', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(new Date(value.length === 10 ? `${value}T12:00:00` : value))
}

=== ./src/shared/lib/index.ts ===
export { formatMoney } from './format-money'

=== ./src/shared/lib/format-money.ts ===
const rubles = new Intl.NumberFormat('ru-RU', {
  style: 'currency',
  currency: 'RUB',
  maximumFractionDigits: 0,
})

const exactRubles = new Intl.NumberFormat('ru-RU', {
  style: 'currency',
  currency: 'RUB',
  minimumFractionDigits: 0,
  maximumFractionDigits: 2,
})

/** Formats rubles, optionally preserving kopecks without changing the default. */
export function formatMoney(
  amount: number,
  options?: { exact?: boolean },
): string {
  return (options?.exact ? exactRubles : rubles).format(amount)
}

=== ./src/app/providers/query-client.ts ===
import { QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { ApiError } from '@/shared/api'

export const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      staleTime: 60_000,
      gcTime: 5 * 60_000,
      retry: (failureCount, error) =>
        !(error instanceof ApiError && error.status >= 400 && error.status < 500) &&
        failureCount < 1,
    },
  },
})

=== ./src/app/layouts/SessionLayout.tsx ===
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { matchPath, Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { useSession, useSessionIntegration } from '@/entities/session'
import { ApiScopeContext, useSessionTransition } from '@/shared/api'
import { ROUTES } from '@/shared/config'
import { RequestState } from '@/shared/ui'
import { BankSkeleton } from './BankSkeleton'
import { CabinetSkeleton } from './CabinetSkeleton'

export function SessionLayout() {
  const session = useSession()
  const transition = useSessionTransition()
  useSessionIntegration()
  const cache = useQueryClient()
  const location = useLocation()
  const isLoginRoute =
    matchPath({ path: ROUTES.login, end: true }, location.pathname) !== null
  const isBankRoute =
    matchPath({ path: ROUTES.bank, end: true }, location.pathname) !== null
  const pendingFallback = isLoginRoute ? (
    <RequestState />
  ) : isBankRoute ? (
    <BankSkeleton />
  ) : (
    <CabinetSkeleton />
  )
  useEffect(() => {
    const currentUserId = session.data?.userId
    if (currentUserId !== undefined) {
      const otherUser = (query: { queryKey: readonly unknown[] }) =>
        (query.queryKey[0] === 'client' || query.queryKey[0] === 'bank') &&
        query.queryKey[1] !== currentUserId
      void cache.cancelQueries({ predicate: otherUser })
      cache.removeQueries({ predicate: otherUser })
    }
    if (session.data === null) {
      void cache.cancelQueries({
        predicate: (q) =>
          q.queryKey[0] !== 'session' && q.queryKey[0] !== 'demo-profiles',
      })
      cache.removeQueries({
        predicate: (q) =>
          q.queryKey[0] !== 'session' && q.queryKey[0] !== 'demo-profiles',
      })
    }
  }, [cache, session.data])
  if (transition.switching) return isLoginRoute ? <Outlet /> : pendingFallback
  if (session.isPending || (!isLoginRoute && !session.data && session.isFetching))
    return pendingFallback
  if (session.error)
    return (
      <RequestState
        error={session.error}
        retry={() => {
          void session.refetch()
        }}
      />
    )
  if (!session.data)
    return isLoginRoute ? <Outlet /> : <Navigate to={ROUTES.login} replace />
  const isBank = session.data.role === 'BANK_OFFICER'
  if (isLoginRoute || (isBank && !isBankRoute) || (!isBank && isBankRoute))
    return <Navigate to={isBank ? ROUTES.bank : ROUTES.dashboard} replace />
  return (
    <ApiScopeContext.Provider value={session.data.userId}>
      <div key={session.data.userId}>
        {transition.error && <RequestState error={transition.error} />}
        <Outlet />
      </div>
    </ApiScopeContext.Provider>
  )
}

=== ./src/app/layouts/CabinetLayout/CabinetLayout.tsx ===
import { Outlet } from 'react-router-dom'
import { HeaderNavigation } from '@/widgets/header-navigation'
import { AiCopilotDrawer } from '@/widgets/ai-copilot-drawer'
import { useDashboard } from '@/entities/dashboard'
import { applicationStatusLabels } from '@/entities/loan-application'
import { LegalForm } from '@/features/demo-session'
import styles from './CabinetLayout.module.scss'

export function CabinetLayout() {
  const dashboard = useDashboard()
  const application = dashboard.data?.currentApplication
  return (
    <div className={styles.layout}>
      <a href="#main-content" className={styles.skipLink}>
        Перейти к содержимому
      </a>
      <HeaderNavigation />
      <div id="main-content" className={styles.content} tabIndex={-1}>
        <Outlet />
      </div>
      {dashboard.data && !dashboard.error && (
        <AiCopilotDrawer
          companyName={dashboard.data.company.name}
          application={
            application
              ? {
                  amount: application.amount,
                  purpose: application.purpose ?? 'Цель не указана',
                  statusLabel: applicationStatusLabels[application.status],
                }
              : null
          }
        />
      )}
      <LegalForm />
    </div>
  )
}

}

export function CabinetSkeleton({ children }: CabinetSkeletonProps) {

          <span className={styles.menuControl}>

              width="2.5rem"

              radius="var(--border-radius-8)"
            />
          </span>

            <AlfaMark />
          </span>
        </div>

          <Skeleton
            width="2.5rem"
            height="2.5rem"
            radius="var(--border-radius-12)"
          />
          <div className={styles.companyText}>
            <Skeleton width="min(12rem, 100%)" height="0.875rem" />
            <Skeleton width="min(9rem, 100%)" height="0.75rem" />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className={styles.headerActions}>
          <Skeleton width="2.5rem" height="2.5rem" radius="50%" />
          <Skeleton
            width="6rem"
            height="2.5rem"
            radius="var(--border-radius-12)"
          />
        </div>
      </header>

      <aside className={styles.sidebar}>
        <Skeleton
          className={styles.sidebarLabel}
          width="4.5rem"
          height="0.75rem"
        />
        <nav className={styles.navigation}>
          {navigationRows.map((width) => (
            <div className={styles.navLink} key={width}>
              <Skeleton width="1.5rem" height="1.5rem" radius="50%" />
              <Skeleton width={width} height="0.875rem" />
            </div>
          ))}
        </nav>

          <div className={styles.profileNote}>
            <Skeleton width="4rem" height="0.75rem" />
            <Skeleton width="9rem" height="0.875rem" />
          </div>
          <Skeleton
            width="6.5rem"
            height="2.5rem"
            radius="var(--border-radius-12)"
          />
        </div>
      </aside>

      <div className={styles.content}>{children ?? <DashboardPage />}</div>
    </div>
  )
}

=== ./src/widgets/header-navigation/ui/HeaderNavigation/index.ts ===
export { HeaderNavigation } from './HeaderNavigation'

=== ./src/widgets/header-navigation/ui/HeaderNavigation/HeaderNavigation.tsx ===
import { useState } from 'react'
import { NavLink, Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { Badge } from '@alfalab/core-components/badge'
import { Button } from '@alfalab/core-components/button'
import { Typography } from '@alfalab/core-components/typography'
import { BriefcaseMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/BriefcaseMIcon'
import { BurgerMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/BurgerMIcon'
import { ChevronDownMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/ChevronDownMIcon'
import { DocumentMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/DocumentMIcon'
import { FolderMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/FolderMIcon'
import { HouseMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/HouseMIcon'
import { InformationCircleLineMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/InformationCircleLineMIcon'
import { WalletMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/WalletMIcon'
import { useDashboard } from '@/entities/dashboard'
import { useApplications } from '@/entities/loan-application'
import { SessionActions } from '@/features/demo-session'
import { ProfileCompany, ProfileContacts } from '@/features/edit-profile'
import { NotificationCenter } from '@/features/read-notifications'
import { ROUTES } from '@/shared/config'
import { AlfaMark, Panel, Skeleton } from '@/shared/ui'
import styles from './HeaderNavigation.module.scss'

const items = [
  { path: ROUTES.dashboard, label: 'Главная', Icon: HouseMIcon },
  { path: ROUTES.applications, label: 'Заявки', Icon: DocumentMIcon },
  { path: ROUTES.credits, label: 'Кредиты и лимиты', Icon: WalletMIcon },
  {
    path: ROUTES.documents,
    label: 'Документы',
    Icon: FolderMIcon,
  },
]

export interface NavigationProps {
  onNavigate?: () => void
}

function Navigation({ onNavigate }: NavigationProps) {
  const applications = useApplications()
  return (
    <nav aria-label="Основная навигация" className={styles.navigation}>
      {items.map(({ path, label, Icon }) => (
        <NavLink
          key={path}
          to={path}
          end
          onClick={onNavigate}
          className={({ isActive }) =>
            `${styles.navLink} ${isActive ? styles.active : ''}`
          }
        >
          <Icon aria-hidden="true" />
          <Typography.Text view="primary-small" weight="medium">
            {label}
          </Typography.Text>
          {path === ROUTES.applications &&
            applications.data &&
            !applications.error && (
              <Badge
                view="count"
                content={applications.data.length}
                color="specialbg-secondary-transparent"
              />
            )}
        </NavLink>
      ))}
    </nav>
  )
}

export function HeaderNavigation() {
  const [panel, setPanel] = useState<
    'menu' | 'notifications' | 'profile' | null
  >(null)
  const dashboard = useDashboard()
  const company = dashboard.error ? undefined : dashboard.data?.company
  const companyPending = !company && !dashboard.error
  const companyName = company?.name ?? 'Кредитный кабинет'
  const companyDetails = company
    ? `ИНН ${company.inn}${company.okved ? ` · ОКВЭД ${company.okved}` : ''}`
    : dashboard.error
      ? 'Не удалось загрузить данные компании'
      : 'Данные компании загружаются'
  const closePanel = () => setPanel(null)

  return (
    <>
      <header className={styles.header}>
        <div className={styles.logoSlot}>
          <span className={styles.menuControl}>
            <Button
              view="transparent"
              size={40}
              aria-label="Открыть меню"
              onClick={() => setPanel('menu')}
            >
              <BurgerMIcon aria-hidden="true" />
            </Button>
          </span>
          <Link
            to={ROUTES.dashboard}
            className={styles.logo}
            aria-label="Альфа-Банк, главная"
          >
            <AlfaMark />
          </Link>
        </div>
        <Button
          view="transparent"
          size={56}
          className={styles.companyButton}
          labelClassName={styles.companyLabel}
          aria-label={
            companyPending ? 'Загружаем данные компании' : undefined
          }
          onClick={() => setPanel('profile')}
          leftAddons={
            companyPending ? (
              <Skeleton
                width="2.5rem"
                height="2.5rem"
                radius="var(--border-radius-12)"
              />
            ) : (
              <span className={styles.avatar}>
                <BriefcaseMIcon aria-hidden="true" />
              </span>
            )
          }
          rightAddons={
            companyPending ? undefined : (
              <ChevronDownMIcon aria-hidden="true" />
            )
          }
        >
          {companyPending ? (
            <span className={styles.companyText} aria-hidden="true">
              <Skeleton width="min(12rem, 100%)" height="0.875rem" />
              <Skeleton width="min(9rem, 100%)" height="0.75rem" />
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className={styles.companyText}>
              <Typography.Text view="primary-small" weight="bold">
                {companyName}
              </Typography.Text>
              <Typography.Text
                view="secondary-medium"
                color="secondary"
                className={styles.companyActivity}
              >
                {companyDetails}
              </Typography.Text>
            </span>
          )}
        </Button>
        <div className={styles.headerActions}>
          <NotificationCenter
            className={styles.notificationControl}
            open={panel === 'notifications'}
            onOpen={() => setPanel('notifications')}
            onClose={closePanel}
          />
          <SessionActions compact />
        </div>
      </header>

      <aside className={styles.sidebar}>
        <Typography.Text
          view="caps"
          color="secondary"
          className={styles.sidebarLabel}
        >
          Финансирование
        </Typography.Text>
        <Navigation />
        <div className={styles.sidebarBottom}>
          <div className={styles.profileNote}>
            <Typography.Text view="secondary-medium" color="secondary">
              Ваш бизнес
            </Typography.Text>
            {companyPending ? (
              <Skeleton width="min(9rem, 100%)" height="0.875rem" />
            ) : (
              <Typography.Text view="primary-small" weight="medium">
                {companyName}
              </Typography.Text>
            )}
          </div>
          <Button
            view="transparent"
            size={40}
            leftAddons={<InformationCircleLineMIcon aria-hidden="true" />}
            onClick={() => setPanel('profile')}
          >
            О компании
          </Button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <Panel
        open={panel === 'menu'}
        title="Кредитный кабинет"
        onClose={closePanel}
      >
        <Navigation onNavigate={closePanel} />
        <div className={styles.panelContent}>
          <Button
            view="secondary"
            block
            onClick={() => setPanel('notifications')}
          >
            Уведомления
          </Button>
        </div>
      </Panel>
      <Panel
        open={panel === 'profile'}
        title="Профиль компании"
        onClose={closePanel}
      >
        <div className={styles.panelContent}>
          {companyPending ? (
            <>
              <Skeleton width="min(12rem, 80%)" height="1.5rem" />
              <Skeleton width="min(10rem, 60%)" height="1rem" />
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Typography.TitleResponsive tag="h2" view="small" font="system">
                {companyName}
              </Typography.TitleResponsive>
              <Typography.Text tag="p" color="secondary">
                {companyDetails}
              </Typography.Text>
            </>
          )}
          {company && (
            <div className={styles.panelNotice}>
              <Typography.Text tag="p" weight="medium">
                Форма бизнеса
              </Typography.Text>
              <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-small" color="secondary">
                {company.legalForm === 'IP'
                  ? 'Индивидуальный предприниматель (ИП)'
                  : company.legalForm === 'OOO'
                    ? 'Общество с ограниченной ответственностью (ООО)'
                    : company.legalForm ?? 'Не указана'}
              </Typography.Text>
            </div>
          )}
          <ProfileCompany />
          <ProfileContacts />
          <SessionActions />
        </div>
      </Panel>
    </>
  )
}

=== ./src/widgets/header-navigation/.gitkeep ===

=== ./src/widgets/bank-workspace/application-queue.test.cjs ===
const assert = require('node:assert/strict')
const { readFileSync } = require('node:fs')
const path = require('node:path')
const vm = require('node:vm')
const { test } = require('node:test')
const ts = require('typescript')

const filename = path.join(__dirname, 'model/queue.ts')
const compiled = ts.transpileModule(readFileSync(filename, 'utf8'), {
  compilerOptions: {
    module: ts.ModuleKind.CommonJS,
    target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2023,
  },
})
const exported = {}
vm.runInNewContext(compiled.outputText, { exports: exported }, { filename })
const { getVisibleApplications } = exported

const applications = [
  { id: 1, companyName: 'ООО Берег', inn: '7700000001', amount: 2000000,
    status: 'IN_REVIEW', createdAt: '2026-10-20T12:00:00Z',
    recommendation: { decision: 'DECLINED' } },
  { id: 2, companyName: 'ИП Анна', inn: '7700000002', amount: 500000,
    status: 'OFFER_READY', createdAt: '2026-10-23T12:00:00Z' },
  { id: 3, companyName: 'ООО Альфа', inn: '7700000003', amount: 10000000,
    status: 'DECLINED', createdAt: '2026-10-22T12:00:00Z' },
  { id: 4, companyName: 'ООО Вектор', inn: '7700000004', amount: 1500000,
    status: 'ACCEPTED', createdAt: '2026-10-21T12:00:00Z' },
  { id: 5, companyName: 'ИП Борис', inn: '7700000005', amount: 90000,
    status: 'IN_REVIEW', createdAt: '2026-10-24T12:00:00Z',
    recommendation: { decision: 'APPROVED' } },
]

function ids(search = '', status = 'ALL', sort = 'review', items = applications) {
  return Array.from(getVisibleApplications(items, search, status, sort), (item) => item.id)
}

test('review sorting puts the oldest undecided applications first, not scoring decisions', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(ids(), [1, 5, 2, 3, 4])
})

test('date, amount and Russian company sorting use the actual field values', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(ids('', 'ALL', 'newest'), [5, 2, 3, 4, 1])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('', 'ALL', 'oldest'), [1, 4, 3, 2, 5])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('', 'ALL', 'amount-desc'), [3, 1, 4, 2, 5])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('', 'ALL', 'amount-asc'), [5, 2, 4, 1, 3])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('', 'ALL', 'company'), [2, 5, 3, 1, 4])
})

test('each status filters independently of the scoring recommendation', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(ids('', 'IN_REVIEW'), [1, 5])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('', 'OFFER_READY'), [2])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('', 'ACCEPTED'), [4])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('', 'DECLINED'), [3])
})

test('search supports company words, case, repeated spaces, INN and application numbers', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(ids('  ООО   альфа  '), [3])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('7700000005'), [5])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('№ 2'), [2])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('#2'), [2])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('Анна 7700000002'), [2])
})

test('explicit application numbers do not match digits inside INNs or company names', () => {
  const items = [...applications, { ...applications[0], id: 22, companyName: 'Компания 2' }]
  assert.deepEqual(ids('№2', 'ALL', 'review', items), [2])
})

test('search and status compose, and whitespace-only input leaves the queue unchanged', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(ids('ООО', 'DECLINED'), [3])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('Анна', 'IN_REVIEW'), [])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('несуществующая компания'), [])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('   '), ids())
  assert.deepEqual(ids('', 'ALL', 'review', []), [])
})

test('date sorting accounts for timezone offsets and uses IDs for ties', () => {
  const items = [
    { ...applications[0], id: 10, createdAt: '2026-10-20T10:00:00+03:00' },
    { ...applications[0], id: 11, createdAt: '2026-10-20T08:00:00Z' },
    { ...applications[0], id: 12, createdAt: '2026-10-20T08:00:00Z' },
  ]
  assert.deepEqual(ids('', 'ALL', 'oldest', items), [10, 11, 12])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('', 'ALL', 'newest', items), [12, 11, 10])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('', 'ALL', 'review', items), [10, 11, 12])
  assert.deepEqual(ids('', 'ALL', 'amount-desc', items), [12, 11, 10])
})

test('filtering and sorting never mutate cached records or their original order', () => {
  const items = Object.freeze(applications.map((item) => Object.freeze({ ...item })))
  const originalIds = items.map((item) => item.id)
  for (const sort of ['review', 'newest', 'oldest', 'amount-desc', 'amount-asc', 'company']) {
    const result = getVisibleApplications(items, '', 'ALL', sort)
    assert.notEqual(result, items)
    assert.equal(result.length, items.length)
    assert.ok(result.every((item) => items.includes(item)))
  }
  assert.deepEqual(items.map((item) => item.id), originalIds)
})

import { Status } from '@alfalab/core-components/status'
import { CheckmarkCircleSIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/CheckmarkCircleSIcon'
import { ClockSIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/ClockSIcon'
import { CrossCircleSIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/CrossCircleSIcon'
import { DocumentLinesLineSIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/DocumentLinesLineSIcon'
import {
  applicationStatusLabels,
  type BankApplicationView,
} from '@/entities/loan-application'
import styles from '../status.module.scss'

/** The application stage, independent of the scoring recommendation. */
export interface ApplicationStatusProps {
  status: BankApplicationView['status']
}

const presentation = {
  IN_REVIEW: { color: 'orange', Icon: ClockSIcon },
  OFFER_READY: { color: 'blue', Icon: DocumentLinesLineSIcon },
  ACCEPTED: { color: 'green', Icon: CheckmarkCircleSIcon },
  DECLINED: { color: 'red', Icon: CrossCircleSIcon },
  SIGNATURE_PENDING: { color: 'blue', Icon: DocumentLinesLineSIcon },
} as const

/** Renders a stage with both a label and a visual indicator. */
export function ApplicationStatus({ status }: ApplicationStatusProps) {
  const { color, Icon } = presentation[status]

  return (
    <Status
      className={styles.badge}
      color={color}
      view="muted"
      size={24}
      shape="rounded"
      uppercase={false}
      leftAddons={<Icon className={styles.icon} aria-hidden="true" />}
    >
      {applicationStatusLabels[status]}
    </Status>
  )
}
