    padding: var(--gap-24) var(--gap-20);
  }

  .detailsButton {
    inline-size: 100%;
  }

  .amount {
    white-space: normal;
  }

  .parameters > div {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: var(--gap-8);
  }
}

=== ./src/widgets/application-overview/ui/ApplicationOverview/ApplicationOverview.tsx ===
import { useId, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { Button } from '@alfalab/core-components/button'
import { Steps } from '@alfalab/core-components/steps'
import { Typography } from '@alfalab/core-components/typography'
import { ArrowRightMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/ArrowRightMIcon'
import { ClockMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/ClockMIcon'
import { CheckmarkMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/CheckmarkMIcon'
import { useApplicationDocuments } from '@/entities/application-document'
import { useDashboard } from '@/entities/dashboard'
import {
  applicationDecisionLabels,
  applicationStatusLabels,
  businessScaleLabels,
  getApplicationProgress,
  getApplicationPurposeLabel,
  useApplications,
  type Application,
  type ApplicationView,
} from '@/entities/loan-application'
import { AcceptOffer } from '@/features/accept-offer'
import { SignDocuments } from '@/features/sign-documents'
import { ApplicationForm } from '@/features/submit-loan-application'
import { ROUTES } from '@/shared/config'
import { useApiScope } from '@/shared/api'
import { formatDate, formatMoney } from '@/shared/lib'
import { Panel, Skeleton } from '@/shared/ui'
import styles from './ApplicationOverview.module.scss'

const skeletonRows = [0, 1, 2, 3]

/** Selects the dashboard hero or displays a specific application in the list. */
export interface ApplicationOverviewProps {
  application?: ApplicationView
}

function ApplicationOverviewSkeleton() {
  const headingId = useId()

  return (
    <section
      className={styles.card}
      aria-labelledby={headingId}
      aria-busy="true"
    >
      <div className={styles.topline}>
        <Skeleton
          width="7.5rem"
          height="1.75rem"
          radius="var(--border-radius-pill)"
        />
        <Skeleton width="3.5rem" height="1rem" />
      </div>
      <div className={styles.heading}>
        <span id={headingId} className={styles.screenReaderOnly}>
          Обзор кредита и заявки
        </span>
        <Skeleton width="min(100%, 24rem)" height="2.25rem" />
        <Skeleton width="min(70%, 18rem)" height="1rem" />
      </div>
      <div className={styles.skeletonBody}>
        <div className={styles.skeletonSteps}>
          {skeletonRows.map((row) => (
            <div className={styles.skeletonStep} key={row}>
              <Skeleton width="1.5rem" height="1.5rem" radius="50%" />
              <Skeleton width="100%" height="0.75rem" />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className={styles.skeletonParameters}>
          {skeletonRows.map((row) => (
            <div className={styles.skeletonParameter} key={row}>
              <Skeleton width="55%" height="0.875rem" />
              <Skeleton width="30%" height="0.875rem" />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className={styles.bottomline}>
        <Skeleton width="min(60%, 18rem)" height="1rem" />
        <Skeleton
          width="8.5rem"
          height="3rem"
          radius="var(--border-radius-12)"
        />
      </div>
      <span className={styles.screenReaderOnly} role="status">
        Загружаем данные…
      </span>
    </section>
  )
}

/** Shows server-backed application progress and strictly related offer terms. */
export function ApplicationOverview({
  application: providedApplication,
}: ApplicationOverviewProps) {
  const [detailsOpen, setDetailsOpen] = useState(false)
  const [documentsOpen, setDocumentsOpen] = useState(false)
  const [selectedApplication, setSelectedApplication] =
    useState<Application | null>(null)
  const detailsButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const headingId = useId()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const dashboard = useDashboard()
  const applications = useApplications()
  const scope = useApiScope()
  const currentApplication =
    providedApplication ?? dashboard.data?.currentApplication
  const applicationId =
    (detailsOpen || documentsOpen) && selectedApplication
      ? selectedApplication.id
      : currentApplication?.id
  const applicationView =
    (providedApplication?.id === applicationId
      ? providedApplication
      : undefined) ??
    applications.data?.find((item) => item.id === applicationId)
  const application =
    applicationView ?? (detailsOpen || documentsOpen ? selectedApplication : currentApplication)
  const signingStage = application?.status === 'SIGNATURE_PENDING'
  const documentExperience = signingStage || application?.status === 'ACCEPTED'
  const documents = useApplicationDocuments(application?.id, documentExperience)
  const documentsSigned = Boolean(documents.data?.signedAt && documents.data.signedBy)
  // Company-wide dashboard offers do not identify their application.
  const offer = applicationView?.offer
  const scoring = applicationView?.scoring
  const isOoo = applicationView?.legalForm === 'OOO'
  const today = new Date()
  today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
  const urgentUntil = new Date(today)
  urgentUntil.setDate(urgentUntil.getDate() + 7)
  const loan = dashboard.data?.activeLoan
  const payment = dashboard.data?.nextPayment
  // LocalDate fields must be compared as local calendar dates, not UTC instants.
  const paymentIsUrgent =
    payment != null &&
    new Date(`${payment.dueDate.slice(0, 10)}T00:00:00`).getTime() <=
      urgentUntil.getTime()
  const hasPendingApplication =
    application?.status === 'IN_REVIEW' || application?.status === 'OFFER_READY' || signingStage || documentsSigned
  const showLoan =
    !providedApplication &&
    !detailsOpen &&
    !documentsOpen &&
    loan != null &&
    (paymentIsUrgent || !hasPendingApplication)
  const offerIsValid =
    offer?.status === 'ACTIVE' &&
    new Date(`${offer.validUntil.slice(0, 10)}T00:00:00`).getTime() >=
      today.getTime()
  const canAcceptOffer =
    application?.status === 'OFFER_READY' &&
    applicationView?.status === application.status &&
    offerIsValid &&
    (providedApplication != null || !applications.isError)

  if (!providedApplication && !detailsOpen && !documentsOpen) {
    if (dashboard.isError) {
      return (
        <section className={styles.card} aria-labelledby={headingId}>
          <div className={styles.heading}>
            <Typography.TitleResponsive
              id={headingId}
              tag="h2"
              view="large"
              font="system"
              weight="bold"
            >
              Обзор кредита и заявки
            </Typography.TitleResponsive>
          </div>
          <div className={styles.progress} role="alert">
            <Typography.Text tag="p" color="secondary">
              {dashboard.error.message}
            </Typography.Text>
          </div>
          <div className={styles.bottomline}>
            <Button
              view="secondary"
              size={48}
              onClick={() => {
                void dashboard.refetch()
              }}
            >
              Повторить загрузку
            </Button>
          </div>
        </section>
      )
    }

    if (dashboard.isPending || !dashboard.data) {
      return <ApplicationOverviewSkeleton />
    }
  }

  if (showLoan && loan) {
    return (
      <>
        <section className={styles.card} aria-labelledby={headingId}>
          <div className={styles.topline}>
            <Typography.Text view="primary-small" weight="medium">
              Действующий кредит
            </Typography.Text>
            <Typography.Text view="secondary-large" color="secondary">
              № {loan.id}
            </Typography.Text>
          </div>
          <div className={styles.heading}>
            <Typography.TitleResponsive
              id={headingId}
              tag="h2"
              view="large"
              font="system"
              weight="bold"
            >
              Остаток долга{' '}
              <span className={styles.amount}>
                {formatMoney(loan.remainingBalance)}
              </span>
            </Typography.TitleResponsive>
            <Typography.Text tag="p" color="secondary">
              Ставка {loan.ratePercent}% годовых · Срок: {loan.termMonths} мес.
            </Typography.Text>
          </div>
          <div className={styles.progress}>
            <dl className={styles.parameters}>
              <div>
                <dt>Сумма кредита</dt>
                <dd>{formatMoney(loan.principal)}</dd>
              </div>
              <div>
                <dt>Ежемесячный платёж</dt>
                <dd>{formatMoney(loan.monthlyPayment)}</dd>
              </div>
              {payment && (
                <>
                  <div>
                    <dt>Ближайший платёж</dt>
                    <dd>{formatMoney(payment.amount)}</dd>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <dt>Дата платежа</dt>
                    <dd>{formatDate(payment.dueDate)}</dd>
                  </div>
                </>
              )}
            </dl>
            {!payment && (
              <Typography.Text tag="p" color="secondary">
                Ближайший платёж не указан.
              </Typography.Text>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className={styles.bottomline}>
            <Typography.Text view="primary-small" color="secondary">
              {paymentIsUrgent
                ? payment && new Date(`${payment.dueDate}T00:00:00`) < today
                  ? 'Дата платежа прошла. Проверьте исполнение обязательства.'
                  : 'Платёж по кредиту в ближайшие 7 дней. Проверьте готовность к оплате.'
                : 'Сведения о действующем кредите.'}
            </Typography.Text>
            <Button
              view="primary"
              size={48}
              className={styles.detailsButton}
              rightAddons={<ArrowRightMIcon aria-hidden="true" />}
              onClick={() => {
                void navigate(ROUTES.credits)
              }}
              ref={detailsButtonRef}
            >
              К кредитам
            </Button>
          </div>
        </section>
        <ApplicationForm resumeOnly />
      </>
    )
  }

  if (!application) {
    return <ApplicationForm headingTag="h2" />
  }

  const awaitingClientChoice = application.status === 'OFFER_READY'
  const officerComment = applicationView?.officerComment?.trim()
  const officerCommentNotice = officerComment && (
    <div className={`${styles.notice} ${styles.officerComment}`}>
      <p>
        <strong>Комментарий кредитного специалиста</strong>
      </p>
      <p>{officerComment}</p>
    </div>
  )
  const stages = getApplicationProgress(application.status)
  const currentStepIndex = stages.findIndex((stage) => stage.state === 'CURRENT')
  const activeStep = currentStepIndex < 0 ? stages.length + 1 : currentStepIndex + 1
  const displayedStep = Math.min(activeStep, stages.length)
  const declined = application.status === 'DECLINED'
  const checkIsStepError = (step: number) =>
    declined && stages[step - 1]?.code === 'DECISION'
  const steps = stages.map((step) => (
    <Typography.Text
      key={step.code}
      view="primary-small"
      weight={step.state === 'CURRENT' ? 'medium' : 'regular'}
      color={step.state === 'PENDING' ? 'secondary' : 'primary'}
      aria-current={step.state === 'CURRENT' ? 'step' : undefined}
    >
      {awaitingClientChoice && step.code === 'DECISION' ? (
        <span className={styles.pendingChoice}>
          {step.title}
          <span className={styles.choiceHint}>Ожидает выбора</span>
        </span>
      ) : (
        step.title
      )}
    </Typography.Text>
  ))
  // The kit keeps step buttons focusable in read-only mode; expose a semantic list instead.
  const progressDescription = (
    <ol className={styles.screenReaderOnly} aria-label="Ход заявки">
      {stages.map((step) => (
        <li
          key={step.code}
          aria-current={step.state === 'CURRENT' ? 'step' : undefined}
        >
          {step.title}:{' '}
          {declined && step.state === 'CURRENT'
            ? 'отказ по заявке'
            : declined && step.state === 'PENDING'
              ? 'не выполняется: заявка отклонена'
              : step.state === 'DONE'
                ? 'завершено'
                : step.state === 'CURRENT'
                  ? awaitingClientChoice
                    ? 'ожидает вашего выбора'
                    : 'текущий этап'
                  : 'предстоит'}
        </li>
      ))}
    </ol>
  )
  const statusMessage = {
    IN_REVIEW: {
      title: 'Заявка на рассмотрении у кредитного специалиста',
      description:
        'Кредитный специалист рассматривает заявку. Действий от вас сейчас не требуется. Решение появится здесь после рассмотрения.',
    },
    OFFER_READY: {
      title:
        application.decision === 'ALTERNATIVE_OFFER' ||
        (offer &&
          (offer.amount !== application.amount ||
            offer.termDays !== application.termDays))
          ? 'Банк предложил другие условия'
          : 'Банк подготовил предложение',
      description: canAcceptOffer
        ? 'Ожидаем вашего выбора. Рассмотрите условия предложения перед подписанием.'
        : 'Принятие предложения недоступно. Проверьте его условия и статус.',
    },
    DECLINED: {
      title: 'В заявке отказано',
      description: officerComment
        ? 'Причина отказа указана в комментарии кредитного специалиста.'
        : !providedApplication && applications.isError
          ? applications.error.message
          : !providedApplication && applications.isPending
            ? 'Загружаем комментарий кредитного специалиста…'
            : 'Комментарий кредитного специалиста пока не указан.',
    },
    ACCEPTED: {
      title: documentsSigned ? 'Документы подписаны' : 'Условия приняты',
      description: 'Оформление завершено.',
    },
    SIGNATURE_PENDING: {
      title: 'Осталась ваша подпись',
      description:
        'Договор и индивидуальные условия. Ознакомьтесь и подпишите оба документа одним действием.',
    },
  }[application.status]
  const offerState =
    !providedApplication && applications.isError
      ? applications.error.message
      : !providedApplication && applications.isPending
        ? 'Загружаем условия предложения…'
        : !offer
          ? 'Условия предложения пока недоступны'
          : offer.status === 'EXPIRED' ||
              (offer.status === 'ACTIVE' && !offerIsValid)
            ? 'Срок действия предложения истёк. Подписание недоступно.'
            : offer.status === 'ACCEPTED'
              ? 'Предложение принято. Подписание документов проходит на отдельном этапе.'
              : !canAcceptOffer
                ? 'Статус заявки обновляется. Подписание пока недоступно.'
                : null

  const offerExperience =
    awaitingClientChoice ||
    application.status === 'ACCEPTED' ||
    application.status === 'SIGNATURE_PENDING' ||
    (detailsOpen &&
      (selectedApplication?.status === 'OFFER_READY' ||
        selectedApplication?.status === 'ACCEPTED' ||
        selectedApplication?.status === 'SIGNATURE_PENDING'))
  const applicationDetails = (
    <div className={styles.details}>
      <Typography.TitleResponsive tag="h2" view="medium" font="system">
        Запрошено: {formatMoney(application.amount)}
      </Typography.TitleResponsive>
      {!offerExperience && officerCommentNotice}
      <dl className={styles.parameters}>
        <div>
          <dt>Дата создания заявки</dt>
          <dd>
            <time dateTime={application.createdAt}>
              {formatDate(application.createdAt)}
            </time>
          </dd>
        </div>
        <div>
          <dt>Цель</dt>
          <dd>{getApplicationPurposeLabel(application.purpose)}</dd>
        </div>
        <div>
          <dt>Запрошенный срок</dt>
          <dd>{application.termDays} дней</dd>
        </div>
        <div>
          <dt>Статус заявки</dt>
          <dd>{applicationStatusLabels[application.status]}</dd>
        </div>
        <div>
          <dt>Решение банка</dt>
          <dd>
            {application.decision
              ? applicationDecisionLabels[application.decision]
              : 'Не указано'}
          </dd>
        </div>
        {applicationView && (
          <>
            <div>
              <dt>Скоринговый балл</dt>
              <dd>
                {applicationView.internalScore == null
                  ? 'Нет оценки'
                  : `${applicationView.internalScore} / 100`}
              </dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt>Класс риска</dt>
              <dd>{applicationView.riskClass ?? 'Нет оценки'}</dd>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </dl>
      {isOoo && (
        <div className={styles.notice}>
          <dl className={styles.parameters}>
            <div>
              <dt>Масштаб бизнеса по расчету банка</dt>
              <dd>
                {scoring?.businessScale
                  ? businessScaleLabels[scoring.businessScale]
                  : 'Банк пока не передал расчет'}
              </dd>
            </div>
          </dl>
          <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-small" color="secondary">
            Масштаб не является классом риска. Итоговые условия определяет банк в
            предложении.
          </Typography.Text>
        </div>
      )}
      {scoring && (
        <>
          <Typography.TitleResponsive tag="h3" view="small" font="system">
            Показатели скоринга
          </Typography.TitleResponsive>
          <dl className={styles.parameters}>
            <div>
              <dt>Долговая нагрузка (DTI)</dt>
              <dd>{scoring.dtiPercent}%</dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt>Сумма / годовая выручка</dt>
              <dd>{scoring.amountToRevenue}</dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt>Платёж скоринга по базовой ставке</dt>
              <dd>
                {scoring.newMonthlyPayment == null
                  ? 'Нет расчёта'
                  : formatMoney(scoring.newMonthlyPayment)}
              </dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt>Расчётный лимит</dt>
              <dd>
                {scoring.calculatedLimit == null
                  ? 'Нет расчёта'
                  : formatMoney(scoring.calculatedLimit)}
              </dd>
            </div>
          </dl>
          <Typography.Text tag="p" color="secondary">
            Скоринг рассчитан сервером для исходной заявки по базовой ставке,
            а не по ставке предложения. Условия предложения указаны отдельно.
          </Typography.Text>
        </>
      )}
      {progressDescription}
      <div inert aria-hidden="true">
        <Steps
          activeStep={activeStep}
          isMarkCompletedSteps={false}
          checkIsStepPositive={(step) => stages[step - 1]?.state === 'DONE'}
          checkIsStepError={checkIsStepError}
          ordered
          interactive={false}
          isVerticalAlign
        >
          {steps}
        </Steps>
      </div>
      <div className={styles.notice}>
        <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-small">
          {statusMessage.description}
        </Typography.Text>
      </div>
      {application.status === 'DECLINED' &&
        !providedApplication &&
        applications.isError && (
          <Button
            view="secondary"
            onClick={() => {
              void applications.refetch()
            }}
          >
            Повторить загрузку комментария
          </Button>
        )}
    </div>
  )

  return (
    <>
      <section
        className={`${styles.card} ${awaitingClientChoice ? styles.offerCard : ''} ${documentExperience ? styles.signingCard : ''}`}
        aria-labelledby={headingId}
      >
        <div className={styles.topline}>
          <div className={styles.statusLine}>
            <span
              className={`${styles.status}${
                declined
                  ? ` ${styles.declined}`
                  : documentsSigned
                    ? ` ${styles.signedStatus}`
                  : awaitingClientChoice
                    ? ` ${styles.warning}`
                    : ''
              }`}
            >
              {application.status === 'IN_REVIEW' && (
                <ClockMIcon aria-hidden="true" />
              )}
              {documentsSigned && <CheckmarkMIcon aria-hidden="true" />}
              <Typography.Text view="primary-small" weight="medium">
                {documentsSigned ? 'Документы подписаны' : awaitingClientChoice
                  ? 'Предложение готово'
                  : applicationStatusLabels[application.status]}
              </Typography.Text>
            </span>
            {awaitingClientChoice && (
              <Typography.Text view="primary-small" color="secondary">
                Ждёт вашего решения
              </Typography.Text>
            )}
          </div>
          <Typography.Text view="secondary-large" color="secondary">
            № {application.id}
          </Typography.Text>
        </div>
        <div className={documentExperience ? styles.signingMain : awaitingClientChoice ? styles.offerMain : undefined}>
          <div className={styles.heading}>
            <Typography.TitleResponsive
              id={headingId}
              tag="h2"
              view="large"
              font="system"
              weight="bold"
            >
              {documentExperience ? (
                signingStage ? 'Два документа до следующего шага' : documentsSigned ? 'Документы подписаны' : 'Ваши документы по заявке'
              ) : awaitingClientChoice ? (
                'Ваш следующий шаг для бизнеса'
              ) : (
                <>
                  Заявка на{' '}
                  <span className={styles.amount}>
                    {formatMoney(application.amount)}
                  </span>
                </>
              )}
            </Typography.TitleResponsive>
            {documentExperience ? (
              <>
                <Typography.Text tag="p" color="secondary">
                  {documentsSigned
                    ? 'Оформление завершено. Договор и индивидуальные условия доступны в разделе «Документы».'
                    : signingStage
                      ? 'Предложение принято. Проверьте документы и поставьте подпись, не покидая кабинет.'
                      : 'Просмотрите сохранённые документы и данные подписания.'}
                </Typography.Text>
                {offer && (
                  <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-small">
                    {formatMoney(offer.amount, { exact: true })} · {offer.termDays} дней · {String(offer.finalRate).replace('.', ',')}% годовых
                  </Typography.Text>
                )}
              </>
            ) : awaitingClientChoice ? (
              <Typography.Text tag="p" color="secondary">
                Цель: {getApplicationPurposeLabel(application.purpose)}
              </Typography.Text>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Typography.Text tag="p" color="secondary">
                  Дата создания заявки:{' '}
                  <time dateTime={application.createdAt}>
                    {formatDate(application.createdAt)}
                  </time>
                </Typography.Text>
                <Typography.Text tag="p" color="secondary">
                  Запрошенный срок: {application.termDays} дней
                  {application.purpose &&
                    ` · ${getApplicationPurposeLabel(application.purpose)}`}
                </Typography.Text>
                {isOoo && (
                  <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-small" color="secondary">
                    Масштаб бизнеса:{' '}
                    {scoring?.businessScale
                      ? businessScaleLabels[scoring.businessScale]
                      : 'Ожидает расчета банка'}
                  </Typography.Text>
                )}
                {officerCommentNotice}
              </>
            )}
          </div>

          {documentExperience && (
            <div className={styles.documentScene} aria-hidden="true">
              <div className={styles.backSheet}>
                <span>02 / ИНДИВИДУАЛЬНЫЕ УСЛОВИЯ</span>
                <i /><i /><i />
              </div>
              <div className={styles.frontSheet}>
                <span>01 / ДОГОВОР</span>
                <strong>Овердрафт</strong>
                <i /><i /><i />
                <div className={documentsSigned ? styles.sheetSigned : styles.sheetSignature}>
                  {documentsSigned && <CheckmarkMIcon />}
                  {documentsSigned ? 'ПОДПИСАНО' : ''}
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className={styles.documentCount}>{documentsSigned ? '2 / 2 подписано' : '02 документа · 01 подпись'}</span>
            </div>
          )}
          {awaitingClientChoice && (
            <div className={styles.offerSummary}>
              {offer && (
                <dl className={styles.offerAmount}>
                  <dt>Банк предлагает</dt>
                  <dd>{formatMoney(offer.amount, { exact: true })}</dd>
                </dl>
              )}
              {!offer && !providedApplication && applications.isPending && (
                <div className={styles.offerSkeleton} aria-hidden="true" />
              )}
              {offer && (
                <Typography.Text tag="p" color="secondary">
                  {offer.amount !== application.amount &&
                  offer.termDays !== application.termDays
                    ? 'Сумма и срок отличаются от заявки. Сравнение доступно в предложении.'
                    : offer.amount !== application.amount
                      ? 'Сумма отличается от заявки. Сравнение доступно в предложении.'
                      : offer.termDays !== application.termDays
                        ? 'Срок отличается от заявки. Сравнение доступно в предложении.'
                        : application.decision === 'ALTERNATIVE_OFFER'
                          ? 'Банк предложил другие условия. Сумма и срок сохранены.'
                          : 'Сумма и срок соответствуют заявке'}
                </Typography.Text>
              )}
              {offerState && (
                <Typography.Text
                  tag="p"
                  view="primary-small"
                  color="secondary"
                  role={
                    !providedApplication && applications.isError
                      ? 'alert'
                      : 'status'
                  }
                >
                  {offerState}
                </Typography.Text>
              )}
              <dl className={styles.offerDates}>
                <div>
                  <dt>Заявка создана</dt>
                  <dd>
                    <time dateTime={application.createdAt}>
                      {formatDate(application.createdAt)}
                    </time>
                  </dd>
                </div>
                {offer && (
                  <div>
                    <dt>Предложение действует до</dt>
                    <dd>
                      <time dateTime={offer.validUntil}>
                        {formatDate(offer.validUntil)}
                      </time>
                    </dd>
                  </div>
                )}
              </dl>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        <div
          className={`${styles.progress} ${awaitingClientChoice ? styles.offerProgress : ''}`}
          aria-label="Этапы заявки"
        >
          {progressDescription}
          <div className={styles.desktopSteps} inert aria-hidden="true">
            <Steps
              activeStep={activeStep}
              isMarkCompletedSteps={false}
              checkIsStepPositive={(step) => stages[step - 1]?.state === 'DONE'}
              checkIsStepError={checkIsStepError}
              ordered
              interactive={false}
              className={styles.steps}
              minSpaceBetweenSteps={16}
            >
              {steps}
            </Steps>
          </div>
          <div className={styles.mobileSteps} inert aria-hidden="true">
            <Typography.Text view="secondary-medium" color="secondary">
              <span
                className={
                  awaitingClientChoice ? styles.pendingChoice : undefined
                }
              >
                {`Этап ${displayedStep} из ${stages.length} · ${stages[displayedStep - 1].title}${application.status === 'ACCEPTED' ? ' · Завершено' : declined ? ' · Отказ' : awaitingClientChoice ? ' · Ожидает выбора' : ''}`}
              </span>
            </Typography.Text>
            <Steps
              activeStep={activeStep}
              isMarkCompletedSteps={false}
              checkIsStepPositive={(step) => stages[step - 1]?.state === 'DONE'}
              checkIsStepError={checkIsStepError}
              ordered
              interactive={false}
              className={styles.steps}
              minSpaceBetweenSteps={16}
            >
              {stages.map((step) => (
                <span key={step.code} />
              ))}
            </Steps>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className={styles.bottomline}>
          {awaitingClientChoice ? (
            <Typography.Text view="primary-small" color="secondary">
              Рассмотрите условия и решите без спешки.
            </Typography.Text>
          ) : (
            <div className={styles.nextStep}>
              <div className={styles.nextStepText}>
                <Typography.Text view="primary-small" weight="medium">
                  {statusMessage.title}
                </Typography.Text>
                <Typography.Text view="primary-small" color="secondary">
                  {statusMessage.description}
                </Typography.Text>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
          <Button
            view="primary"
            size={48}
            className={styles.detailsButton}
            rightAddons={<ArrowRightMIcon aria-hidden="true" />}
            onClick={() => {
              setSelectedApplication(application)
              if (documentExperience) setDocumentsOpen(true)
              else setDetailsOpen(true)
            }}
            ref={detailsButtonRef}
          >
            {documentExperience
              ? signingStage ? 'Подписать документы' : 'Посмотреть документы'
              : awaitingClientChoice
              ? 'Посмотреть предложение'
              : 'Подробнее о заявке'}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </section>
      {!providedApplication && <ApplicationForm resumeOnly />}

      {offerExperience ? (
        <AcceptOffer
          key={`${scope}-${application.id}`}
          open={detailsOpen}
          application={application}
          offer={offer}
          officerComment={officerComment}
          returnFocusRef={detailsButtonRef}
          onClose={() => setDetailsOpen(false)}
          onViewDocuments={() => {
            setDetailsOpen(false)
            setSelectedApplication(application)
            setDocumentsOpen(true)
          }}
        >
          {applicationDetails}
        </AcceptOffer>
      ) : (
        <Panel
          open={detailsOpen}
          returnFocusRef={detailsButtonRef}
          title={`Заявка № ${application.id}`}
          onClose={() => setDetailsOpen(false)}
          footer={
            <Button
              view="secondary"
              block
              onClick={() => {
                setDetailsOpen(false)
                void navigate(ROUTES.documents)
              }}
            >
              Документы по заявке
            </Button>
          }
        >
          {applicationDetails}
        </Panel>
      )}
      <SignDocuments
        key={`documents-${scope}-${application.id}`}
        application={application}
        offer={offer}
        open={documentsOpen}
        onClose={() => setDocumentsOpen(false)}
        returnFocusRef={detailsButtonRef}
      />
    </>
  )
}

=== ./src/widgets/ai-copilot-drawer/AiCopilotDrawer/index.ts ===
export { AiCopilotDrawer } from './AiCopilotDrawer'
export type { AiCopilotDrawerProps } from './AiCopilotDrawer'

=== ./src/widgets/ai-copilot-drawer/AiCopilotDrawer/AiCopilotDrawer.tsx ===
import { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom'
import { Button } from '@alfalab/core-components/button'
import { IconButton } from '@alfalab/core-components/icon-button'
import { useMatchMedia } from '@alfalab/core-components/mq'
import { SidePanel } from '@alfalab/core-components/side-panel'
import { Textarea } from '@alfalab/core-components/textarea'
import { Typography } from '@alfalab/core-components/typography'
import { stackingOrder } from '@alfalab/core-components-stack-context'
import { ArrowUpMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/ArrowUpMIcon'
import { MagicWandMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/MagicWandMIcon'
import { CrossMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/CrossMIcon'

import {
  maxAssistantQuestionLength,
  useAssistantChat,
  useAssistantScreen,
} from '@/features/send-ai-message'
import { formatMoney } from '@/shared/lib'

import styles from './AiCopilotDrawer.module.scss'

/** The assistant reads fresh screen context and server-owned cabinet data. */
export interface AiCopilotDrawerProps {
  companyName: string
  application: {
    amount: number
    purpose: string
    statusLabel: string
  } | null
}

export function AiCopilotDrawer({ companyName, application }: AiCopilotDrawerProps) {
  const screen = useAssistantScreen()
  const chat = useAssistantChat()
  const [desktop] = useMatchMedia('(min-width: 768px)')
  const [panel, setPanel] = useState({ dialog: screen.dialog, open: false })
  const [question, setQuestion] = useState('')
  const launcherRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null)
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const returnFocusRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null)
  const id = useId()
  const titleId = `${id}-title`
  const panelId = `${id}-panel`
  const hintId = `${id}-hint`

  // A newly opened business dialog must never be covered by an already open chat.
  if (panel.dialog !== screen.dialog) {
    setPanel({ dialog: screen.dialog, open: false })
  }
  const open = panel.open && panel.dialog === screen.dialog
  const context = screen.context
  const popup = desktop && Boolean(screen.dialog && context.step)
  const focusedField = context.fields.find((field) => field.id === context.focusedFieldId)
  const relevantField = focusedField ?? context.fields.find((field) => field.invalid) ??
    context.fields.find((field) => /оквэд/iu.test(field.label))
  const prompts = context.fields.length > 0
    ? [
        relevantField ? `Где взять «${relevantField.label}»?` : 'Что заполнить на этом шаге?',
        context.fields.some((field) => field.invalid) ? 'Как исправить ошибки на этом шаге?' : 'Что важно проверить перед продолжением?',
      ]
    : ['Что мне делать дальше?', 'Объясни условия на экране', 'Чем ты можешь помочь?']
  const lastTurn = chat.turns.at(-1)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return
    const frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => inputRef.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true }))
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame)
  }, [open, popup])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open || chat.turns.length === 0) return
    const element = scrollRef.current
    element?.scrollTo({ top: element.scrollHeight })
  }, [open, chat.turns, chat.pending, chat.error])

  function restoreFocus() {
    const target = returnFocusRef.current ?? launcherRef.current
    if (target?.isConnected) target.focus({ preventScroll: true })
    returnFocusRef.current = null
  }

  function close(target?: HTMLElement) {
    returnFocusRef.current = target ?? launcherRef.current
    setPanel({ dialog: screen.dialog, open: false })
    if (popup) requestAnimationFrame(restoreFocus)
  }

  function ask(value: string) {
    if (!value.trim() || chat.pending) return
    void chat.send(value, screen.capture())
    setQuestion('')
  }

  function submit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault()
    ask(question)
  }

  function showField(fieldId: string) {
    const target = document.getElementById(fieldId)
    if (!target || !screen.capture().fields.some((field) => field.id === fieldId)) return
    target.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center', behavior: 'instant' })
    close(target)
  }

  const header = (
    <div className={styles.header}>
      <div className={styles.heading}>
        <Typography.TitleResponsive id={titleId} tag="h2" view="xsmall" font="system">
          Помощник
        </Typography.TitleResponsive>
        <Typography.Text tag="p" view="secondary-small" color="secondary">
          {context.step}
        </Typography.Text>
      </div>
      <IconButton
        icon={CrossMIcon}
        size={32}
        view="secondary"
        aria-label="Свернуть помощника"
        onClick={() => close()}
      />
    </div>
  )

  const body = (
    <div className={styles.body} ref={scrollRef}>
      {!popup && chat.turns.length === 0 && (
        <section className={styles.welcome} aria-label="О помощнике">
          <Typography.TitleResponsive tag="h3" view="small" font="system">
            Заявка под рукой
          </Typography.TitleResponsive>
          <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-medium" color="secondary">
            Помогу сориентироваться в данных {companyName}.
          </Typography.Text>
        </section>
      )}

      {!popup && application && chat.turns.length === 0 && (
        <section className={styles.contextCard} aria-label="Данные заявки">
          <Typography.Text tag="span" view="secondary-medium" color="secondary">Ваша заявка</Typography.Text>
          <Typography.TitleResponsive tag="h3" view="small" font="system">{formatMoney(application.amount)}</Typography.TitleResponsive>
          <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-small">{application.purpose}</Typography.Text>
          <Typography.Text tag="p" view="secondary-medium" color="secondary">{application.statusLabel}</Typography.Text>
        </section>
      )}

      {!popup && <div className={styles.contextLine} aria-label="Контекст помощника">
        <Typography.Text tag="p" view="secondary-medium" color="secondary">
          {context.step ?? `На экране: ${context.title}`}
        </Typography.Text>
        {focusedField && (
          <Typography.Text tag="p" view="secondary-medium" color="secondary">Поле: {focusedField.label}</Typography.Text>
        )}
      </div>}
      {popup && chat.turns.length === 0 && (
        <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-small">
          Задайте вопрос по заполнению. Подскажу, где взять данные и как исправить ошибки.
        </Typography.Text>
      )}

      <div className={styles.dialogue} role="log" aria-label="Диалог с помощником" aria-live="polite" aria-relevant="additions text">
        {chat.turns.map((turn) => (
          <div className={styles.turn} key={turn.id}>
            <div className={styles.userMessage}>
              <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-small">{turn.question}</Typography.Text>
            </div>
            {turn.answer && (
              <div className={styles.assistantMessage}>
                <Typography.Text tag="span" view="secondary-small" color="secondary">
                  {turn.contextTitle}
                </Typography.Text>
                <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-small" className={styles.answer}>
                  {turn.answer.answer}
                </Typography.Text>
                {turn.answer.focusFieldId && turn.contextTitle === (context.step ?? context.title) &&
                  context.fields.some((field) => field.id === turn.answer?.focusFieldId) && (
                    <Button type="button" view="outlined" size={32} onClick={() => showField(turn.answer!.focusFieldId!)}>
                      Показать поле
                    </Button>
                  )}
              </div>
            )}
            {turn.error && turn.id !== lastTurn?.id && (
              <Typography.Text tag="p" view="secondary-medium" color="secondary">Ответ не получен. Задайте вопрос повторно.</Typography.Text>
            )}
          </div>
        ))}
        {chat.pending && (
          <Typography.Text className={styles.pending} tag="p" view="primary-small" role="status">
            Готовим ответ...
          </Typography.Text>
        )}
      </div>

      {chat.error && !chat.pending && (
        <div className={styles.error} role="alert">
          <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-small">
            {chat.error.message || 'Не удалось получить ответ помощника.'}
          </Typography.Text>
          {lastTurn && !lastTurn.answer && (
            <Button type="button" view="outlined" size={32} onClick={() => void chat.send(lastTurn.question, screen.capture(), lastTurn)}>
              Повторить запрос
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {!chat.pending && (
        <div className={styles.prompts} role="group" aria-label="Вопросы по текущему экрану">
          {prompts.map((prompt) => (
            <Button key={prompt} className={styles.prompt} type="button" view="outlined" size={32} onClick={() => ask(prompt)}>
              {prompt}
            </Button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )

  const composer = (
    <div className={styles.composer}>
      {chat.turns.length > 0 && (
        <Button className={styles.newChat} type="button" view="transparent" size={32} disabled={chat.pending} onClick={() => { chat.clear(); setQuestion('') }}>
          Новый диалог
        </Button>
      )}
      <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={submit}>
        <Textarea
          ref={inputRef}
          className={styles.input}
          block={true}
          size={48}
          autosize={true}
          minRows={1}
          maxRows={4}
          aria-label="Вопрос помощнику"
          placeholder="Ваш вопрос"
          value={question}
          maxLength={maxAssistantQuestionLength}
          autoComplete="off"
          aria-describedby={hintId}
          onChange={(_, { value }) => setQuestion(value)}
          onKeyDown={(event) => {
            if (event.key === 'Enter' && !event.shiftKey && !event.nativeEvent.isComposing) {
              event.preventDefault()
              ask(question)
            }
          }}
        />
        <Button
          className={styles.sendButton}
          type="submit"
          view="primary"
          size={48}
          loading={chat.pending}
          disabled={!question.trim() || chat.pending}
          aria-label="Отправить вопрос"
          leftAddons={<ArrowUpMIcon aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" />}
        />
      </form>
      <Typography.Text id={hintId} className={styles.hint} tag="p" view="secondary-small" color="secondary">
        Ответы ИИ стоит проверять. Значения личных полей не передаются.
      </Typography.Text>
    </div>
  )

  const wrapperProps = {
    id: panelId,
    'aria-labelledby': titleId,
    'data-ai-assistant': true,
  }

  return (
    <>
      {createPortal(
        <div className={styles.floatingRoot} data-ai-assistant>
          {popup && open && (
            <section
              className={styles.popup}
              id={panelId}
              role="dialog"
              aria-modal="false"
              aria-labelledby={titleId}
              onKeyDown={(event) => {
                if (event.key === 'Escape') {
                  event.stopPropagation()
                  close()
                }
              }}
            >
              {header}
              {body}
              {composer}
            </section>
          )}
          <Button
            ref={launcherRef}
            className={`${styles.launcher} ${screen.dialog ? styles.compactLauncher : ''}`}
            labelClassName={styles.launcherLabel}
            type="button"
            view="primary"
            size={56}
            leftAddons={<MagicWandMIcon aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" />}
            aria-haspopup="dialog"
            aria-label={open ? 'Свернуть помощника' : 'Помощник'}
            aria-expanded={open}
            aria-controls={open ? panelId : undefined}
            onClick={() => open ? close() : setPanel({ dialog: screen.dialog, open: true })}
          >
            Помощник
          </Button>
        </div>,
        screen.dialog ?? document.body,
      )}
      {!popup && (
        <SidePanel
          open={open}
          className={styles.nativePanel}
          onClose={() => close()}
          size="s"
          breakpoint={768}
          zIndex={stackingOrder.TOAST}
          scrollLock={true}
          disableRestoreFocus={true}
          onUnmount={restoreFocus}
          wrapperProps={wrapperProps}
        >
          <SidePanel.Header
            title={<Typography.TitleResponsive id={titleId} tag="h2" view="xsmall" font="system">Помощник</Typography.TitleResponsive>}
            hasCloser={true}
            closerProps={{ 'aria-label': 'Закрыть помощника', icon: () => <CrossMIcon aria-hidden="true" /> }}
            sticky={true}
            trim={false}
          />
          <SidePanel.Content className={styles.panelContent}>{body}</SidePanel.Content>
          <SidePanel.Footer sticky={true} layout="column">{composer}</SidePanel.Footer>
        </SidePanel>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

=== ./src/widgets/credit-summary/ui/CreditSummary/CreditSummary.tsx ===
import { useId } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { Button } from '@alfalab/core-components/button'
import { Typography } from '@alfalab/core-components/typography'
import { ArrowRightMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/ArrowRightMIcon'
import { WalletMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/WalletMIcon'
import { useDashboard } from '@/entities/dashboard'
import { ROUTES } from '@/shared/config'
import { formatDate, formatMoney } from '@/shared/lib'
import { Skeleton } from '@/shared/ui'
import styles from './CreditSummary.module.scss'

const skeletonRows = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4]

function CreditSummarySkeleton() {
  const headingId = useId()

  return (
    <section
      className={styles.card}
      aria-labelledby={headingId}
      aria-busy="true"
    >
      <span id={headingId} className={styles.screenReaderOnly}>
        Кредиты и лимиты
      </span>
      <Skeleton width="10rem" height="1.5rem" />
      <div className={styles.body}>
        <Skeleton
          width="var(--gap-48)"
          height="var(--gap-48)"
          radius="var(--border-radius-16)"
        />
        <Skeleton width="min(85%, 18rem)" height="1.25rem" />
        <div className={styles.skeletonParameters}>
          {skeletonRows.map((row) => (
            <div className={styles.skeletonParameter} key={row}>
              <Skeleton width="42%" height="0.875rem" />
              <Skeleton width="25%" height="0.875rem" />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <Skeleton
          width="7rem"
          height="2.5rem"
          radius="var(--border-radius-12)"
        />
      </div>
      <span className={styles.screenReaderOnly} role="status">
        Загружаем сведения о кредите…
      </span>
    </section>
  )
}

/** Summarizes the active loan and next payment returned by the dashboard API. */
export function CreditSummary() {
  const headingId = useId()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const dashboard = useDashboard()
  const loan = dashboard.data?.activeLoan
  const payment = dashboard.data?.nextPayment

  if (dashboard.isPending || (!dashboard.isError && !dashboard.data)) {
    return <CreditSummarySkeleton />
  }

  const unavailable = dashboard.isError || !dashboard.data

  return (
    <section
      className={styles.card}
      aria-labelledby={headingId}
      aria-busy={dashboard.isPending}
    >
      <Typography.TitleResponsive
        tag="h2"
        id={headingId}
        view="xsmall"
        font="system"
        weight="bold"
      >
        Кредиты и лимиты
      </Typography.TitleResponsive>
      <div className={styles.body}>
        <span className={styles.icon}>
          <WalletMIcon aria-hidden="true" />
        </span>
        {unavailable ? (
          <div role={dashboard.isError ? 'alert' : 'status'}>
            <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-small" color="secondary">
              {dashboard.isError
                ? 'Не удалось загрузить сведения о кредите'
                : 'Загружаем сведения о кредите…'}
            </Typography.Text>
          </div>
        ) : loan ? (
          <>
            <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-medium" weight="medium">
              Остаток долга: {formatMoney(loan.remainingBalance)}
            </Typography.Text>
            <dl className={styles.parameters}>
              <div>
                <dt>Кредит</dt>
                <dd>№ {loan.id}</dd>
              </div>
              <div>
                <dt>Годовая ставка</dt>
                <dd>{loan.ratePercent}%</dd>
              </div>
              <div>
                <dt>Срок</dt>
                <dd>{loan.termMonths} мес.</dd>
              </div>
              <div>
                <dt>Дата начала</dt>
                <dd>{formatDate(loan.startDate)}</dd>
              </div>
              <div>
                <dt>Ежемесячный платёж</dt>
                <dd>{formatMoney(loan.monthlyPayment)}</dd>
              </div>
              {payment && (
                <>
                  <div>
                    <dt>Ближайший платёж</dt>
                    <dd>{formatMoney(payment.amount)}</dd>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <dt>Дата платежа</dt>
                    <dd>{formatDate(payment.dueDate)}</dd>
                  </div>
                </>
              )}
            </dl>
            {!payment && (
              <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-small" color="secondary">
                Ближайший платёж не указан.
              </Typography.Text>
            )}
            <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-small" color="secondary">
              Полный график платежей пока недоступен.
            </Typography.Text>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-medium" weight="medium">
              Действующих кредитов пока нет
            </Typography.Text>
            <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-small" color="secondary">
              Принятие предложения не означает выдачу кредита.
            </Typography.Text>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
      {dashboard.isError ? (
        <div>
          <Button
            view="secondary"
            size={40}
            onClick={() => {
              void dashboard.refetch()
            }}
          >
            Повторить загрузку
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : !unavailable && loan ? (
        <div>
          <Button
            view="text"
            size={40}
            rightAddons={<ArrowRightMIcon aria-hidden="true" />}
            onClick={() => {
              void navigate(ROUTES.credits)
            }}
          >
            К кредитам
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  )
}

=== ./src/features/edit-profile/profile-contacts.test.cjs ===
const assert = require('node:assert/strict')
const { readFileSync } = require('node:fs')
const path = require('node:path')
const vm = require('node:vm')
const { test } = require('node:test')
const ts = require('typescript')
const React = require('react')
const { renderToStaticMarkup } = require('react-dom/server')
const { QueryClient, QueryObserver, MutationObserver } = require('@tanstack/react-query')

const root = path.resolve(__dirname, '../..')
const compiled = new Map()
const plain = (value) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value))
const settle = () => new Promise(setImmediate)
const session = {
  userId: 1, fullName: 'Тестовый Предприниматель', role: 'CLIENT', companyId: 1, legalForm: 'IP',
  phone: '+7 (999) 111-00-01', email: 'owner@example.com',
}
const profile = { userId: session.userId, fullName: session.fullName, phone: session.phone, email: session.email }

function load(relative, dependencies) {
  const file = path.join(root, relative)
  if (!compiled.has(file)) compiled.set(file, ts.transpileModule(readFileSync(file, 'utf8'), {
    compilerOptions: { module: ts.ModuleKind.CommonJS, target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2023, jsx: ts.JsxEmit.ReactJSX },
  }).outputText)
  const module = { exports: {} }
  vm.runInNewContext(`(function(require, module, exports) { ${compiled.get(file)}\n})`, { Error }, { filename: file })((id) => {
    if (id === 'react/jsx-runtime') return require(id)
    assert.ok(Object.hasOwn(dependencies, id), `Unexpected dependency: ${id}`)
    return dependencies[id]
  }, module, module.exports)
  return module.exports
}

const contracts = load('entities/profile/index.ts', { zod: require('zod'), '@tanstack/react-query': {}, '@/shared/api': {} })
const auth = load('entities/session/index.ts', { zod: require('zod'), react: {}, '@tanstack/react-query': {}, '@/shared/api': {} })

class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(status, code, message = code) {
    super(message)
    this.status = status
    this.code = code
  }
}

const Input = ({ name, label, value, error, disabled }) => React.createElement('label', null, label,
  React.createElement('input', { name, value, disabled, readOnly: true }), error)
const Button = ({ children, disabled }) => React.createElement('button', { disabled }, children)
const Typography = { TitleResponsive: ({ tag, id, children }) => React.createElement(tag, { id }, children) }
const RequestState = ({ error, children, retry }) => React.createElement('div', { role: error ? 'alert' : 'status' },
  error ? error.message : children, error && retry ? React.createElement('button', null, 'Повторить') : null)

function elements(element) {
  return React.isValidElement(element) ? [element, ...React.Children.toArray(element.props.children).flatMap(elements)] : []
}

function hookRuntime() {
  const slots = []
  let cursor = 0
  let effects = []
  let mounted = true
  let staleWrites = 0
  return {
    begin() { cursor = 0 },
    flush() { const pending = effects; effects = []; pending.forEach((effect) => effect()) },
    useState(initial) {
      const index = cursor++
      if (!slots[index]) slots[index] = { value: typeof initial === 'function' ? initial() : initial }
      return [slots[index].value, (next) => {
        if (!mounted) { staleWrites++; return }
        slots[index].value = typeof next === 'function' ? next(slots[index].value) : next
      }]
    },
    useRef(initial) { return this.useState(() => ({ current: initial }))[0] },
    useId() { return this.useState(() => `profile-${cursor}`)[0] },
    useEffect(effect, dependencies) {
      const index = cursor++
      const previous = slots[index]
      if (previous && dependencies?.every((value, i) => Object.is(value, previous.dependencies[i]))) return
      const slot = { dependencies }
      slots[index] = slot
      effects.push(() => { previous?.cleanup?.(); slot.cleanup = effect() })
    },
    unmount() { mounted = false; slots.forEach((slot) => slot.cleanup?.()) },
    get staleWrites() { return staleWrites },
  }
}

function editor(t, { scope = 1, initialSession = session } = {}) {
  const cache = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: {
    queries: { retry: false, gcTime: Infinity }, mutations: { retry: false, gcTime: Infinity },
  } })
  cache.setQueryData(['session'], initialSession)
  const requests = []
  const invalidations = []
  const runtimes = []
  const state = { scope, generation: 0, switching: false }
  let runtime
  let key
  let element
  const hooks = Object.fromEntries(['useId', 'useRef', 'useState', 'useEffect'].map((name) => [name, (...args) => runtime[name](...args)]))
  const invalidate = cache.invalidateQueries.bind(cache)
  cache.invalidateQueries = (filters) => { invalidations.push(filters); return invalidate(filters) }
  const api = {
    ApiError, useApiScope: () => state.scope, useSessionTransition: () => state,
    getSessionGeneration: () => state.generation, isSessionSwitching: () => state.switching, syncSessionChanges() {},
    apiRequest: (url, schema, options = {}) => {
      let resolve
      let reject
      const pending = new Promise((done, fail) => { resolve = done; reject = fail })
      requests.push({ url, options, resolve, reject })
      return pending.then((value) => {
        const parsed = schema.safeParse(value)
        if (!parsed.success) throw new ApiError(200, 'INVALID_RESPONSE')
        return parsed.data
      })
    },
  }
  const query = {
    useQueryClient: () => cache,
    useQuery: (options) => {
      const [observer] = hooks.useState(() => new QueryObserver(cache, options))
      hooks.useEffect(() => observer.setOptions(options), [observer, options])
      hooks.useEffect(() => observer.subscribe(() => {}), [observer])
      return observer.getCurrentResult()
    },
    useMutation: (options) => {
      const [observer] = hooks.useState(() => new MutationObserver(cache, options))
      hooks.useEffect(() => observer.setOptions(options), [observer, options])
      hooks.useEffect(() => observer.subscribe(() => {}), [observer])
      return {
        ...observer.getCurrentResult(),
        mutate: (variables, callbacks) => { void observer.mutate(variables, callbacks).catch(() => {}) },
      }
    },
  }
  const sessionEntity = load('entities/session/index.ts', { react: hooks, zod: require('zod'), '@tanstack/react-query': query, '@/shared/api': api })
  const profileEntity = load('entities/profile/index.ts', { zod: require('zod'), '@tanstack/react-query': query, '@/shared/api': api })
  const { ProfileContacts } = load('features/edit-profile/ui/ProfileContacts/ProfileContacts.tsx', {
    react: hooks, '@tanstack/react-query': query, '@/shared/api': api, '@/entities/profile': profileEntity,
    '@/entities/session': { ...sessionEntity, useSession: () => ({ data: cache.getQueryData(['session']) }) },
    '@alfalab/core-components/input': { Input }, '@alfalab/core-components/button': { Button },
    '@alfalab/core-components/typography': { Typography }, '@/shared/ui': { RequestState },
    './ProfileContacts.module.scss': { __esModule: true, default: new Proxy({}, { get: (_, name) => String(name) }) },
  })
  const h = {
    cache, state, requests, invalidations, runtimes,
    render() {
      const outer = ProfileContacts()
      if (!outer) { runtime?.unmount(); runtime = null; element = null; return null }
      if (!runtime || key !== outer.key) {
        runtime?.unmount()
        runtime = hookRuntime()
        runtimes.push(runtime)
        key = outer.key
      }
      runtime.begin()
      element = outer.type(outer.props)
      runtime.flush()
      return element
    },
    find(predicate) { return elements(element).find(predicate) },
    field(name) { return this.find((item) => item.type === Input && item.props.name === name) },
    get form() { return this.find((item) => item.type === 'form') },
    get button() { return this.find((item) => item.type === Button) },
    get requestState() { return this.find((item) => item.type === RequestState) },
    html() { return renderToStaticMarkup(element) },
    edit(name, value) { this.field(name).props.onChange(null, { value }); this.render() },
    submit() { this.form.props.onSubmit({ preventDefault() {} }); this.render() },
    latest(url = '/api/profile', method) { return requests.findLast((item) => item.url === url && item.options.method === method) },
    async resolveProfile(value = profile) {
      await settle()
      this.latest().resolve(value)
      await settle()
      this.render()
    },
    observeSession() {
      const observer = new QueryObserver(cache, {
        queryKey: ['session'], staleTime: Infinity,
        queryFn: ({ signal }) => api.apiRequest('/api/auth/me', auth.sessionSchema, { signal }),
      })
      t.after(observer.subscribe(() => {}))
    },
    unmount() { runtime?.unmount(); runtime = null },
  }
  h.render()
  t.after(() => { h.unmount(); cache.clear() })
  return h
}

test('profile and login/me contracts require present nullable contacts and positive user IDs', () => {
  for (const [schema, data] of [[contracts.profileSchema, profile], [auth.sessionSchema, session]]) {
    assert.deepEqual(plain(schema.parse({ ...data, phone: null, email: null })), { ...data, phone: null, email: null })
    for (const field of ['phone', 'email']) {
      const incomplete = { ...data }
      delete incomplete[field]
      assert.equal(schema.safeParse(incomplete).success, false)
    }
    for (const userId of [0, -1, 1.5]) assert.equal(schema.safeParse({ ...data, userId }).success, false)
  }
})

test('optional PUT validation matches the backend phone expression without trimming or a digit-count rule', () => {
  for (const [phone, valid] of [
    [null, true], ['', true], ['1234567890', true], ['+7 (999) 111-00-01', true],
    ['1'.repeat(19), true], [`+${'1'.repeat(19)}`, true], ['7         ', true], ['1234567890 ', true],
    ['123456789', false], ['1'.repeat(20), false], ['+1'.repeat(10), false], [' 1234567890', false],
    ['(999)1110001', false], ['          ', false], ['+7999abc0000', false],
  ]) assert.equal(contracts.profileContactsSchema.safeParse({ phone, email: null }).success, valid, String(phone))
  const raw = { phone: '1234567890 ', email: 'Owner+bank@Example.com' }
  assert.deepEqual(plain(contracts.profileContactsSchema.parse(raw)), raw)
  const email255 = `a@${['b'.repeat(63), 'c'.repeat(63), 'd'.repeat(63), 'e'.repeat(58), 'ru'].join('.')}`
  assert.equal(email255.length, 255)
  for (const email of ['', null, 'owner@example.com', email255]) {
    assert.equal(contracts.profileContactsSchema.safeParse({ phone: null, email }).success, true)
  }
  for (const email of ['invalid', ' owner@example.com', 'owner@example.com ', ' ', `a${email255}`]) {
    assert.equal(contracts.profileContactsSchema.safeParse({ phone: null, email }).success, false)
  }
  assert.equal(contracts.profileContactsSchema.safeParse({ phone: null }).success, false)
  assert.equal(contracts.profileContactsSchema.safeParse({ email: null }).success, false)
})

test('mount loads the scoped profile with loading, backend errors, retry and optional Alfa inputs', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t)
  assert.ok(h.html().includes('Загружаем профиль'))
  await settle()
  assert.equal(h.requests.length, 1)
  assert.equal(h.requests[0].url, '/api/profile')
  assert.ok(h.requests[0].options.signal instanceof AbortSignal)
  assert.ok(h.cache.getQueryState(['client', 1, 'profile']))
  h.requests[0].reject(new ApiError(503, 'PROFILE_UNAVAILABLE', 'Профиль временно недоступен'))
  await settle()
  h.render()
  assert.ok(h.html().includes('Профиль временно недоступен'))
  assert.ok(h.html().includes('Повторить'))
  h.requestState.props.retry()
  await h.resolveProfile()
  assert.ok(h.html().includes('Контактные данные'))
  assert.equal(h.field('phone').props.type, 'tel')
  assert.equal(h.field('email').props.type, 'email')
  for (const field of ['phone', 'email']) {
    assert.equal(h.field(field).props.labelView, 'outer')
    assert.equal(h.field(field).props.block, true)
    assert.equal(h.field(field).props.required, undefined)
    assert.equal(h.field(field).props.value, profile[field])
  }
  assert.equal(h.button.props.disabled, true)
})

test('the embeddable UI does not request a profile for a missing scope, another user or an officer', async (t) => {
  for (const options of [
    { scope: null }, { initialSession: null }, { scope: 2 },
    { initialSession: { ...session, role: 'BANK_OFFICER', companyId: null, legalForm: null } },
  ]) {
    const h = editor(t, options)
    await settle()
    assert.equal(h.render(), null)
    assert.equal(h.requests.length, 0)
  }
})

test('refetches, including a clear or a failure, never wipe dirty contact inputs', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t)
  await h.resolveProfile()
  h.edit('phone', '')
  void h.cache.refetchQueries({ queryKey: ['client', 1, 'profile'] })
  await h.resolveProfile({ ...profile, phone: '+79991110002', email: 'refetched@example.com' })
  assert.equal(h.field('phone').props.value, '')
  assert.equal(h.field('email').props.value, 'refetched@example.com')
  h.edit('email', 'typed@example.com')
  void h.cache.refetchQueries({ queryKey: ['client', 1, 'profile'] })
  await settle()
  h.latest().reject(new ApiError(500, 'PROFILE_ERROR', 'Не удалось обновить профиль'))
  await settle()
  h.render()
  assert.ok(h.form)
  assert.ok(h.html().includes('Не удалось обновить профиль'))
  h.requestState.props.retry()
  await h.resolveProfile({ ...profile, phone: null, email: null })
  assert.equal(h.field('phone').props.value, '')
  assert.equal(h.field('email').props.value, 'typed@example.com')
})

test('PUT always sends both contacts, prevents duplicate saves and reconciles normalized contacts with me', async (t) => {
  for (const field of ['phone', 'email']) {
    const h = editor(t)
    await h.resolveProfile()
    h.observeSession()
    const value = field === 'phone' ? '1234567890 ' : 'new@example.com'
    h.edit(field, value)
    const submit = h.form.props.onSubmit
    submit({ preventDefault() {} })
    submit({ preventDefault() {} })
    await settle()
    h.render()
    assert.equal(h.requests.filter((request) => request.options.method === 'PUT').length, 1)
    const request = h.latest('/api/profile', 'PUT')
    assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(request.options.body), { phone: profile.phone, email: profile.email, [field]: value })
    assert.equal(h.form.props['aria-busy'], true)
    assert.equal(h.button.props.loading, true)
    assert.ok(h.html().includes('Сохраняем контакты'))
    assert.equal(h.field('phone').props.disabled, true)
    assert.equal(h.field('email').props.disabled, true)
    const saved = { ...profile, [field]: value.trim() }
    request.resolve(saved)
    await settle()
    h.render()
    assert.deepEqual(plain(h.cache.getQueryData(['client', 1, 'profile'])), saved)
    assert.equal(h.cache.getQueryData(['session'])[field], saved[field])
    assert.ok(h.latest('/api/auth/me'))
    assert.equal(h.button.props.loading, true)
    assert.ok(!h.html().includes('Контакты сохранены.'))
    h.latest('/api/auth/me').resolve({ ...session, phone: saved.phone, email: saved.email })
    await settle()
    h.render()
    assert.equal(h.field(field).props.value, saved[field])
    assert.equal(h.field(field).props.disabled, false)
    assert.equal(h.button.props.disabled, true)
    assert.ok(h.html().includes('Контакты сохранены.'))
    assert.deepEqual(plain(h.invalidations), [{ queryKey: ['session'], exact: true }])
    h.edit(field, '')
    assert.ok(!h.html().includes('Контакты сохранены.'))
  }
})

test('empty input clears a contact and preserves the other nullable field in the PUT body', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t)
  await h.resolveProfile({ ...profile, email: null })
  h.edit('phone', '')
  h.submit()
  await settle()
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(h.latest('/api/profile', 'PUT').options.body), { phone: '', email: null })
  h.latest('/api/profile', 'PUT').resolve({ ...profile, phone: null, email: null })
  await settle()
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.field('phone').props.value, '')
  assert.equal(h.field('email').props.value, '')
  assert.equal(h.cache.getQueryData(['session']).phone, null)
  assert.equal(h.cache.getQueryData(['session']).email, null)
  assert.ok(h.html().includes('Контакты сохранены.'))
})

test('invalid input is blocked locally; server validation errors remain visible and retry preserves edits', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t)
  await h.resolveProfile()
  h.edit('email', 'invalid')
  h.submit()
  assert.ok(h.field('email').props.error)
  assert.equal(h.requests.length, 1)
  h.edit('email', 'valid@example.com')
  h.submit()
  await settle()
  h.latest('/api/profile', 'PUT').reject(new ApiError(400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'email: rejected by server'))
  await settle()
  h.render()
  assert.ok(h.html().includes('email: rejected by server'))
  assert.equal(h.field('email').props.value, 'valid@example.com')
  assert.equal(h.button.props.disabled, false)
  assert.ok(!h.html().includes('Контакты сохранены.'))
  assert.equal(h.invalidations.length, 0)
  h.submit()
  await settle()
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(h.latest('/api/profile', 'PUT').options.body), { phone: profile.phone, email: 'valid@example.com' })
})

test('GET and PUT responses for another user are rejected without updating private caches', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t)
  await h.resolveProfile({ ...profile, userId: 2 })
  assert.equal(h.form, undefined)
  assert.ok(h.html().includes('Не удалось проверить владельца профиля'))
  h.requestState.props.retry()
  await h.resolveProfile()
  h.edit('email', 'new@example.com')
  h.submit()
  await settle()
  h.latest('/api/profile', 'PUT').resolve({ ...profile, userId: 2, email: 'other@example.com' })
  await settle()
  h.render()
  assert.ok(h.html().includes('Не удалось проверить владельца профиля'))
  assert.deepEqual(plain(h.cache.getQueryData(['session'])), session)
  assert.deepEqual(plain(h.cache.getQueryData(['client', 1, 'profile'])), profile)
  assert.equal(h.invalidations.length, 0)
})

test('older in-flight profile and me responses cannot overwrite a successful PUT', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t)
  await h.resolveProfile()
  h.observeSession()
  void h.cache.refetchQueries()
  await settle()
  const oldReads = h.requests.slice(1)
  assert.equal(oldReads.length, 2)
  h.edit('email', 'saved@example.com')
  h.submit()
  await settle()
  const saved = { ...profile, email: 'saved@example.com' }
  h.latest('/api/profile', 'PUT').resolve(saved)
  await settle()
  assert.ok(oldReads.every((request) => request.options.signal.aborted))
  for (const request of oldReads) request.resolve(request.url === '/api/auth/me' ? session : profile)
  await settle()
  assert.equal(h.cache.getQueryData(['session']).email, saved.email)
  assert.equal(h.cache.getQueryData(['client', 1, 'profile']).email, saved.email)
  h.latest('/api/auth/me').resolve({ ...session, email: saved.email })
  await settle()
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.field('email').props.value, saved.email)
})

test('scope and generation fences apply before PUT, after its response and after query cancellation', async (t) => {
  for (const moment of ['before-request', 'response', 'after-cancel', 'same-user-generation', 'different-scope']) {
    const h = editor(t)
    await h.resolveProfile()
    h.edit('email', 'stale@example.com')
    const nextSession = { ...session, userId: moment === 'same-user-generation' ? 1 : 2, email: 'current@example.com' }
    const change = () => {
      if (moment !== 'different-scope') h.state.generation++
      h.cache.setQueryData(['session'], nextSession)
    }
    if (moment === 'after-cancel') {
      const cancel = h.cache.cancelQueries.bind(h.cache)
      h.cache.cancelQueries = async (...args) => { await cancel(...args); change() }
    }
    h.submit()
    if (moment === 'before-request') change()
    await settle()
    if (moment === 'before-request') assert.equal(h.latest('/api/profile', 'PUT'), undefined)
    else {
      if (moment !== 'after-cancel') change()
      h.latest('/api/profile', 'PUT').resolve({ ...profile, email: 'stale@example.com' })
      await settle()
    }
    assert.deepEqual(plain(h.cache.getQueryData(['session'])), nextSession, moment)
    assert.deepEqual(plain(h.cache.getQueryData(['client', 1, 'profile'])), profile, moment)
    assert.equal(h.invalidations.length, 0, moment)
  }
})

test('closing the panel still reconciles a valid save without writing to the unmounted form', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t)
  await h.resolveProfile()
  h.edit('email', 'saved@example.com')
  h.submit()
  await settle()
  h.unmount()
  h.latest('/api/profile', 'PUT').resolve({ ...profile, email: 'saved@example.com' })
  await settle()
  assert.equal(h.cache.getQueryData(['session']).email, 'saved@example.com')
  assert.equal(h.runtimes[0].staleWrites, 0)
})

=== ./src/features/edit-profile/index.ts ===
export { ProfileCompany } from './ui/ProfileCompany'
export { ProfileContacts } from './ui/ProfileContacts'

=== ./src/features/edit-profile/ui/ProfileContacts/ProfileContacts.tsx ===
import { useId, useRef, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { Button } from '@alfalab/core-components/button'
import { Input } from '@alfalab/core-components/input'
import { Typography } from '@alfalab/core-components/typography'
import {
  profileContactsSchema,
  profileKey,
  profileSchema,
  useProfile,
  type ProfileContactsInput,
} from '@/entities/profile'
import {
  isCurrentSession,
  sessionKey,
  useSession,
  type Session,
} from '@/entities/session'
import {
  apiRequest,
  ApiError,
  useApiScope,
  useSessionTransition,
} from '@/shared/api'
import { RequestState } from '@/shared/ui'
import styles from './ProfileContacts.module.scss'

/** Optional client contacts for embedding in the existing company profile panel. */
export function ProfileContacts() {
  const scope = useApiScope()
  const session = useSession()
  const transition = useSessionTransition()
  if (
    scope === null ||
    transition.switching ||
    session.data?.userId !== scope ||
    session.data.role !== 'CLIENT'
  )
    return null
  return (
    <ProfileContactsEditor
      key={`${scope}:${transition.generation}`}
      scope={scope}
      generation={transition.generation}
    />
  )
}

function ProfileContactsEditor({
  scope,
  generation,
}: {
  scope: number
  generation: number
}) {
  const id = useId()
  const cache = useQueryClient()
  const profile = useProfile()
  const [edits, setEdits] = useState<Partial<Record<'phone' | 'email', string>>>({})
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({})
  const formRef = useRef<HTMLFormElement>(null)
  const saving = useRef(false)
  const contacts = {
    phone: edits.phone ?? profile.data?.phone ?? null,
    email: edits.email ?? profile.data?.email ?? null,
  }
  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (body: ProfileContactsInput) => {
      if (!isCurrentSession(cache, scope, generation))
        throw new ApiError(
          409,
          'SESSION_CHANGED',
          'Профиль изменился. Повторите действие.',
        )
      const saved = await apiRequest('/api/profile', profileSchema, {
        method: 'PUT',
        body: JSON.stringify(body),
      })
      if (saved.userId !== scope)
        throw new ApiError(
          200,
          'INVALID_RESPONSE',
          'Не удалось проверить владельца профиля.',
        )
      return saved
    },
    onSuccess: async (saved) => {
      if (!isCurrentSession(cache, scope, generation)) return
      await Promise.all([
        cache.cancelQueries({ queryKey: profileKey(scope), exact: true }),
        cache.cancelQueries({ queryKey: sessionKey, exact: true }),
      ])
      if (!isCurrentSession(cache, scope, generation)) return
      cache.setQueryData(profileKey(scope), saved)
      cache.setQueryData<Session | null>(sessionKey, (current) =>
        current?.userId === scope
          ? { ...current, phone: saved.phone, email: saved.email }
          : current,
      )
      await cache.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: sessionKey, exact: true })
    },
    onSettled: () => {
      saving.current = false
    },
    retry: false,
  })

  function edit(field: 'phone' | 'email', value: string) {
    if (saving.current) return
    setEdits((previous) => ({ ...previous, [field]: value }))
    setErrors({})
    save.reset()
  }

  function submit(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (
      saving.current ||
      !profile.data ||
      !isCurrentSession(cache, scope, generation)
    )
      return
    const result = profileContactsSchema.safeParse(contacts)
    if (!result.success) {
      setErrors(
        Object.fromEntries(
          result.error.issues.map((issue) => [String(issue.path[0]), issue.message]),
        ),
      )
      const field = String(result.error.issues[0]?.path[0])
      formRef.current?.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>(`[name="${field}"]`)?.focus()
      return
    }
    setErrors({})
    saving.current = true
    save.mutate(result.data, {
      onSuccess: () => {
        if (isCurrentSession(cache, scope, generation)) setEdits({})
      },
    })
  }

  if (!profile.data)
    return (
      <RequestState
        error={profile.error}
        retry={() => {
          void profile.refetch()
        }}
      >
        Загружаем профиль...
      </RequestState>
    )

  return (
    <form
      ref={formRef}
      className={styles.form}
      onSubmit={submit}
      noValidate
      aria-labelledby={`${id}-title`}
      aria-busy={save.isPending}
    >
      <Typography.TitleResponsive
        id={`${id}-title`}
        tag="h3"
        view="xsmall"
        font="system"
        className={styles.title}
      >
        Контактные данные
      </Typography.TitleResponsive>
      <Input
        id={`${id}-phone`}
        name="phone"
        type="tel"
        autoComplete="tel"
        block
        label="Телефон"
        labelView="outer"
        aria-describedby={`${id}-hint`}
        value={contacts.phone ?? ''}
        error={errors.phone}
        disabled={save.isPending}
        onChange={(_, { value }) => edit('phone', value)}
      />
      <Input
        id={`${id}-email`}
        name="email"
        type="email"
        autoComplete="email"
        block
        label="Email"
        labelView="outer"
        maxLength={255}
        aria-describedby={`${id}-hint`}
        value={contacts.email ?? ''}
        error={errors.email}
        disabled={save.isPending}
        onChange={(_, { value }) => edit('email', value)}
      />
      {profile.error && (
        <RequestState
          error={profile.error}
          retry={() => {
            void profile.refetch()
          }}
        />
      )}
      {save.error && <RequestState error={save.error} />}
      <Button
        type="submit"
        view="primary"
        size={48}
        block
        loading={save.isPending}
        disabled={save.isPending || Object.keys(edits).length === 0}
      >
        Сохранить контакты
      </Button>
      <p
        className={styles.status}
        role="status"
        aria-live="polite"
        aria-atomic="true"
      >
        {save.isPending
          ? 'Сохраняем контакты...'
          : save.isSuccess
            ? 'Контакты сохранены.'
            : ''}
      </p>
    </form>
  )
}
