function clock() {
  let now = 0
  let id = 0
  const timers = new Map()
  return {
    setTimeout(callback, delay) {
      timers.set(++id, { callback, at: now + delay })
      return id
    },
    clearTimeout: (key) => timers.delete(key),
    get now() { return now },
    get size() { return timers.size },
    advance(milliseconds) {
      const target = now + milliseconds
      for (;;) {
        const next = [...timers].filter(([, timer]) => timer.at <= target).sort((a, b) => a[1].at - b[1].at)[0]
        if (!next) break
        now = next[1].at
        timers.delete(next[0])
        next[1].callback()
      }
      now = target
    },
  }
}

function hookRuntime() {
  const slots = []
  let cursor = 0
  let effects = []
  let mounted = true
  let staleWrites = 0
  return {
    begin() { cursor = 0 },
    flush() {
      const pending = effects
      effects = []
      pending.forEach((effect) => effect())
    },
    useState(initial) {
      const index = cursor++
      if (!slots[index]) slots[index] = { value: typeof initial === 'function' ? initial() : initial }
      return [slots[index].value, (next) => {
        if (!mounted) { staleWrites++; return }
        slots[index].value = typeof next === 'function' ? next(slots[index].value) : next
      }]
    },
    useRef(initial) { return this.useState(() => ({ current: initial }))[0] },
    useId() { return this.useState(() => `field-${cursor}`)[0] },
    useEffect(effect, dependencies) {
      const index = cursor++
      const previous = slots[index]
      if (previous && dependencies?.every((value, i) => Object.is(value, previous.dependencies[i]))) return
      const slot = { dependencies, effect }
      slots[index] = slot
      effects.push(() => {
        previous?.cleanup?.()
        slot.cleanup = effect()
      })
    },
    replayEffects() {
      slots.filter((slot) => slot.effect).forEach((slot) => {
        slot.cleanup?.()
        slot.cleanup = slot.effect()
      })
    },
    unmount() {
      mounted = false
      slots.forEach((slot) => slot.cleanup?.())
    },
    get staleWrites() { return staleWrites },
  }
}

function elements(element) {
  if (!React.isValidElement(element)) return []
  return [element, ...React.Children.toArray(element.props.children).flatMap(elements)]
}

const settle = () => new Promise(setImmediate)
const plain = (value) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value))

function editor(t, { abortAware = false, localDraft: initialLocal = null, initialDraft = null, session: initialSession = {} } = {}) {
  const time = clock()
  const storage = new Map()
  if (initialLocal) storage.set('application-draft:1', JSON.stringify(initialLocal))
  const sessionEvents = new EventTarget()
  const requests = []
  const invalidations = []
  const navigations = []
  const runtimes = []
  const observers = []
  const cache = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { mutations: { retry: 2, gcTime: Infinity } } })
  const invalidate = cache.invalidateQueries.bind(cache)
  cache.invalidateQueries = (options) => { invalidations.push(options); return invalidate(options) }
  let scope = 1
  let generation = 0
  let switching = false
  let runtime
  let key
  let element
  const hooks = Object.fromEntries(['useState', 'useRef', 'useEffect', 'useId'].map((name) => [name, (...args) => runtime[name](...args)]))
  const api = {
    ApiError, sessionEvents, useApiScope: () => scope,
    getSessionGeneration: () => generation, isSessionSwitching: () => switching,
    syncSessionChanges() {},
    apiRequest: (url, schema, options) => {
      let resolve
      let reject
      const promise = new Promise((done, fail) => { resolve = done; reject = fail })
      requests.push({ url, options, resolve, reject })
      const abort = () => reject(options.signal.reason)
      if (abortAware) options.signal?.addEventListener('abort', abort, { once: true })
      return promise.then((value) => {
        const result = schema.safeParse(value)
        if (!result.success) throw new ApiError(200, 'INVALID_RESPONSE')
        return result.data
      }).finally(() => options.signal?.removeEventListener('abort', abort))
    },
  }
  const query = {
    useQueryClient: () => cache,
    useMutation: (options) => {
      const [observer] = hooks.useState(() => {
        const instance = new MutationObserver(cache, options)
        observers.push(instance)
        return instance
      })
      hooks.useEffect(() => observer.setOptions(options), [observer, options])
      hooks.useEffect(() => observer.subscribe(() => {}), [observer])
      return {
        ...observer.getCurrentResult(),
        mutate: (variables, callbacks) => { void observer.mutate(variables, callbacks).catch(() => {}) },
      }
    },
  }
  const globals = {
    AbortController, DOMException, Error, Event,
    performance: { now: () => time.now },
    setTimeout: time.setTimeout, clearTimeout: time.clearTimeout,
    sessionStorage: {
      getItem: (name) => storage.get(name) ?? null,
      setItem: (name, value) => storage.set(name, value),
      removeItem: (name) => storage.delete(name),
    },
    window: new EventTarget(),
    document: { querySelector: () => null },
    requestAnimationFrame: (callback) => callback(),
  }
  const draft = { data: initialDraft, isPending: false, error: null }
  const dashboard = {
    data: { company: {
      legalForm: 'IP', inn: '123456789012', name: 'ИП Тестовый Предприниматель', okved: '47.11',
      externalDemo: { bkiScore: 680, delinquencies12m: 1, inquiries12m: 4, revenueTrend: 'DECLINING', companyAgeYears: 9 },
    } },
    isPending: false, error: null,
  }
  const localDraft = load(`${feature}/model/local-draft.ts`, {
    zod: require('zod'), react: hooks, '@/entities/application-form': formEntity,
  }, globals)
  const prefill = load(`${feature}/model/use-application-prefill.ts`, {
    react: hooks, '@tanstack/react-query': query, '@/entities/application-form': formEntity, '@/shared/api': api,
  }, globals)
  const sessionEntity = load('entities/session/index.ts', {
    react: hooks, zod: require('zod'), '@tanstack/react-query': query, '@/shared/api': api,
  }, globals)
  cache.setQueryData(sessionEntity.sessionKey, {
    userId: 1, fullName: 'Тестовый Предприниматель', role: 'CLIENT', companyId: 1,
    legalForm: 'IP', phone: null, email: null, ...initialSession,
  })
  const { ApplicationForm } = load(`${feature}/ui/ApplicationForm/ApplicationForm.tsx`, {
    react: hooks, zod: require('zod'), '@tanstack/react-query': query,
    'react-router-dom': { useNavigate: () => (...args) => navigations.push(args) },
    '@alfalab/core-components/button': { Button }, '@alfalab/core-components/input': { Input },
    '@alfalab/core-components/select': { Select }, '@alfalab/core-components/textarea': { Textarea },
    '@alfalab/core-components/modal': { Modal }, '@alfalab/core-components/typography': { Typography },
    '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/ArrowRightMIcon': { ArrowRightMIcon: () => null },
    '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/CrossMIcon': { CrossMIcon: () => null },
    '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/DocumentMIcon': { DocumentMIcon: () => null },
    '@/entities/loan-application': loanEntity,
    '@/entities/application-form': { ...formEntity, useApplicationDraft: () => draft },
    '@/entities/dashboard': { useDashboard: () => dashboard }, '@/entities/company': companyEntity,
    '@/entities/session': { ...sessionEntity, useSession: () => ({ data: cache.getQueryData(sessionEntity.sessionKey) }) }, '@/shared/api': api,
    '@/shared/config': { ROUTES: { applications: '/applications' } },
    '@/shared/lib': { formatMoney: (value) => String(value) },
    '@/shared/ui': { FieldLabel: () => null, RequestState: () => null, Panel: () => null },
    '@/features/edit-profile': { ProfileCompany: () => null },
    '../IpApplicationFields': { IpApplicationFields }, '../ApplicationPreparation': { ApplicationPreparation, ApplicationPreparationVisual },
    '../OooApplicationFields': { OooApplicationFields },
    '../ExternalDataFields': { ExternalDataFields },
    '../../model/local-draft': { ...localDraft, useLocalDraft: localDraft.loadLocalDraft },
    '../../model/use-application-prefill': prefill,
    './ApplicationForm.module.scss': cssModule,
  }, globals)
  const h = {
    time, requests, storage, invalidations, navigations, observers, dashboard, runtimes, cache, api, sessionEntity,
    render() {
      const outer = ApplicationForm({ headingTag: 'h2' })
      if (!runtime || key !== outer.key) {
        runtime?.unmount()
        runtime = hookRuntime()
        runtimes.push(runtime)
        key = outer.key
      }
      runtime.begin()
      element = outer.type(outer.props)
      runtime.flush()
      return element
    },
    find(predicate) { return elements(element).find(predicate) },
    get card() { return this.find((item) => item.props.className === 'card') },
    get preparation() { return this.find((item) => item.type === ApplicationPreparation) },
    get live() { return this.find((item) => item.props['aria-live'] === 'polite') },
    get modal() { return this.find((item) => item.type === Modal && item.props.className === 'modal') },
    get form() { return this.find((item) => item.type === 'form') },
    get ip() { return this.find((item) => item.type === IpApplicationFields) },
    get external() { return this.find((item) => item.type === ExternalDataFields) },
    get buttons() { return React.Children.toArray(this.find((item) => item.props.className === 'actions').props.children) },
    field(name) { return this.find((item) => item.props.name === name) },
    change(name, value) {
      const field = this.field(name)
      if (field.type === Select) field.props.onChange({ selected: { key: value } })
      else field.props.onChange(null, { value })
      this.render()
    },
    click(index) { this.buttons[index].props.onClick({ currentTarget: { isConnected: true, focus() {} } }) },
    begin(text = description) { this.change('description', text); this.click(0) },
    html() { return renderToStaticMarkup(element) },
    next() { this.form.props.onSubmit({ preventDefault() {} }) },
    editIp(patch, section = 'business') {
      this.find((item) => item.type === IpApplicationFields && item.props.section === section).props.onChange(patch)
      this.render()
    },
    editExternal(patch) {
      this.external.props.onChange(patch)
      this.render()
    },
    blurExternal(field) {
      this.external.props.onBlur(field)
      this.render()
    },
    async resolveDraft(registrationDate) {
      await settle()
      const request = requests.at(-1)
      assert.equal(request.url, '/api/applications/draft')
      const snapshot = JSON.parse(request.options.body)
      const savedForm = plain(snapshot.form)
      if (registrationDate !== undefined) savedForm.step2_businessData.applicant.registrationDate = registrationDate
      request.resolve({
        ...snapshot, form: savedForm, revision: (snapshot.revision ?? -1) + 1,
        updatedAt: '2026-10-23T12:00:00Z',
      })
      await settle()
      this.render()
      return snapshot
    },
    resume() {
      this.find((item) => item.type === Button && item.props.children === 'Продолжить заявку').props.onClick({ currentTarget: {} })
      this.render()
    },
    close() { this.modal.props.onClose(); this.render() },
    changeScope() { scope = 2; this.render() },
    refreshSession(patch) {
      cache.setQueryData(sessionEntity.sessionKey, (previous) => ({ ...previous, ...patch }))
      this.render()
    },
    changeSession(notify = true) {
      generation++
      switching = true
      if (notify) sessionEvents.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'))
    },
    replayEffects() { runtime.replayEffects() },
    unmount() { runtime.unmount() },
  }
  h.render()
  t.after(() => { h.unmount(); cache.clear() })
  return h
}

test('empty input still validates, while manual entry needs no preparation request', (t) => {
  const h = editor(t)
  h.click(0)
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.requests.length, 0)
  assert.equal(h.field('description').props.error, 'Опишите, на что нужны деньги')
  assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, false)
  h.click(1)
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, true)
  assert.equal(h.requests.length, 0)
})

test('a double click sends one request and a fast response waits for three seconds of animation', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t)
  h.replayEffects()
  h.change('description', description)
  const click = h.buttons[0].props.onClick
  click({ currentTarget: {} })
  click({ currentTarget: {} })
  await settle()
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.requests.length, 1)
  assert.equal(h.requests[0].url, '/api/ai/prefill')
  assert.equal(h.requests[0].options.method, 'POST')
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(h.requests[0].options.body), { text: description.trim() })
  assert.equal(h.card.props['aria-busy'], true)
  assert.equal(h.buttons[0].props.disabled, true)
  assert.equal(h.buttons[1].props.disabled, false)
  assert.equal(h.field('description').props.readOnly, true)
  assert.equal(h.preparation.props.visible, true)
  h.time.advance(100)
  h.requests[0].resolve(response())
  await settle()
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, false)
  assert.equal(h.card.props['aria-busy'], true)
  h.time.advance(2899)
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, false)
  assert.equal(h.preparation.props.visible, true)
  h.time.advance(1)
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, true)
  assert.equal(h.card.props['aria-busy'], false)
  assert.equal(h.preparation.props.visible, false)
  assert.equal(h.time.size, 0)
  assert.equal(h.field('amount').props.value, '200000')
  assert.equal(h.field('description').props.value, description)
  assert.equal(h.requests.length, 1)
})

test('IP and OOO wait only for the remainder of three seconds, never after a slower response', async (t) => {
  for (const legalForm of ['IP', 'OOO']) {
    for (const duration of [100, 2999, 3000, 3500]) {
      const h = editor(t, { session: { legalForm } })
      h.dashboard.data.company.legalForm = legalForm
      h.render()
      h.begin()
      await settle()
      h.time.advance(duration)
      const prepared = response()
      prepared.form.step2_businessData.applicant.type = legalForm
      h.requests[0].resolve(prepared)
      await settle()
      h.render()
      assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, duration >= 3000, `${legalForm}: ${duration} ms`)
      if (duration < 3000) {
        assert.equal(h.preparation.props.visible, true)
        h.time.advance(3000 - duration)
        h.render()
      }
      assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, true)
      assert.equal(h.field('amount').props.value, '200000')
      assert.equal(h.card.props['aria-busy'], false)
      assert.equal(h.time.size, 0)
      assert.equal(h.requests.length, 1)
    }
  }
})

test('cancelling a successful response during the minimum display time clears its delayed application', async (t) => {
  for (const reason of ['manual', 'session', 'scope', 'unmount']) {
    const h = editor(t)
    h.begin()
    await settle()
    h.time.advance(100)
    h.requests[0].resolve(response({ amount: 999999 }))
    await settle()
    h.render()
    assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, false)
    assert.equal(h.card.props['aria-busy'], true)
    if (reason === 'manual') {
      h.click(1)
      h.render()
      h.change('amount', '700000')
      h.close()
    } else if (reason === 'session') h.changeSession()
    else if (reason === 'scope') h.changeScope()
    else h.unmount()
    const before = [...h.storage]
    assert.equal(h.time.size, 0)
    assert.equal(h.requests[0].options.signal.aborted, true)
    h.time.advance(60000)
    await settle()
    if (reason !== 'unmount') {
      h.render()
      assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, false)
      assert.equal(h.card.props['aria-busy'], false)
      assert.equal(h.field('amount').props.value, reason === 'manual' ? '700000' : '')
    }
    assert.equal(h.runtimes[0].staleWrites, 0)
    assert.deepEqual([...h.storage], before)
  }
})

  assert.equal(h.time.size, 0)
})

    assert.ok(!h.html().includes(fallbackMessage))
    assert.equal(h.requests.length, 1)
  }
})

test('an abort-aware transport does not report intentional cancellation as a failure', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t, { abortAware: true })
  h.begin()
  await settle()
  h.click(1)
  await settle()
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, true)
  assert.equal(h.field('description').props.value, description)
  assert.ok(!h.html().includes(fallbackMessage))
  assert.equal(h.time.size, 0)
})

test('cancellation or a generation change before mutation execution prevents the initial API call', async (t) => {
  for (const reason of ['manual', 'unmount', 'session']) {
    const h = editor(t)
    h.begin()
    if (reason === 'unmount') h.unmount()
    else if (reason === 'session') h.changeSession(false)
    else h.click(1)
    await settle()
    assert.equal(h.requests.length, 0)
    assert.equal(h.time.size, 0)
    assert.equal(h.runtimes[0].staleWrites, 0)
    if (reason !== 'unmount') {
      h.render()
      assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, reason === 'manual')
      assert.equal(h.card.props['aria-busy'], false)
      assert.equal(h.field('description').props.value, description)
      assert.ok(!h.html().includes(fallbackMessage))
    }
  }
})

test('settling an abandoned request cannot stop or apply data over a newer attempt', async (t) => {
  for (const fail of [false, true]) {
    const h = editor(t)
    h.begin()
    await settle()
    h.click(1)
    h.render()
    h.close()
    h.begin('Новая бизнес-задача')
    await settle()
    h.time.advance(150)
    if (fail) h.requests[0].reject(new ApiError(409, 'LEGAL_FORM_REQUIRED'))
    else h.requests[0].resolve(response({ amount: 999999 }))
    await settle()
    h.render()
    assert.equal(h.card.props['aria-busy'], true)
    assert.equal(h.preparation.props.visible, true)
    assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, false)
    assert.equal(h.requests[1].options.signal.aborted, false)
    assert.equal(h.time.size, 2)
    assert.equal(h.invalidations.length, 0)
    h.requests[1].resolve(response({ amount: 300000 }))
    await settle()
    h.time.advance(2850)
    h.render()
    assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, true)
    assert.equal(h.field('amount').props.value, '300000')
    assert.equal(h.field('description').props.value, 'Новая бизнес-задача')
  }
})

test('unmount aborts transport and timers; hook-level mutation callbacks do not write afterward', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t)
  h.begin()
  await settle()
  const before = [...h.storage]
  h.unmount()
  assert.equal(h.requests[0].options.signal.aborted, true)
  assert.equal(h.time.size, 0)
  h.requests[0].resolve(response())
  await settle()
  h.time.advance(60000)
  assert.equal(h.runtimes[0].staleWrites, 0)
  assert.deepEqual([...h.storage], before)
  assert.equal(h.invalidations.length, 0)
})

test('the scope key isolates a new editor even if the old API ignores cancellation', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t)
  h.begin()
  await settle()
  h.changeScope()
  assert.equal(h.requests[0].options.signal.aborted, true)
  assert.equal(h.field('description').props.value, '')
  h.begin('Задача другого профиля')
  await settle()
  h.requests[0].resolve(response({ amount: 999999 }))
  await settle()
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.runtimes.length, 2)
  assert.equal(h.runtimes[0].staleWrites, 0)
  assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, false)
  assert.equal(h.card.props['aria-busy'], true)
  assert.equal(h.field('description').props.value, 'Задача другого профиля')
  assert.equal(h.field('amount').props.value, '')
  assert.equal(h.requests[1].options.signal.aborted, false)
})

test('session transition stops waiting before a scope remount without opening the fallback form', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t)
  h.begin()
  await settle()
  h.changeSession()
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.card.props['aria-busy'], false)
  assert.equal(h.requests[0].options.signal.aborted, true)
  assert.equal(h.time.size, 0)
  h.requests[0].reject(new ApiError(409, 'SESSION_CHANGED'))
  await settle()
  h.click(0)
  await settle()
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.requests.length, 1)
  assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, false)
  assert.equal(h.field('description').props.value, description)
  assert.ok(!h.html().includes(fallbackMessage))
})

test('generation is checked again before applying a response or a stale guard error', async (t) => {
  for (const fail of [false, true]) {
    const h = editor(t)
    h.begin()
    await settle()
    h.changeSession(false)
    if (fail) h.requests[0].reject(new ApiError(409, 'LEGAL_FORM_REQUIRED'))
    else h.requests[0].resolve(response())
    await settle()
    h.render()
    assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, false)
    assert.equal(h.card.props['aria-busy'], false)
    assert.equal(h.field('amount').props.value, '')
    assert.equal(h.invalidations.length, 0)
    assert.equal(h.time.size, 0)
  }
})

test('the 60 s deadline aborts and opens the same form even when transport never rejects', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t)
  h.begin()
  await settle()
  h.time.advance(59999)
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.card.props['aria-busy'], true)
  assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, false)
  h.time.advance(1)
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.requests[0].options.signal.aborted, true)
  assert.equal(h.requests[0].options.signal.reason.name, 'TimeoutError')
  assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, true)
  assert.ok(h.html().includes(fallbackMessage))
  assert.equal(h.field('description').props.value, description)
  h.change('amount', '800000')
  h.close()
  h.requests[0].resolve(response())
  await settle()
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, false)
  assert.equal(h.field('amount').props.value, '800000')
  assert.equal(h.time.size, 0)
  assert.equal(h.requests.length, 1)
})

test('network, server and invalid-response failures fall back without retrying or clearing description', async (t) => {
  for (const error of [new Error('Network failed'), new ApiError(500, 'SERVER_ERROR'), null]) {
    const h = editor(t)
    h.begin()
    await settle()
    const mutation = h.observers.find((observer) => observer.getCurrentResult().variables?.controller)
    assert.equal(mutation.options.retry, false)
    if (error) h.requests[0].reject(error)
    else h.requests[0].resolve({ invalid: true })
    await settle()
    h.render()
    assert.equal(h.card.props['aria-busy'], false)
    assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, true)
    assert.ok(h.html().includes(fallbackMessage))
    assert.equal(h.field('description').props.value, description)
    assert.equal(h.time.size, 0)
    assert.equal(h.requests.length, 1)
  }
})

test('legal form, session, validation and authorization guards retain their dedicated behavior', async (t) => {
  for (const [status, code] of [[409, 'LEGAL_FORM_REQUIRED'], [409, 'SESSION_CHANGED'], [400, 'BAD_REQUEST'], [401, 'UNAUTHORIZED'], [403, 'FORBIDDEN']]) {
    const h = editor(t)
    h.begin()
    await settle()
    h.requests[0].reject(new ApiError(status, code, `Guard: ${code}`))
    await settle()
    h.render()
    assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, false)
    assert.equal(h.card.props['aria-busy'], false)
    assert.equal(h.field('description').props.value, description)
    assert.ok(!h.html().includes(fallbackMessage))
    if (status === 409) assert.ok(!h.html().includes(`Guard: ${code}`))
    else assert.ok(h.html().includes(`Guard: ${code}`))
    assert.deepEqual(plain(h.invalidations), code === 'LEGAL_FORM_REQUIRED' ? [{ queryKey: ['session'] }] : [])
    assert.equal(h.time.size, 0)
  }
})

test('partial data preserves the source description, missing annotations and automatic field hints', async (t) => {
  for (const businessTask of [null, '']) {
    const h = editor(t)
    h.begin()
    await settle()
    h.requests[0].resolve(response({ businessTask, amount: null }, ['step1_financingParameters.amount']))
    await settle()
    h.time.advance(3000)
    h.render()
    assert.equal(h.modal.props.open, true)
    assert.equal(h.field('description').props.value, description)
    assert.equal(h.field('amount').props.error, 'Заполните поле')
    assert.equal(h.field('termDays').props.hint, 'Заполнено автоматически, проверьте')
    assert.ok(h.html().includes(partialMessage))
    assert.ok(!h.html().includes('Заполнено ИИ, проверьте'))
    h.next()
    h.render()
    assert.equal(h.requests.length, 1)
    assert.equal(h.field('amount').props.error, 'Минимальная сумма 50 000 ₽')
  }
})

test('preparation never saves a server draft; Continue preserves submission semantics and wire fields', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t)
  h.begin()
  await settle()
  const prepared = response()
  h.requests[0].resolve(prepared)
  await settle()
  h.time.advance(3000)
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.requests.length, 1)
  h.next()
  await settle()
  h.render()
  assert.equal(h.requests.length, 2)
  assert.equal(h.requests[1].url, '/api/applications/draft')
  assert.equal(h.requests[1].options.method, 'PUT')
  const saved = JSON.parse(h.requests[1].options.body)
  assert.equal(saved.currentStep, 1)
  assert.equal(saved.revision, null)
  assert.equal(saved.form.step1_financingParameters.businessTask, description.trim())
  assert.equal(saved.form.step1_financingParameters.termDays, 60)
  assert.deepEqual(saved.aiFilled, prepared.aiFilled)
  assert.deepEqual(saved.missingFields, prepared.missingFields)
  assert.deepEqual(saved.form.step3_verification.consents, prepared.form.step3_verification.consents)
  assert.equal(saved.form.step2_businessData.applicant.mainOkved, '47.11')
  const business = h.find((item) => item.type === IpApplicationFields)
  assert.equal(business.props.value.monthlyRevenue, '500000')
  assert.equal(business.props.value.existingDebt, '0')
  assert.equal(business.props.value.personalData, false)
  assert.equal(business.props.value.bkiRequest, false)
  assert.equal(business.props.value.fnsRequest, false)
})

test('preparation is a decorative, token-styled composition with reserved space and reduced motion', () => {
  const view = ApplicationPreparation({ visible: true, slow: false, headingTag: 'h2' })
  const visual = elements(view).find((item) => item.type === ApplicationPreparationVisual)
  assert.ok(visual)
  const graphic = ApplicationPreparationVisual()
  assert.equal(graphic.props['aria-hidden'], 'true')
  assert.equal(graphic.props['data-active'], true)
  assert.equal(elements(graphic).filter((item) => item.props.className === 'traveller').length, 3)
  const html = renderToStaticMarkup(view)
  assert.ok(html.includes('<h2>Готовим вашу заявку</h2>'))
  for (const label of ['Сумма', 'Срок', 'Цель']) assert.ok(html.includes(label))
  assert.ok(!html.includes('style='))
  const text = html.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '')
  assert.ok(text.includes('Обработка в среднем занимает 10 секунд.'))
  assert.doesNotMatch(
    text.replace('Обработка в среднем занимает 10 секунд.', ''),
    /\d|%|ИИ|БКИ|одобр/i,
  )
  const preparationCss = readFileSync(path.join(root, `${feature}/ui/ApplicationPreparation/ApplicationPreparation.module.scss`), 'utf8')
  const formCss = readFileSync(path.join(root, `${feature}/ui/ApplicationForm/ApplicationForm.module.scss`), 'utf8')
  assert.match(preparationCss, /prefers-reduced-motion: reduce[\s\S]*animation: none/)
  assert.match(preparationCss, /\.slow\[data-visible='false'\][\s\S]*?visibility: hidden/)
  assert.match(preparationCss, /@media \(max-width:[\s\S]*grid-template-columns: minmax\(0, 1fr\)/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(preparationCss, /!important|#[\da-f]{3,8}\b|rgba?\(/i)
  assert.match(formCss, /\.startContent > \*\s*\{\s*grid-area: 1 \/ 1/)
  assert.match(formCss, /\.intent\[data-hidden='true'\]\s*\{\s*visibility: hidden/)
  assert.match(preparationCss, /--cycle: 2\.4s/)
  assert.match(preparationCss, /--row-delay: 0\.2s/)
  assert.match(preparationCss, /--row-delay: 0\.4s/)
  assert.match(preparationCss, /\[data-active='true'\] \.traveller\s*\{\s*animation: transfer-line/)
  assert.match(preparationCss, /transform: translate\(var\(--flight-end\), var\(--flight-drop\)\)/)
  assert.match(preparationCss, /\.explanation > p\s*\{\s*grid-area: 1 \/ 1/)
  assert.match(preparationCss, /\.flights\s*\{\s*position: absolute;[^}]*overflow: clip/)
})

test('the initial screen has no illustration and keeps the actual term restriction', (t) => {
  const h = editor(t)
  const motif = h.find((item) => item.type === ApplicationPreparationVisual)
  assert.equal(motif, undefined)
  assert.ok(!h.html().includes('class="illustration"'))
  assert.equal(h.buttons[0].props.children, 'Подготовить заявку')
  assert.ok(h.find((item) => item.type === 'header' && item.props.className === 'intentHeader'))
  assert.ok(h.html().includes('Срок транша: только 30, 60 или 90 дней.'))
  assert.ok(h.html().includes('Это не срок всего договора.'))
  assert.equal(h.field('description').props['aria-label'], 'Ваша бизнес-задача')
  assert.match(h.field('description').props['aria-describedby'], /intent-help .*intent-terms/)
  const inactive = ApplicationPreparation({ visible: false, slow: false, headingTag: 'h2' })
  const hiddenMotif = elements(inactive).find((item) => item.type === ApplicationPreparationVisual)
  assert.equal(hiddenMotif, undefined)
})

    react: React, '@alfalab/core-components/input': { Input }, '@alfalab/core-components/select': { Select },
    '@alfalab/core-components/button': { Button }, '@alfalab/core-components/checkbox': { Checkbox: () => null },

  })
  const html = renderToStaticMarkup(React.createElement(Fields, {
    section: 'business', onChange() {},
    value: { companyName: 'ИП Тестовый Предприниматель', inn: '123456789012', legalForm: 'ИП', monthlyRevenue: '500000', existingDebt: '0', taxSystem: 'USN' },
    aiFilled: new Set(['step2_businessData.financials.monthlyRevenue', 'step2_businessData.applicant.taxSystem']),
  }))
  assert.equal((html.match(/Заполнено автоматически, проверьте/g) || []).length, 2)

})

test('external data hints expose the current demo values', () => {
  const hints = []
  const { ExternalDataFields: Fields } = load(`${feature}/ui/ExternalDataFields/ExternalDataFields.tsx`, {
    react: React,
    '@alfalab/core-components/input': { Input },
    '@alfalab/core-components/select': { Select },
    '@/entities/application-form': formEntity,
    '@/shared/ui': {
      FieldLabel: (label) => {
        hints.push(label)
        return React.createElement('span', null, label.text)
      },
    },
    './ExternalDataFields.module.scss': cssModule,
  })
  const hintOf = (text) => hints.find((hint) => hint.text === text)?.hint
  const demo = {
    bkiScore: 680,
    delinquencies12m: 1,
    inquiries12m: 4,
    revenueTrend: 'DECLINING',
    companyAgeYears: 9,
  }
  renderToStaticMarkup(
    React.createElement(Fields, { value: {}, onChange() {}, demo }),
  )
  assert.match(hintOf('БКИ-скор'), /Сейчас: 680\./)
  assert.match(hintOf('Просрочки за 12 месяцев'), /Сейчас: 1\./)
  assert.match(hintOf('Запросы в БКИ за 12 месяцев'), /Сейчас: 4\./)
  assert.match(hintOf('Возраст бизнеса для скоринга, лет'), /Сейчас: 9\./)
  assert.match(hintOf('Динамика выручки'), /Сейчас: снижается\./)

  hints.length = 0
  renderToStaticMarkup(React.createElement(Fields, { value: {}, onChange() {} }))
  assert.ok(hints.every((hint) => !String(hint.hint).includes('Сейчас:')))
})

test('phone validation accepts only Russian +7 numbers for IP and OOO', () => {
  const schemas = [
    formEntity.applicationApplicantSubmissionSchema.shape.phone,
    formEntity.oooApplicantSubmissionSchema.shape.phone,
  ]
  for (const schema of schemas) {
    for (const phone of ['+79990000000', '+7 (999) 111-00-01', '+7 999 111 00 01']) {
      assert.equal(schema.safeParse(phone).success, true, phone)
    }
    for (const phone of ['89990000000', '+7999000000', '+1 999 111 0001', 'телефон']) {
      const result = schema.safeParse(phone)
      assert.equal(result.success, false, phone)
      assert.match(result.error.issues[0].message, /\+7/)
    }
  }
})

test('bank form summary keeps manual external overrides for transparent scoring', () => {
  const externalData = {
    bkiScore: 810,
    delinquencies12m: 0,
    inquiries12m: 2,
    revenueTrend: 'GROWING',
    companyAgeYears: 12,
  }
  const parsed = formEntity.applicationBusinessSummarySchema.parse({
    step2_businessData: { externalData },
  })
  assert.deepEqual(plain(parsed.step2_businessData.externalData), externalData)
})

test('external field validation runs on blur and blocks advancing the step', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t, { localDraft: readyLocalDraft({}, 1) })
  h.resume()
  for (const [field, value, message] of [
    ['bkiScore', '1001', 'Укажите целое число от 0 до 1 000'],
    ['delinquencies12m', '25', 'Укажите целое число от 0 до 24'],
    ['inquiries12m', '31', 'Укажите целое число от 0 до 30'],
    ['companyAgeYears', '101', 'Укажите целое число от 0 до 100'],
  ]) {
    h.editExternal({ [field]: value })
    h.blurExternal(field)
    assert.equal(h.external.props.errors[field], message, field)
  }
  h.next()
  await settle()
  assert.equal(h.requests.length, 0)
  assert.equal(h.external.props.errors.bkiScore, 'Укажите целое число от 0 до 1 000')

  for (const field of ['bkiScore', 'delinquencies12m', 'inquiries12m', 'companyAgeYears']) {
    h.editExternal({ [field]: '1' })
    h.blurExternal(field)
    assert.equal(h.external.props.errors[field], undefined, field)
  }
})

test('external scoring overrides are persisted in the draft and submitted', async (t) => {
  const overrides = {
    bkiScore: '810',
    delinquencies12m: '0',
    inquiries12m: '2',
    revenueTrend: 'GROWING',
    companyAgeYears: '12',
  }
  const expected = {
    bkiScore: 810,
    delinquencies12m: 0,
    inquiries12m: 2,
    revenueTrend: 'GROWING',
    companyAgeYears: 12,
  }
  const h = editor(t, { localDraft: readyLocalDraft({}, 1) })
  h.resume()
  assert.deepEqual(plain(h.external.props.demo), {
    bkiScore: 680,
    delinquencies12m: 1,
    inquiries12m: 4,
    revenueTrend: 'DECLINING',
    companyAgeYears: 9,
  })
  h.editExternal(overrides)
  h.next()
  assert.deepEqual(
    (await h.resolveDraft()).form.step2_businessData.externalData,
    expected,
  )
  h.next()
  const reviewed = await h.resolveDraft()
  assert.equal(reviewed.currentStep, 3)
  assert.deepEqual(reviewed.form.step2_businessData.externalData, expected)
  h.next()
  await settle()
  assert.equal(h.requests.at(-1).url, '/api/applications')
  assert.deepEqual(
    JSON.parse(h.requests.at(-1).options.body).step2_businessData.externalData,
    expected,
  )
})

function readyLocalDraft(ipEdits = {}, currentStep = 3, externalEdits = {}) {
  return {
    description, amount: '200000', termDays: '60', purpose: 'Закупка товара',
    currentStep, started: true, revision: null, serverForm: null,
    acceptedAt: '2026-10-01T12:00:00Z', externalEdits,
    ipEdits: {
      companyName: 'ИП Тестовый Предприниматель', inn: '123456789012', legalForm: 'ИП',
      taxSystem: 'USN', monthlyRevenue: '500000', existingDebt: '0',
      personalData: true, bkiRequest: true, fnsRequest: true,
      fullName: 'Тестовый Предприниматель', ogrnip: '123456789012345', registrationDate: '',
      okved: '47.11', industry: 'trade', registrationAddress: 'Тестовый адрес',
      phone: '+79990000000', email: 'test@example.com', ...ipEdits,
    },
  }
}

function renderIpFields(section, props = {}) {
  const inputs = []
  const selects = []
  const fieldLabels = []
  const { IpApplicationFields: Fields } = load(`${feature}/ui/IpApplicationFields/IpApplicationFields.tsx`, {
    react: React,
    '@alfalab/core-components/input': { Input: (input) => { inputs.push(input); return React.createElement(Input, input) } },
    '@alfalab/core-components/select': { Select: (select) => { selects.push(select); return React.createElement(Select, select) } },
    '@alfalab/core-components/button': { Button }, '@alfalab/core-components/checkbox': { Checkbox: () => null },
    '@/entities/company': { ...companyEntity, industryGroups: [] },
    '@/shared/ui': { FieldLabel: (label) => { fieldLabels.push(label); return React.createElement('label', { htmlFor: label.htmlFor }, label.text) } },
    './IpApplicationFields.module.scss': cssModule,
  })
  const html = renderToStaticMarkup(React.createElement(Fields, {
    section, value: readyLocalDraft().ipEdits, onChange() {}, ...props,
  }))
  return { html, inputs, selects, fieldLabels }
}

test('registration date is absent from business, registration and review field rendering', () => {
  const value = readyLocalDraft({ registrationDate: '2014-05-06' }).ipEdits
  for (const section of ['business', 'registration']) {
    const rendered = renderIpFields(section, {
      value, profileMatches: true, onConfirm() {},
      missingFields: new Set(['step2_businessData.applicant.registrationDate']),
      errors: { registrationDate: 'Укажите дату' },
    })
    assert.ok(!rendered.inputs.some((input) => input.name === 'registrationDate'))
    assert.ok(!rendered.html.includes('Дата регистрации ИП'))
    assert.ok(!rendered.html.includes('2014-05-06'))
    assert.ok(!rendered.html.includes('Укажите дату'))
    if (section === 'registration') {
      assert.ok(rendered.inputs.some((input) => input.name === 'ogrnip'))
      assert.ok(rendered.inputs.some((input) => input.name === 'okved'))
      assert.ok(rendered.selects.some((select) => select.name === 'industry'))
    }
  }
})

test('legal form and tax system share label rows and an explanatory tax hint', () => {
  const rendered = renderIpFields('business')
  const legal = rendered.inputs.find((input) => input.name === 'legalForm')
  const tax = rendered.selects.find((select) => select.name === 'taxSystem')
  const legalLabel = rendered.fieldLabels.find((label) => label.text === 'Форма бизнеса')
  const taxLabel = rendered.fieldLabels.find((label) => label.text === 'Система налогообложения')
  assert.ok(legalLabel)
  assert.ok(taxLabel)
  assert.equal(legal.label, undefined)
  assert.equal(tax.label, undefined)
  assert.equal(tax.size, legal.size)
  assert.equal(taxLabel.htmlFor, `${tax.id}-input`)
  assert.ok(rendered.html.includes(`id="${tax.id}-label"`))
  assert.match(taxLabel.hint, /Режим, по которому ИП платит налоги/)
  assert.match(taxLabel.hint, /УСН или ОСНО/)
  assert.match(taxLabel.hint, /личном кабинете ИП на сайте ФНС/)
  assert.equal(tax.fieldProps['aria-required'], true)
  assert.deepEqual(plain(tax.options.map((option) => option.key)), ['OSNO', 'USN', 'PSN', 'AUSN', 'NPD'])
  const css = readFileSync(path.join(root, `${feature}/ui/IpApplicationFields/IpApplicationFields.module.scss`), 'utf8')
  assert.match(css, /\.grid\s*\{[^}]*align-items: start/)
  assert.match(css, /\.grid > \.pairedField\s*\{[^}]*grid-template-rows: subgrid/)
})

test('a prefill without registry date can reach review and submit without editing a hidden field', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t)
  h.begin()
  await settle()
  const prepared = response()
  prepared.form.step2_businessData.applicant.registrationDate = null
  h.requests[0].resolve(prepared)
  await settle()
  h.time.advance(3000)
  h.render()
  h.next()
  assert.equal((await h.resolveDraft()).form.step2_businessData.applicant.registrationDate, null)
  h.editIp({ personalData: true, bkiRequest: true, fnsRequest: true })
  h.next()

  h.next()

  assert.equal(reviewed.form.step2_businessData.applicant.registrationDate, null)

  const submitted = JSON.parse(h.requests.at(-1).options.body)
  assert.equal(submitted.step2_businessData.applicant.registrationDate, null)
  assert.equal(submitted.step2_businessData.applicant.ogrnip, '123456789012345')

})

  assert.equal(JSON.parse(h.requests.at(-1).options.body).step2_businessData.applicant.registrationDate, '2016-04-12')

})

test('legacy missing, invalid and future dates cannot trap a resumed draft', async (t) => {
  for (const [value, expected] of [['', null], ['31.02.2020', null], ['not a date', null], ['2999-01-01', null], ['29.02.2020', '2020-02-29'], ['2011-03-04', '2011-03-04']]) {
    for (const step of [0, 3]) {
      const h = editor(t, { localDraft: readyLocalDraft({ registrationDate: value }, step) })
      h.resume()
      h.next()
      await settle()
      assert.equal(h.requests.length, 1, `Hidden date blocked step ${step}: ${value}`)
      const request = h.requests[0]
      assert.equal(request.url, step === 0 ? '/api/applications/draft' : '/api/applications')
      const body = JSON.parse(request.options.body)
      const form = step === 0 ? body.form : body
      assert.equal(form.step2_businessData.applicant.registrationDate, expected)
    }
  }
})

test('session defaults fill only unused contacts and never overwrite typed values or explicit clears', (t) => {
  const local = readyLocalDraft({ phone: '' })
  delete local.ipEdits.email
  const h = editor(t, { localDraft: local, session: { phone: '+79991110001', email: 'profile@example.com' } })
  h.resume()
  assert.equal(h.ip.props.value.phone, '')
  assert.equal(h.ip.props.value.email, 'profile@example.com')
  h.refreshSession({ phone: '+79991110002', email: 'updated@example.com' })
  assert.equal(h.ip.props.value.phone, '')
  assert.equal(h.ip.props.value.email, 'updated@example.com')
  h.editIp({ phone: '+79992223344', email: '' }, 'registration')
  h.refreshSession({ phone: null, email: null })
  assert.equal(h.ip.props.value.phone, '+79992223344')
  assert.equal(h.ip.props.value.email, '')
  h.refreshSession({ phone: '+79991110003', email: 'refetched@example.com' })
  assert.equal(h.ip.props.value.phone, '+79992223344')
  assert.equal(h.ip.props.value.email, '')
})

test('local and server drafts, including null contacts, take precedence over session defaults', (t) => {
  for (const source of ['local', 'server', 'local-server-form']) {
    const form = response().form
    form.step2_businessData.applicant.phone = null
    form.step2_businessData.applicant.email = 'draft@example.com'
    const local = readyLocalDraft({ phone: '', email: 'draft@example.com' })
    if (source === 'local-server-form') {
      local.serverForm = form
      delete local.ipEdits.phone
      delete local.ipEdits.email
    }
    const h = editor(t, {
      session: { phone: '+79991110001', email: 'profile@example.com' },
      ...(source === 'server'
        ? { initialDraft: { form, currentStep: 3, aiFilled: [], missingFields: [], revision: 1, updatedAt: '2026-10-23T12:00:00Z' } }
        : { localDraft: local }),
    })
    h.resume()
    h.refreshSession({ phone: '+79991110002', email: null })
    assert.equal(h.ip.props.value.phone, '', source)
    assert.equal(h.ip.props.value.email, 'draft@example.com', source)
  }
})

test('prefill contacts and their automatic or missing annotations override unused session defaults exactly', async (t) => {
  for (const contacts of [
    { phone: '+7 (999) 222-33-44', email: 'prepared@example.com' },
    { phone: null, email: 'prepared@example.com' },
    { phone: '+79992223344', email: null },
    { phone: null, email: null },
  ]) {
    const h = editor(t, { session: { phone: '+79991110001', email: 'session@example.com' } })
    const prepared = response()
    Object.assign(prepared.form.step2_businessData.applicant, contacts)
    for (const field of ['phone', 'email']) {
      prepared[contacts[field] === null ? 'missingFields' : 'aiFilled'].push(`step2_businessData.applicant.${field}`)
    }
    h.begin()
    await settle()
    h.requests[0].resolve(prepared)
    await settle()
    h.time.advance(3000)
    h.render()
    h.next()
    await h.resolveDraft()
    h.refreshSession({ phone: '+79991110002', email: 'refresh@example.com' })
    const rendered = renderIpFields('registration', { ...h.ip.props, section: 'registration' })
    for (const field of ['phone', 'email']) {
      assert.equal(h.ip.props.value[field], contacts[field] ?? '')
      const input = rendered.inputs.find((item) => item.name === field)
      assert.equal(input.required, true)
      assert.equal(input.error, contacts[field] === null ? 'Заполните поле' : undefined)
    }
    assert.deepEqual([...h.ip.props.aiFilled], prepared.aiFilled)
    assert.deepEqual([...h.ip.props.missingFields], prepared.missingFields)
    assert.equal((rendered.html.match(/Заполнено автоматически, проверьте/g) || []).length, Object.values(contacts).filter(Boolean).length)
    assert.ok(!rendered.html.includes('Заполнено ИИ'))
  }
})

function submittedApplication() {
  return {
    id: 51, amount: 200000, termDays: 60, rate: 24.75, purpose: 'Закупка товара',
    businessTask: description.trim(), companyName: 'ИП Тестовый Предприниматель', inn: '123456789012', legalForm: 'IP',
    status: 'IN_REVIEW', decision: null, finalRate: null, offer: null, recommendation: null,
    internalScore: 85, riskClass: 'A', aiComment: null, officerComment: null, scoring: null,
    createdAt: '2026-10-23T12:00:00Z',
  }
}

function observeContacts(t, h) {
  const session = h.cache.getQueryData(h.sessionEntity.sessionKey)
  const profile = { userId: session.userId, fullName: session.fullName, phone: session.phone, email: session.email }
  h.cache.setQueryData(profileEntity.profileKey(1), profile)
  for (const [queryKey, url, schema] of [
    [h.sessionEntity.sessionKey, '/api/auth/me', h.sessionEntity.sessionSchema],
    [profileEntity.profileKey(1), '/api/profile', profileEntity.profileSchema],
  ]) {
    const observer = new QueryObserver(h.cache, {
      queryKey, staleTime: Infinity, gcTime: Infinity, retry: false,
      queryFn: ({ signal }) => h.api.apiRequest(url, schema, { signal }),
    })
    t.after(observer.subscribe(() => {}))
  }
  return { session, profile }
}

test('initial POST stays IN_REVIEW with no decision or offer and refetches authoritative contacts instead of copying the form', async (t) => {
  for (const existing of [
    { phone: null, email: null },
    { phone: '+79991110001', email: 'existing@example.com' },
    { phone: null, email: 'existing@example.com' },
  ]) {
    const local = readyLocalDraft()
    const h = editor(t, { localDraft: local, session: existing })
    const before = observeContacts(t, h)
    h.resume()
    h.next()
    await settle()
    const request = h.requests[0]
    assert.equal(request.url, '/api/applications')
    assert.equal(request.options.method, 'POST')
    request.resolve(submittedApplication())
    await settle()
    assert.deepEqual(plain(h.cache.getQueryData(h.sessionEntity.sessionKey)), before.session)
    assert.deepEqual(plain(h.cache.getQueryData(profileEntity.profileKey(1))), before.profile)
    assert.equal(h.navigations.length, 0)
    assert.equal(h.cache.getQueryData(profileEntity.profileKey(1)).phone, existing.phone)
    const contacts = {
      phone: existing.phone ?? local.ipEdits.phone,
      email: existing.email ?? local.ipEdits.email,
    }
    const me = h.requests.find((item) => item.url === '/api/auth/me')
    const profile = h.requests.find((item) => item.url === '/api/profile')
    assert.ok(me)
    assert.ok(profile)
    me.resolve({ ...before.session, ...contacts })
    profile.resolve({ ...before.profile, ...contacts })
    await settle()
    h.render()
    const result = h.observers.find((observer) => observer.getCurrentResult().variables?.body).getCurrentResult()
    assert.equal(result.isSuccess, true)
    assert.equal(result.data.status, 'IN_REVIEW')
    for (const field of ['decision', 'finalRate', 'offer', 'recommendation']) assert.equal(result.data[field], null)
    for (const field of ['phone', 'email']) {
      assert.equal(h.cache.getQueryData(h.sessionEntity.sessionKey)[field], contacts[field])
      assert.equal(h.cache.getQueryData(profileEntity.profileKey(1))[field], contacts[field])
    }
    assert.equal(h.cache.getQueryData(['client', 1, 'application-draft']), null)
    assert.equal(h.storage.has('application-draft:1'), false)
    assert.deepEqual(plain(h.navigations), [['/applications', { replace: true }]])
    assert.deepEqual(plain(h.invalidations), [{ queryKey: ['client', 1] }, { queryKey: ['session'], exact: true }])
  }
})

test('successful submission cancels older session and profile reads before refreshing contacts', async (t) => {
  const h = editor(t, { localDraft: readyLocalDraft() })
  const before = observeContacts(t, h)
  void h.cache.refetchQueries()
  await settle()
  const oldReads = [...h.requests]
  assert.equal(oldReads.length, 2)
  h.resume()
  h.next()
  await settle()
  h.requests.find((item) => item.url === '/api/applications').resolve(submittedApplication())
  await settle()
  assert.ok(oldReads.every((request) => request.options.signal.aborted))
  const contacts = { phone: '+79990000000', email: 'test@example.com' }
  for (const request of h.requests.filter((item) => !oldReads.includes(item) && item.options.method !== 'POST')) {
    request.resolve({ ...(request.url === '/api/auth/me' ? before.session : before.profile), ...contacts })
  }
  await settle()
  for (const request of oldReads) request.resolve(request.url === '/api/auth/me' ? before.session : before.profile)
  await settle()
  assert.equal(h.cache.getQueryData(h.sessionEntity.sessionKey).phone, contacts.phone)
  assert.equal(h.cache.getQueryData(profileEntity.profileKey(1)).email, contacts.email)
})

test('obsolete submit callbacks cannot reset a draft, refresh a new session or navigate', async (t) => {
  for (const moment of ['before-request', 'response', 'after-cancel']) {
    const h = editor(t, { localDraft: readyLocalDraft() })
    h.resume()
    const before = [...h.storage]
    if (moment === 'after-cancel') {
      const cancel = h.cache.cancelQueries.bind(h.cache)
      h.cache.cancelQueries = async (...args) => { await cancel(...args); h.changeSession(false) }
    }
    h.next()
    if (moment === 'before-request') h.changeSession(false)
    await settle()
    if (moment === 'before-request') assert.equal(h.requests.length, 0)
    else {
      if (moment === 'response') h.changeSession(false)
      h.requests[0].resolve(submittedApplication())
      await settle()
    }
    assert.deepEqual([...h.storage], before)
    assert.equal(h.invalidations.length, 0)
    assert.equal(h.navigations.length, 0)
    assert.equal(h.cache.getQueryData(['client', 1, 'application-draft']), undefined)
  }
})

=== ./src/features/submit-loan-application/model/local-draft.ts ===
import { z } from 'zod'
import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react'
import {
  applicationApplicantSubmissionSchema,
  applicationDraftPathsSchema,
  applicationFormSchema,
  type ApplicationDraft,
} from '@/entities/application-form'

const draftSchema = z.object({
  description: z.string(),
  amount: z.string(),
  termDays: z.string(),
  purpose: z.string(),
  ipEdits: z
    .object({
      companyName: z.string(),
      inn: z.string(),
      legalForm: z.string(),
      taxSystem: z.string(),
      monthlyRevenue: z.string(),
      existingDebt: z.string(),
      personalData: z.boolean(),
      bkiRequest: z.boolean(),
      fnsRequest: z.boolean(),
      fullName: z.string(),
      ogrnip: z.string(),
      registrationDate: z.string(),
      okved: z.string(),
      registrationAddress: z.string(),
      phone: z.string(),
      email: z.string(),
      industry: z.string(),
    })
    .partial()
    .default({}),
  oooEdits: z
    .object({
      companyName: z.string(),
      inn: z.string(),
      taxSystem: z.string(),
      monthlyRevenue: z.string(),
      annualRevenue: z.string(),
      averageEmployeeCount: z.string(),
      existingDebt: z.string(),
      otherMonthlyObligations: z.string(),
      personalData: z.boolean(),
      bkiRequest: z.boolean(),
      fnsRequest: z.boolean(),
      ogrn: z.string(),
      kpp: z.string(),
      registrationDate: z.string(),
      okved: z.string(),
      registrationAddress: z.string(),
      phone: z.string(),
      email: z.string(),
      industry: z.string(),
    })
    .partial()
    .default({}),
  externalEdits: z
    .object({
      bkiScore: z.string(),
      delinquencies12m: z.string(),
      inquiries12m: z.string(),
      revenueTrend: z.string(),
      companyAgeYears: z.string(),
    })
    .partial()
    .default({}),
  currentStep: z.number().int().min(0).max(4).default(0),
  wizardVersion: z.literal(2).optional(),
  started: z.boolean().default(false),
  serverForm: applicationFormSchema.nullable().default(null),
  confirmed: z.boolean().default(false),
  needsDebtReview: z.boolean().default(false),
  acceptedAt: z.iso.datetime({ offset: true }).nullable().default(null),
  createdAt: z.iso
    .datetime({ offset: true })
    .default(() => new Date().toISOString()),
  aiFilled: applicationDraftPathsSchema.default([]),
  missingFields: applicationDraftPathsSchema.default([]),
  revision: z.number().int().nonnegative().nullable().default(null),
  updatedAt: z.iso
    .datetime({ offset: true })
    .default(() => new Date().toISOString()),
})

export const localDraftSchema = z.union([
  draftSchema,
  draftSchema
    .omit({ termDays: true, serverForm: true })
    .extend({ termMonths: z.string(), serverForm: z.unknown().optional() })
    .transform((draft) =>
      draftSchema.parse({
        ...draft,
        termDays: '',
        ipEdits: { ...draft.ipEdits, existingDebt: '' },
        oooEdits: { ...draft.oooEdits, existingDebt: '' },
        serverForm: null,
        currentStep: 0,
        needsDebtReview: true,
        missingFields: [
          ...draft.missingFields,
          'step1_financingParameters.termDays',
          'step2_businessData.financials.existingDebt',
        ],
      }),
    ),
]).transform((draft) => ({
  ...draft,
  currentStep:
    draft.wizardVersion !== 2 &&
    draft.serverForm?.step2_businessData.applicant.type === 'OOO' &&
    draft.currentStep >= 3
      ? draft.currentStep - 1
      : Math.min(draft.currentStep, 3),
  wizardVersion: 2 as const,
}))

export function loadLocalDraft(key: string) {
  try {
    const result = localDraftSchema.safeParse(
      JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(key) ?? 'null'),
    )
    return result.success ? result.data : null
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

function subscribe(callback: () => void) {
  window.addEventListener('application-draft-changed', callback)
  window.addEventListener('storage', callback)
  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener('application-draft-changed', callback)
    window.removeEventListener('storage', callback)
  }
}

export function useLocalDraft(key: string) {
  const value = useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribe,
    () => {
      try {
        return sessionStorage.getItem(key)
      } catch {
        return null
      }
    },
    () => null,
  )
  if (!value) return null
  try {
    const result = localDraftSchema.safeParse(JSON.parse(value))
    return result.success ? result.data : null
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

export function numberOrNull(value: string) {
  if (!value.trim()) return null
  const number = Number(value.replace(/\s/g, '').replace(',', '.'))
  return Number.isFinite(number) ? number : null
}

/** Corporate UI steps map to the three stages accepted by the draft API. */
export function getServerDraftStep(step: number, legalForm: string | null) {
  return legalForm === 'OOO' ? (step === 0 ? 0 : step < 3 ? 1 : 2) : step
}

/** Restore the UI step from supported form metadata, including on another device. */
export function getApplicationDraftStep(draft: ApplicationDraft | null) {
  if (!draft) return 0
  if (draft.form.step2_businessData.applicant.type !== 'OOO')
    return draft.currentStep
  const events = draft.form.meta?.events ?? []
  const event = events.find((item) => /^ooo_step_v2_[0-3]$/.test(item))
  const step = event ? Number(event.slice(-1)) : null
  if (step !== null && getServerDraftStep(step, 'OOO') === draft.currentStep)
    return step
  const legacyEvent = events.find((item) => /^ooo_step_[0-4]$/.test(item))
  if (legacyEvent) {
    const legacyStep = Number(legacyEvent.slice(-1))
    if (getServerDraftStep(legacyStep, 'OOO') === draft.currentStep)
      return legacyStep >= 3 ? legacyStep - 1 : legacyStep
  }
  return draft.currentStep === 0 ? 0 : 1
}

/** Preserve valid stored dates; missing or invalid legacy input stays absent. */
export function registrationDateValue(value: string) {
  const text = value.trim()
  if (!text) return null
  const match = /^(\d{2})\.(\d{2})\.(\d{4})$/.exec(text)
  const result =
    applicationApplicantSubmissionSchema.shape.registrationDate.safeParse(
      match ? `${match[3]}-${match[2]}-${match[1]}` : text,
    )
  return result.success ? result.data : null
}

=== ./src/features/submit-loan-application/model/use-application-prefill.ts ===
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { useMutation } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import {
  prefillResponseSchema,
  type PrefillResponse,
} from '@/entities/application-form'
import {
  apiRequest,
  getSessionGeneration,
  isSessionSwitching,
  sessionEvents,
} from '@/shared/api'

type PreparationStage = 'idle' | 'preparing' | 'slow'

interface PrefillAttempt {
  text: string
  startedAt: number
  generation: number
  controller: AbortController
  timers: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>[]
}

/** Callbacks for the current editor's preparation attempt only. */
export interface ApplicationPrefillOptions {
  onSuccess: (response: PrefillResponse, description: string) => void
  onError: (error: Error) => void
}

/** Own preparation timing and cancellation for the scope-keyed form editor. */
export function useApplicationPrefill({
  onSuccess,
  onError,
}: ApplicationPrefillOptions) {
  const [stage, setStage] = useState<PreparationStage>('idle')
  const active = useRef<PrefillAttempt | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    const interrupt = () => {
      const attempt = active.current
      active.current = null
      if (attempt) {
        attempt.timers.forEach(clearTimeout)
        attempt.controller.abort()
      }
    }
    const sessionChanged = () => {
      if (
        active.current &&
        active.current.generation !== getSessionGeneration()
      ) {
        interrupt()
        setStage('idle')
      }
    }
    sessionEvents.addEventListener('change', sessionChanged)
    return () => {
      sessionEvents.removeEventListener('change', sessionChanged)
      interrupt()
    }
  }, [])

  function isCurrent(attempt: PrefillAttempt) {
    return (
      active.current === attempt &&
      attempt.generation === getSessionGeneration() &&
      !attempt.controller.signal.aborted
    )
  }

  function finish(attempt: PrefillAttempt) {
    const current = isCurrent(attempt)
    if (active.current === attempt) {
      active.current = null
      attempt.timers.forEach(clearTimeout)
      setStage('idle')
    }
    return current
  }

  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (attempt: PrefillAttempt) => {
      if (!isCurrent(attempt)) attempt.controller.abort()
      attempt.controller.signal.throwIfAborted()
      return apiRequest('/api/ai/prefill', prefillResponseSchema, {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({ text: attempt.text.trim() }),
        signal: attempt.controller.signal,
      })
    },
    retry: false,
    onSuccess: (response, attempt) => {
      const apply = () => {
        if (finish(attempt)) onSuccess(response, attempt.text)
      }
      const remaining = 3_000 - (performance.now() - attempt.startedAt)
      if (isCurrent(attempt) && remaining > 0) {
        attempt.timers.push(setTimeout(apply, remaining))
      } else {
        apply()
      }
    },
    onError: (error, attempt) => {
      if (finish(attempt)) onError(error)
    },
  })

  function start(text: string) {
    if (active.current || isSessionSwitching()) return
    const attempt: PrefillAttempt = {
      text,
      startedAt: performance.now(),
      generation: getSessionGeneration(),
      controller: new AbortController(),
      timers: [],
    }
    active.current = attempt
    setStage('preparing')
    attempt.timers.push(
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (isCurrent(attempt)) setStage('slow')
      }, 8_000),
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (!finish(attempt)) return
        const error = new DOMException('Prefill timed out', 'TimeoutError')
        attempt.controller.abort(error)
        onError(error)
      }, 60_000),
    )
    mutation.mutate(attempt)
  }

  function cancel() {
    const attempt = active.current
    if (!attempt) return
    finish(attempt)
    attempt.controller.abort()
  }

  return { stage, isPending: stage !== 'idle', start, cancel }
}

=== ./src/features/send-ai-message/index.ts ===
export { useAssistantChat, maxAssistantQuestionLength } from './model/use-assistant-chat'
export { useAssistantScreen, readAssistantScreen } from './model/screen-context'

=== ./src/features/send-ai-message/model/use-assistant-chat.ts ===
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { useMutation } from '@tanstack/react-query'

import {
  assistantAnswerSchema,
  type AssistantAnswer,
  type AssistantMessage,
  type AssistantRequest,
  type AssistantScreen,
} from '@/entities/ai-message'
import { apiRequest } from '@/shared/api'

export const maxAssistantQuestionLength = 1000

/** A conversation stays in memory and is discarded with the authenticated layout. */
export interface AssistantTurn {
  id: number
  question: string
  contextTitle: string
  answer?: AssistantAnswer
  error?: string
}

export function useAssistantChat() {
  const [turns, setTurns] = useState<AssistantTurn[]>([])
  const request = useRef<AbortController | null>(null)
  const nextId = useRef(0)
  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ body, signal }: { body: AssistantRequest; signal: AbortSignal }) =>
      apiRequest('/api/assistant/messages', assistantAnswerSchema, {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify(body),
        signal,
      }),
    retry: false,
  })

  useEffect(() => () => request.current?.abort(), [])

  async function send(value: string, screen: AssistantScreen, retryTurn?: AssistantTurn) {
    const message = value.trim().slice(0, maxAssistantQuestionLength)
    if (!message || request.current) return false

    const controller = new AbortController()
    request.current = controller
    const turn: AssistantTurn = {
      id: retryTurn?.id ?? nextId.current++,
      question: message,
      contextTitle: screen.step ?? screen.title,
    }
    let history: AssistantMessage[] = turns
      .filter((item) => item.answer && item.id !== turn.id)
      .slice(-6)
      .flatMap((item) => [
        { role: 'user' as const, content: item.question },
        { role: 'assistant' as const, content: item.answer!.answer },
      ])
    while (history.reduce((length, item) => length + item.content.length, 0) > 20_000) {
      history = history.slice(2)
    }
    setTurns((previous) => retryTurn
      ? previous.map((item) => item.id === turn.id ? turn : item)
      : [...previous, turn].slice(-20))

    try {
      const answer = await mutation.mutateAsync({ body: { message, history, screen }, signal: controller.signal })
      if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
        const focusFieldId = screen.fields.some((field) => field.id === answer.focusFieldId)
          ? answer.focusFieldId : null
        setTurns((previous) => previous.map((item) => item.id === turn.id
          ? { ...item, answer: { ...answer, focusFieldId } } : item))
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
        setTurns((previous) => previous.map((item) => item.id === turn.id
          ? { ...item, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Не удалось получить ответ помощника.' }
          : item))
      }
    } finally {
      if (request.current === controller) request.current = null
    }
    return true
  }

  function clear() {
    request.current?.abort()
    request.current = null
    mutation.reset()
    setTurns([])
  }

  return {
    turns,
    send,
    clear,
    pending: mutation.isPending,
    error: mutation.error,
  }
}

=== ./src/features/send-ai-message/model/screen-context.ts ===
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'

import type { AssistantScreen } from '@/entities/ai-message'

const assistantSelector = '[data-ai-assistant]'
const privateSelector = '[data-assistant-private]'
const fieldSelector = 'input:not([type="hidden"]), textarea, select, [role="combobox"]'
const contextFields = new Set([
  'okved', 'legalForm', 'amount', 'reviewAmount', 'termDays', 'reviewTermDays',
  'purpose', 'reviewPurpose', 'businessTask', 'reviewDescription',
  'monthlyRevenue', 'existingDebt', 'otherMonthlyObligations',
])

function visible(element: Element) {
  return (
    !element.closest(`[hidden], [aria-hidden="true"], ${assistantSelector}, ${privateSelector}`) &&
    element.getClientRects().length > 0 &&
    getComputedStyle(element).visibility !== 'hidden'
  )
}

function clean(value: string, limit: number) {
  return value
    .replace(/[\w.+-]+@[\w.-]+\.[a-z]{2,}/giu, '[email скрыт]')
    .replace(/\+?\d[\d\s()-]{8,}\d/gu, (match) => match.replace(/\D/gu, '').length >= 10 ? '[реквизиты скрыты]' : match)
    .replace(/[\t ]+/gu, ' ')
    .replace(/\n\s*\n/gu, '\n')
    .trim()
    .slice(0, limit)
}

function referencedText(element: Element, attribute: string) {
  return (element.getAttribute(attribute) ?? '')
    .split(/\s+/u)
    .map((id) => document.getElementById(id)?.textContent ?? '')
    .join(' ')
}

function screenText(root: Element) {
  const walker = document.createTreeWalker(
    root,
    NodeFilter.SHOW_ELEMENT | NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT,
    {
      acceptNode(node) {
        if (node instanceof Element) {
          if (
            node.matches(`script, style, noscript, svg, input, textarea, select, ${assistantSelector}, ${privateSelector}`) ||
            !visible(node)
          ) return NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT
          return NodeFilter.FILTER_SKIP
        }
        return node.textContent?.trim() ? NodeFilter.FILTER_ACCEPT : NodeFilter.FILTER_SKIP
      },
    },
  )
  const parts: string[] = []
  let length = 0
  while (walker.nextNode() && length < 16_000) {
    const text = walker.currentNode.textContent?.trim() ?? ''
    parts.push(text)
    length += text.length
  }
  return clean(parts.join('\n'), 12_000)
}

/** Capture the active dialog or page, never hidden DOM or arbitrary input values. */
export function readAssistantScreen(focusedFieldId: string | null = null) {
  const dialogs = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[role="dialog"], [role="alertdialog"]'))
    .filter(visible)
    .sort((a, b) => (Number.parseInt(getComputedStyle(a).zIndex) || 0) - (Number.parseInt(getComputedStyle(b).zIndex) || 0))
  const dialog = dialogs.at(-1) ?? null
  const root = dialog ?? document.querySelector('#main-content, main') ?? document.body
  const metadata = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-assistant-title]')
  const heading = root.querySelector('h1, h2')
  const stepper = root.querySelector('ol[aria-label="Заполнение заявки"]')
  const step = stepper?.querySelector('[aria-current="step"]')
  const steps = Array.from(stepper?.children ?? [])
  const stepTitle = step ? `Шаг ${steps.indexOf(step) + 1} из ${steps.length}: ${heading?.textContent ?? ''}` : null
  const floatingHelp = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[role="tooltip"], [role="listbox"], [role="menu"]'))
    .filter((element) => visible(element) && !root.contains(element))
    .map(screenText)
  const fields = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement | HTMLSelectElement>(fieldSelector))
    .filter((element) => visible(element) && element.id)
    .slice(0, 40)
    .map((element) => ({
      id: element.id.slice(0, 160),
      label: clean(
        Array.from(element.labels ?? []).map((label) => label.textContent ?? '').join(' ') ||
        referencedText(element, 'aria-labelledby') || element.getAttribute('aria-label') ||
        element.getAttribute('placeholder') || element.name || 'Поле формы',
        200,
      ),
      hint: clean(`${referencedText(element, 'aria-describedby')} ${referencedText(element, 'aria-errormessage')}`, 600),
      value: contextFields.has(element.name) ? clean(element.value ?? '', 300) : null,
      filled: element instanceof HTMLInputElement && ['checkbox', 'radio'].includes(element.type)
        ? element.checked : Boolean(element.value),
      required: element.required || element.getAttribute('aria-required') === 'true',
      invalid: element.getAttribute('aria-invalid') === 'true',
      readOnly: 'readOnly' in element ? element.readOnly : element.getAttribute('aria-readonly') === 'true',
    }))
  const context: AssistantScreen = {
    path: window.location.pathname.slice(0, 200),
    title: clean(metadata?.dataset.assistantTitle || (dialog && referencedText(dialog, 'aria-labelledby')) || heading?.textContent || document.title, 300),
    step: metadata?.dataset.assistantStep?.slice(0, 160) ?? stepTitle?.slice(0, 160) ?? null,
    product: metadata?.dataset.assistantProduct?.slice(0, 200) ?? null,
    text: [screenText(root), ...floatingHelp].join('\n').trim().slice(0, 12_000),
    focusedFieldId: fields.some((field) => field.id === focusedFieldId) ? focusedFieldId : null,
    fields,
  }
  return { dialog, context }
}

/** Refresh context on screen changes, coalescing DOM events into one frame. */
export function useAssistantScreen() {
  const focusedField = useRef<string | null>(null)
  const [screen, setScreen] = useState(() => readAssistantScreen())

  useEffect(() => {
    let frame = 0
    function update() {
      frame = 0
      const next = readAssistantScreen(focusedField.current)
      setScreen((previous) => previous.dialog === next.dialog &&
        JSON.stringify(previous.context) === JSON.stringify(next.context) ? previous : next)
    }
    function schedule() {
      if (!frame) frame = requestAnimationFrame(update)
    }
    function onFocus(event: FocusEvent) {
      if (event.target instanceof Element && visible(event.target) && event.target.matches(fieldSelector)) {
        focusedField.current = event.target.id || null
        schedule()
      }
    }
    function onInput(event: Event) {
      if (!(event.target instanceof Element) || !event.target.closest(assistantSelector)) schedule()
    }
    const observer = new MutationObserver((records) => {
      if (records.some((record) => {
        const element = record.target instanceof Element ? record.target : record.target.parentElement
        return !element?.closest(assistantSelector)
      })) schedule()
    })
    observer.observe(document.body, {
      subtree: true,
      childList: true,
      characterData: true,
      attributes: true,
      attributeFilter: ['hidden', 'aria-hidden', 'aria-invalid', 'class', 'data-assistant-title', 'data-assistant-step', 'data-assistant-product'],
    })
    document.addEventListener('focusin', onFocus)
    document.addEventListener('input', onInput)
    document.addEventListener('change', onInput)
    schedule()
    return () => {
      observer.disconnect()
      cancelAnimationFrame(frame)
      document.removeEventListener('focusin', onFocus)
      document.removeEventListener('input', onInput)
      document.removeEventListener('change', onInput)
    }
  }, [])

  return {
    ...screen,
    capture: () => readAssistantScreen(focusedField.current).context,
  }
}
