=== ./src/features/read-notifications/ui/NotificationCenter/index.ts ===
export { NotificationCenter, type NotificationCenterProps } from './NotificationCenter'

=== ./src/features/read-notifications/ui/NotificationCenter/NotificationCenter.tsx ===
import { useRef } from 'react'
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { Badge } from '@alfalab/core-components/badge'
import { Button } from '@alfalab/core-components/button'
import { IconButton } from '@alfalab/core-components/icon-button'
import { Typography } from '@alfalab/core-components/typography'
import { BellMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/BellMIcon'
import { ArrowsCwMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/ArrowsCwMIcon'
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useNotifications } from '@/entities/notification'
import { useDashboard } from '@/entities/dashboard'
import {
  apiEmpty,
  ApiError,
  getSessionGeneration,
  isSessionSwitching,
  syncSessionChanges,
  useApiScope,
} from '@/shared/api'
import { ROUTES } from '@/shared/config'
import { formatDate } from '@/shared/lib'
import { Panel, RequestState } from '@/shared/ui'
import { useNotificationStream } from '../../model/use-notification-stream'
import styles from './NotificationCenter.module.scss'

/** Controlled panel state keeps header overlays mutually exclusive. */
export interface NotificationCenterProps {
  open: boolean
  onOpen: () => void
  onClose: () => void
  className?: string
}

/** List, read-state actions and the single client notification subscription. */
export function NotificationCenter({
  open,
  onOpen,
  onClose,
  className,
}: NotificationCenterProps) {
  const triggerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const scope = useApiScope()
  const cache = useQueryClient()
  const notifications = useNotifications()
  const dashboard = useDashboard()
  const stream = useNotificationStream()
  const count = notifications.data
    ? notifications.data.filter((item) => !item.read).length
    : dashboard.data?.unreadNotifications
  const read = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, generation }: { id: number | null; generation: number }) => {
      syncSessionChanges()
      if (generation !== getSessionGeneration() || isSessionSwitching())
        throw new ApiError(409, 'SESSION_CHANGED', 'Профиль изменился. Повторите действие.')
      return apiEmpty(
        id === null ? '/api/notifications/read-all' : `/api/notifications/${id}/read`,
        { method: id === null ? 'POST' : 'PATCH' },
      )
    },
    onSuccess: async (_, { generation }) => {
      if (generation !== getSessionGeneration()) return
      await cache.cancelQueries({ queryKey: ['client', scope, 'notifications'] })
      if (generation !== getSessionGeneration()) return
      await Promise.all([
        cache.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['client', scope, 'notifications'] }),
        cache.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['client', scope, 'dashboard'] }),
      ])
    },
    retry: false,
  })

  function show() {
    stream.dismiss()
    onOpen()
    void notifications.refetch()
  }

  return (
    <>
      <span className={`${styles.control} ${className ?? ''}`}>
        <Button
          ref={triggerRef}
          type="button"
          view="transparent"
          size={48}
          aria-label={count ? `Уведомления, непрочитанных: ${count}` : 'Уведомления'}
          aria-expanded={open}
          title="Уведомления"
          onClick={show}
        >
          <BellMIcon aria-hidden="true" />
        </Button>
        {!!count && (
          <span className={styles.badge} aria-hidden="true">
            <Badge view="count" content={count} color="accent" />
          </span>
        )}
      </span>

      {stream.latest && !open && (
        <aside className={styles.toast} aria-label="Новое уведомление">
          <p role="status">{stream.latest.text}</p>
          <div className={styles.actions}>
            <Button type="button" view="secondary" size={32} onClick={show}>
              Посмотреть
            </Button>
            <Button type="button" view="transparent" size={32} onClick={stream.dismiss}>
              Скрыть
            </Button>
          </div>
        </aside>
      )}

      <Panel open={open} title="Уведомления" onClose={onClose} returnFocusRef={triggerRef}>
        <div className={styles.content}>
          <Typography.Text tag="p" view="primary-small" color="secondary" role="status">
            {stream.state === 'connected'
              ? 'Новые уведомления поступают в реальном времени.'
              : stream.state === 'connecting'
                ? 'Подключаем уведомления в реальном времени...'
                : 'Восстанавливаем соединение. Список можно обновить вручную.'}
          </Typography.Text>
          <div className={styles.actions}>
            <IconButton
              className={styles.refreshButton}
              type="button"
              view="secondary"
              size={40}
              icon={<ArrowsCwMIcon aria-hidden="true" />}
              aria-label="Обновить уведомления"
              aria-busy={notifications.isFetching}
              title="Обновить уведомления"
              disabled={notifications.isPending || read.isPending}
              onClick={() => {
                if (!notifications.isFetching) void notifications.refetch()
              }}
            />
            <Button
              type="button"
              view="transparent"
              size={40}
              loading={read.isPending && read.variables?.id === null}
              disabled={!count || read.isPending || notifications.isPending}
              onClick={() => read.mutate({ id: null, generation: getSessionGeneration() })}
            >
              Прочитать все
            </Button>
          </div>
          <Typography.Text view="secondary-medium" color="secondary">
            {count == null ? 'Счётчик пока недоступен' : `Непрочитанных: ${count}`}
          </Typography.Text>
          {notifications.isPending && <RequestState />}
          {notifications.error && (
            <RequestState error={notifications.error} retry={() => { void notifications.refetch() }} />
          )}
          {read.error && <RequestState error={read.error} />}
          {notifications.data?.length === 0 && <p>Уведомлений пока нет.</p>}
          {notifications.data && notifications.data.length > 0 && (
            <ul className={styles.list} aria-label="Уведомления компании">
              {notifications.data.map((notification) => (
                <li
                  key={notification.id}
                  className={`${styles.item} ${notification.read ? '' : styles.unread}`}
                >
                  <div className={styles.meta}>
                    <span>{notification.read ? 'Прочитано' : 'Новое'}</span>
                    <time dateTime={notification.createdAt}>
                      {formatDate(notification.createdAt)}
                      {' · '}
                      {new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ru-RU', {
                        hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
                      }).format(new Date(notification.createdAt))}
                    </time>
                  </div>
                  <p>{notification.text}</p>
                  {!notification.read && (
                    <Button
                      type="button"
                      view="text"
                      size={32}
                      disabled={read.isPending}
                      loading={read.isPending && read.variables?.id === notification.id}
                      aria-label={`Отметить уведомление от ${formatDate(notification.createdAt)} прочитанным`}
                      onClick={() => read.mutate({ id: notification.id, generation: getSessionGeneration() })}
                    >
                      Отметить прочитанным
                    </Button>
                  )}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
          <Link to={ROUTES.applications} onClick={onClose} className={styles.link}>
            Перейти к заявкам
          </Link>
        </div>
      </Panel>
    </>
  )
}

=== ./src/features/read-notifications/notification-stream.test.cjs ===
const assert = require('node:assert/strict')
const { readFileSync } = require('node:fs')
const path = require('node:path')
const vm = require('node:vm')
const { test } = require('node:test')
const ts = require('typescript')
const { z } = require('zod')

const root = path.resolve(__dirname, '../..')

function runtime() {
  const slots = []
  let cursor = 0
  let effects = []
  return {
    begin() {
      cursor = 0
    },
    flush() {
      const pending = effects
      effects = []
      pending.forEach((effect) => effect())
    },
    useState(initial) {
      const index = cursor++
      if (!slots[index])
        slots[index] = { value: typeof initial === 'function' ? initial() : initial }
      return [
        slots[index].value,
        (next) => {
          slots[index].value =
            typeof next === 'function' ? next(slots[index].value) : next
        },
      ]
    },
    useEffect(effect, dependencies) {
      const index = cursor++
      const previous = slots[index]
      if (
        previous &&
        dependencies?.every((value, item) =>
          Object.is(value, previous.dependencies[item]),
        )
      )
        return
      const slot = { dependencies }
      slots[index] = slot
      effects.push(() => {
        previous?.cleanup?.()
        slot.cleanup = effect()
      })
    },
  }
}

function loadHook(hooks, dependencies) {
  const file = path.join(
    root,
    'features/read-notifications/model/use-notification-stream.ts',
  )
  const compiled = ts.transpileModule(readFileSync(file, 'utf8'), {
    compilerOptions: {
      module: ts.ModuleKind.CommonJS,
      target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2023,
    },
  }).outputText
  const module = { exports: {} }
  vm.runInNewContext(
    `(function(require, module, exports) { ${compiled}\n})`,
    { JSON, setTimeout, clearTimeout },
    { filename: file },
  )((id) => {
    if (id === 'react') return hooks
    if (Object.hasOwn(dependencies, id)) return dependencies[id]
    throw new Error(`Unexpected dependency: ${id}`)
  }, module, module.exports)
  return module.exports.useNotificationStream
}

test('SSE starts after a session transition and restarts for a new generation', () => {
  const hooks = runtime()
  const state = {
    scope: 7,
    session: { role: 'CLIENT', companyId: 3 },
    transition: { generation: 1, switching: true },
  }
  const streams = []
  const cache = {
    invalidateQueries() {},
    setQueryData() {},
  }
  const useNotificationStream = loadHook(hooks, {
    '@tanstack/react-query': { useQueryClient: () => cache },
    '@/entities/notification': {
      notificationSchema: z.object({
        id: z.number(),
        text: z.string(),
        read: z.boolean(),
        createdAt: z.string(),
      }),
    },
    '@/entities/session': {
      sessionKey: ['session'],
      useSession: () => ({ data: state.session }),
    },
    '@/shared/api': {
      useApiScope: () => state.scope,
      useSessionTransition: () => state.transition,
      apiEventStream: (url, options) => {
        const stream = { url, options, closed: false }
        streams.push(stream)
        return () => {
          stream.closed = true
        }
      },
    },
  })

  function render() {
    hooks.begin()
    useNotificationStream()
    hooks.flush()
  }

  render()
  assert.equal(streams.length, 0)

  state.transition = { generation: 2, switching: false }
  render()
  assert.equal(streams.length, 1)
  assert.equal(streams[0].url, '/api/notifications/stream')
  assert.equal(streams[0].closed, false)

  state.transition = { generation: 3, switching: false }
  render()
  assert.equal(streams[0].closed, true)
  assert.equal(streams.length, 2)
  assert.equal(streams[1].closed, false)
})

=== ./src/features/read-notifications/model/use-notification-stream.ts ===
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import {
  notificationSchema,
  type Notification,
} from '@/entities/notification'
import { sessionKey, useSession } from '@/entities/session'
import {
  apiEventStream,
  useApiScope,
  useSessionTransition,
} from '@/shared/api'

/** The backend only streams CLIENT events and does not replay missed messages. */
export function useNotificationStream() {
  const scope = useApiScope()
  const session = useSession()
  const role = session.data?.role
  const companyId = session.data?.companyId
  const transition = useSessionTransition()
  const cache = useQueryClient()
  const [state, setState] = useState<'connecting' | 'connected' | 'reconnecting'>(
    'connecting',
  )
  const [latest, setLatest] = useState<Notification | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      scope === null ||
      role !== 'CLIENT' ||
      companyId == null ||
      transition.switching
    )
      return
    const notificationKey = ['client', scope, 'notifications']
    const seen = new Set<number>()
    let checkingSession = false

    function refresh() {
      for (const resource of ['notifications', 'dashboard', 'applications', 'documents'])
        void cache.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['client', scope, resource] })
    }

    return apiEventStream('/api/notifications/stream', {
      events: {
        connected: (data) => {
          if (data !== 'ok') {
            setState('reconnecting')
            return
          }
          checkingSession = false
          setState('connected')
          refresh()
        },
        notification: (data) => {
          let payload: unknown
          try {
            payload = JSON.parse(data)
          } catch {
            refresh()
            return
          }
          const result = notificationSchema.safeParse(payload)
          if (!result.success) {
            refresh()
            return
          }
          const notification = result.data
          if (seen.has(notification.id)) return
          seen.add(notification.id)
          if (seen.size > 200) seen.delete(seen.values().next().value!)
          cache.setQueryData<Notification[]>(notificationKey, (current) => {
            if (!current) return current
            const previous = current.find((item) => item.id === notification.id)
            return [
              { ...notification, read: previous?.read || notification.read },
              ...current.filter((item) => item.id !== notification.id),
            ].sort(
              (a, b) =>
                b.createdAt.localeCompare(a.createdAt) || b.id - a.id,
            )
          })
          setLatest(notification)
          refresh()
        },
      },
      onError: () => {
        setState('reconnecting')
        if (!checkingSession) {
          checkingSession = true
          void cache.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: sessionKey })
        }
      },
    })
  }, [
    cache,
    companyId,
    role,
    scope,
    transition.generation,
    transition.switching,
  ])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!latest) return
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => setLatest(null), 10_000)
    return () => clearTimeout(timeout)
  }, [latest])

  return { state, latest, dismiss: () => setLatest(null) }
}

=== ./src/features/demo-session/ui/SessionActions/SessionActions.tsx ===
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { Button } from '@alfalab/core-components/button'
import { LogOutMIcon } from '@alfalab/icons/glyph/dist/LogOutMIcon'
import { changeSession } from '@/entities/session'
import { apiEmpty, useSessionTransition } from '@/shared/api'

/** Selects an icon-only or labeled sign-out control. */
export interface SessionActionsProps {
  compact?: boolean
}
/** Signs out through the shared cookie-session transition. */
export function SessionActions({ compact = false }: SessionActionsProps) {
  const cache = useQueryClient()
  const transition = useSessionTransition()
  const logout = useMutation({
    networkMode: 'always',
    mutationFn: () =>
      changeSession(cache, async () => {
        await apiEmpty('/api/auth/logout', { method: 'POST' })
        return null
      }),
  })
  return (
    <div>
      <Button
        type="button"
        view="transparent"
        size={compact ? 40 : 48}
        aria-label="Выйти из аккаунта"
        title={compact ? 'Выйти из аккаунта' : undefined}
        loading={logout.isPending}
        disabled={transition.switching || logout.isPending}
        onClick={() => logout.mutate()}
        leftAddons={<LogOutMIcon aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" />}
      >
        {!compact && 'Выйти из аккаунта'}
      </Button>
      {logout.error && <p role="alert">{logout.error.message}</p>}
    </div>
  )
}

=== ./src/features/demo-session/ui/DemoLogin/DemoLogin.tsx ===
import { useId, useRef, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import {
  useIsMutating,
  useMutation,
  useQuery,
  useQueryClient,
} from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { Button } from '@alfalab/core-components/button'
import { Input } from '@alfalab/core-components/input'
import { Modal } from '@alfalab/core-components/modal'
import { Typography } from '@alfalab/core-components/typography'
import {
  changeSession,
  demoProfilesSchema,
  sessionSchema,
} from '@/entities/session'
import { ApiError, apiRequest, useSessionTransition } from '@/shared/api'
import { AlfaMark, RequestState } from '@/shared/ui'
import styles from './DemoLogin.module.scss'

interface LoginCredentials {
  login: string
  password: string
  quick?: boolean
}

/** Signs in with credentials or a profile returned by the quick-entry service. */
export function DemoLogin() {
  const cache = useQueryClient()
  const transition = useSessionTransition()
  const mutations = useIsMutating()
  const id = useId()
  const loginRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)
  const passwordRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [missing, setMissing] = useState({ login: false, password: false })
  const [clientPickerOpen, setClientPickerOpen] = useState(false)
  const clientButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const profiles = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['demo-profiles'],
    queryFn: ({ signal }) =>
      apiRequest('/api/demo/profiles', demoProfilesSchema, { signal }),
    enabled: !transition.switching,
  })
  const login = useMutation({
    networkMode: 'always',
    mutationFn: (credentials: LoginCredentials) =>
      changeSession(cache, () =>
        apiRequest('/api/auth/login', sessionSchema, {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify({
            login: credentials.login,
            password: credentials.password,
          }),
        }),
      ),
  })
  const busy = transition.switching || mutations > 0
  const error = login.error ?? transition.error
  const badCredentials =
    error instanceof ApiError && error.code === 'BAD_CREDENTIALS'
  // Стартовые аккаунты могут быть скрыты из списка демо-профилей.
  // Тогда для быстрого входа берём первый доступный ИП/ООО-сценарий.
  const newIp =
    profiles.data?.find((profile) => profile.login === 'client_ip') ??
    profiles.data?.find((profile) => profile.login.startsWith('demo_ip'))
  const newOoo =
    profiles.data?.find((profile) => profile.login === 'client_ooo') ??
    profiles.data?.find((profile) => profile.login.startsWith('demo_ooo'))
  const officer = profiles.data?.find(
    (profile) => profile.role === 'BANK_OFFICER',
  )
  const otherProfiles =
    profiles.data?.filter(
      (profile) =>
        profile.role === 'CLIENT' &&
        profile.login !== newIp?.login &&
        profile.login !== newOoo?.login,
    ) ?? []

  function quickLogin(profile: NonNullable<typeof officer>) {
    if (busy) return
    setClientPickerOpen(false)
    setMissing({ login: false, password: false })
    login.mutate({
      login: profile.login,
      password: profile.password,
      quick: true,
    })
  }

  function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (busy) return
    const required = { login: !username.trim(), password: !password }
    setMissing(required)
    if (required.login || required.password) {
      const firstMissing = required.login ? loginRef : passwordRef
      firstMissing.current?.focus()
      return
    }
    login.mutate({ login: username.trim(), password })
  }
  return (
    <main className={styles.page}>
      <AlfaMark />
      <Typography.TitleResponsive
        tag="h1"
        view="large"
        font="system"
        weight="bold"
      >
        Вход в кредитный кабинет предпринимателя
      </Typography.TitleResponsive>
      <Typography.Text tag="p" color="secondary">
        Войдите в кредитный кабинет, чтобы работать с заявками и данными
        компании.
      </Typography.Text>
      <form
        className={`${styles.card} ${styles.form}`}
        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
        noValidate
        aria-labelledby={`${id}-login-title`}
        aria-busy={busy}
      >
        <Typography.TitleResponsive
          id={`${id}-login-title`}
          tag="h2"
          view="small"
          font="system"
        >
          Вход в аккаунт
        </Typography.TitleResponsive>
        <Input
          ref={loginRef}
          id={`${id}-login`}
          name="login"
          type="text"
          label="Логин"
          labelView="outer"
          autoComplete="username"
          autoCapitalize="none"
          spellCheck={false}
          block
          required
          disabled={busy}
          value={username}
          error={
            missing.login && <span id={`${id}-login-error`}>Введите логин</span>
          }
          aria-invalid={missing.login || badCredentials}
          aria-describedby={
            missing.login
              ? `${id}-login-error`
              : error
                ? `${id}-auth-error`
                : undefined
          }
          onChange={(_, { value }) => {
            setUsername(value)
            setMissing((previous) => ({ ...previous, login: false }))
          }}
        />
        <Input
          ref={passwordRef}
          id={`${id}-password`}
          name="password"
          type="password"
          label="Пароль"
          labelView="outer"
          autoComplete="current-password"
          block
          required
          disabled={busy}
          value={password}
          error={
            missing.password && (
              <span id={`${id}-password-error`}>Введите пароль</span>
            )
          }
          aria-invalid={missing.password || badCredentials}
          aria-describedby={
            missing.password
              ? `${id}-password-error`
              : error
                ? `${id}-auth-error`
                : undefined
          }
          onChange={(_, { value }) => {
            setPassword(value)
            setMissing((previous) => ({ ...previous, password: false }))
          }}
        />
        {error && (
          <p id={`${id}-auth-error`} role="alert">
            {badCredentials ? 'Неверный логин или пароль' : error.message}
          </p>
        )}
        <Button
          type="submit"
          view="primary"
          block
          disabled={busy}
          loading={login.isPending && !login.variables?.quick}
        >
          Войти
        </Button>
      </form>
      <section
        className={styles.quickEntry}
        aria-labelledby={`${id}-profiles-title`}
      >
        <Typography.TitleResponsive
          id={`${id}-profiles-title`}
          tag="h2"
          view="small"
          font="system"
        >
          Быстрый вход
        </Typography.TitleResponsive>
        {profiles.isPending ? (
          <RequestState />
        ) : profiles.error ? (
          <RequestState
            error={profiles.error}
            retry={() => {
              void profiles.refetch()
            }}
          />
        ) : profiles.data.length === 0 ? (
          <Typography.Text tag="p" color="secondary">
            Нет доступных профилей
          </Typography.Text>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className={styles.cards}>
              <section className={styles.card}>
                <Typography.TitleResponsive tag="h3" view="small" font="system">
                  Новая бизнес-задача
                </Typography.TitleResponsive>
                <p>
                  Начните с нуля: выберите ИП или ООО, опишите, на что нужны
                  деньги, и оформите первую заявку.
                </p>
                <Button
                  ref={clientButtonRef}
                  view="primary"
                  block
                  disabled={busy || (!newIp && !newOoo)}
                  onClick={() => setClientPickerOpen(true)}
                >
                  Зайти под пользователем
                </Button>
              </section>
              <section className={styles.card}>
                <Typography.TitleResponsive tag="h3" view="small" font="system">
                  Работа с заявками
                </Typography.TitleResponsive>
                <p>
                  Рассмотрите заявки клиентов, изучите оценку риска и примите
                  решение о финансировании.
                </p>
                <Button
                  view="secondary"
                  block
                  disabled={busy || !officer}
                  onClick={() => {
                    if (officer) quickLogin(officer)
                  }}
                >
                  Зайти под сотрудником банка
                </Button>
              </section>
            </div>
            <Typography.TitleResponsive tag="h3" view="xsmall" font="system">
              Другие сценарии
            </Typography.TitleResponsive>
            <div className={styles.secondaryCards}>
              {otherProfiles.map((profile) => (
                <section
                  className={`${styles.card} ${styles.secondaryCard}`}
                  key={profile.login}
                >
                  <Typography.TitleResponsive
                    tag="h3"
                    view="xsmall"
                    font="system"
                  >
                    {profile.fullName}
                  </Typography.TitleResponsive>
                  <p>
                    {profile.login === 'ivanov'
                      ? 'Первый вход: выбор формы бизнеса и новая заявка.'
                      : profile.login === 'romashka'
                        ? 'Заявка на рассмотрении: статус и следующий шаг.'
                        : profile.login === 'petrova'
                          ? 'Результат рассмотрения: условия и подписание.'
                          : profile.login === 'vektor'
                            ? 'Действующий кредит: остаток долга и ближайший платеж.'
                            : (profile.description ??
                              'Кабинет компании и ее заявки.')}
                  </p>
                  <Button
                    type="button"
                    view="secondary"
                    block
                    aria-label={`Выбрать профиль: ${profile.fullName}`}
                    disabled={busy}
                    loading={
                      login.isPending &&
                      login.variables?.quick === true &&
                      login.variables?.login === profile.login
                    }
                    onClick={() => quickLogin(profile)}
                  >
                    Выбрать профиль
                  </Button>
                </section>
              ))}
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </section>
      <Modal
        open={clientPickerOpen}
        size={600}
        fixedPosition
        scrollLock
        wrapperProps={{ 'aria-labelledby': `${id}-client-picker` }}
        onClose={() => setClientPickerOpen(false)}
        onUnmount={() =>
          requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            if (
              clientButtonRef.current?.isConnected &&
              !document.querySelector('[role="dialog"]')
            )
              clientButtonRef.current.focus()
          })
        }
      >
        <Modal.Header
          hasCloser
          title={<span id={`${id}-client-picker`}>Выберите форму бизнеса</span>}
          closerProps={{ 'aria-label': 'Закрыть выбор формы бизнеса' }}
        />
        <Modal.Content>
          <div className={styles.picker}>
            <p>
              Выберите аккаунт для первой заявки. Данные бизнеса уже заполнены.
            </p>
            <Button
              view="primary"
              block
              disabled={busy || !newIp}
              onClick={() => {
                if (newIp) quickLogin(newIp)
              }}
            >
              Зайти под ИП
            </Button>
            <Button
              view="secondary"
              block
              disabled={busy || !newOoo}
              onClick={() => {
                if (newOoo) quickLogin(newOoo)
              }}
            >
              Зайти под ООО
            </Button>
            {(!newIp || !newOoo) && (
              <p role="status">
                Не все стартовые профили доступны. Обновите список после запуска
                сервера.
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </Modal.Content>
      </Modal>
    </main>
  )
}

=== ./src/features/demo-session/ui/LegalForm/LegalForm.tsx ===
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { Button } from '@alfalab/core-components/button'
import { useSession, sessionKey, sessionSchema } from '@/entities/session'
import { apiEmpty, apiRequest } from '@/shared/api'
import { Panel } from '@/shared/ui'
import { SessionActions } from '../SessionActions/SessionActions'
import styles from './LegalForm.module.scss'

export function LegalForm() {
  const session = useSession()
  const cache = useQueryClient()
  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (legalForm: 'IP' | 'OOO') => {
      await apiEmpty('/api/company/legal-form', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({ legalForm }),
      })
      return apiRequest('/api/auth/me', sessionSchema)
    },
    onSuccess: async (me) => {
      cache.setQueryData(sessionKey, me)
      await cache.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['client', me.userId] })
    },
  })
  return (
    <Panel
      open={session.data?.role === 'CLIENT' && session.data.legalForm === null}
      title="Уточните форму бизнеса"
      dismissible={false}
      onClose={() => {}}
    >
      <div className={styles.body}>
        <p>
          Название и регистрационные данные уже сохранены. Выберите правовую
          форму, чтобы подать заявку.
        </p>
        <Button
          view="primary"
          block
          disabled={mutation.isPending}
          loading={mutation.isPending && mutation.variables === 'IP'}
          onClick={() => mutation.mutate('IP')}
        >
          Индивидуальный предприниматель (ИП)
        </Button>
        <Button
          view="secondary"
          block
          disabled={mutation.isPending}
          loading={mutation.isPending && mutation.variables === 'OOO'}
          onClick={() => mutation.mutate('OOO')}
        >
          Общество с ограниченной ответственностью (ООО)
        </Button>
        {mutation.error && <p role="alert">{mutation.error.message}</p>}
        <SessionActions />
      </div>
    </Panel>
  )
}

    <script>
      if (!window.crypto.randomUUID) {
        window.crypto.randomUUID = function () {
          return ([1e7] + -1e3 + -4e3 + -8e3 + -1e11).replace(/[018]/g, function (c) {
            return (c ^ (crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(1))[0] & (15 >> (c / 4)))).toString(16);
          });
        };
      }
    </script>
    <script type="module" src="/src/main.tsx"></script>
  </body>
</html>

import { fileURLToPath, URL } from 'node:url'
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
import { defineConfig } from 'vite'

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react()],
  server: {
    host: '127.0.0.1',
    port: 5173,
    strictPort: true,
    proxy: {
      '/api': {
        target: 'http://localhost:8080',
        changeOrigin: true,
        configure: (proxy) => {
          // Browser requests are same-origin; the upstream hop is not a CORS request.
          proxy.on('proxyReq', (request) => request.removeHeader('origin'))
        },
      },
    },
  },
  resolve: {
    alias: {
      '@': fileURLToPath(new URL('./src', import.meta.url)),
    },
  },
})

=== ./README.md ===
# React + TypeScript + Vite

This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some ESLint rules.

Currently, two official plugins are available:

- [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Oxc](https://oxc.rs)
- [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/)

## React Compiler

The React Compiler is not enabled on this template because of its impact on dev & build performances. To add it, see [this documentation](https://react.dev/learn/react-compiler/installation).

## Expanding the ESLint configuration

If you are developing a production application, we recommend updating the configuration to enable type-aware lint rules:

```js
export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
    extends: [
      // Other configs...

      // Remove tseslint.configs.recommended and replace with this
      tseslint.configs.recommendedTypeChecked,
      // Alternatively, use this for stricter rules
      tseslint.configs.strictTypeChecked,
      // Optionally, add this for stylistic rules
      tseslint.configs.stylisticTypeChecked,

      // Other configs...
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: {
        project: ['./tsconfig.node.json', './tsconfig.app.json'],
        tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname,
      },
      // other options...
    },
  },
])

```

You can also install [eslint-plugin-react-x](https://npmx.dev/package/eslint-plugin-react-x) and [eslint-plugin-react-dom](https://npmx.dev/package/eslint-plugin-react-dom) for React-specific lint rules:

```js
// eslint.config.js
import reactX from 'eslint-plugin-react-x'
import reactDom from 'eslint-plugin-react-dom'

export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
    extends: [
      // Other configs...
      // Enable lint rules for React
      reactX.configs['recommended-typescript'],
      // Enable lint rules for React DOM
      reactDom.configs.recommended,
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: {
        project: ['./tsconfig.node.json', './tsconfig.app.json'],
        tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname,
      },
      // other options...
    },
  },
])

```

=== ./Dockerfile ===
# Этап сборки
FROM node:20-alpine AS build
WORKDIR /app
COPY package.json yarn.lock ./
RUN yarn install --frozen-lockfile
COPY . .
RUN yarn build

# Этап раздачи статики (Nginx)
FROM nginx:stable-alpine
COPY --from=build /app/dist /usr/share/nginx/html
COPY nginx.conf /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf
EXPOSE 80
CMD ["nginx", "-g", "daemon off;"]
